# Skyfall Implementation Plan

> **For agentic workers:** REQUIRED SUB-SKILL: Use superpowers:subagent-driven-development (recommended) or superpowers:executing-plans to implement this plan task-by-task. Steps use checkbox (`- [ ]`) syntax for tracking.

**Goal:** Add Skyfall to QuickshotRing: the over-the-top admin skill given by console command. A full draw held at the sky opens a sigil where the aim meets the sky. On release, giant arrows rain into danger domes in waves, and the World-Nail finale follows.

**Architecture:**
- Every rule that can be pure lives in one new Unity-free file, `SkyfallRules.cs`, compiled into the tests: trigger, aim, pattern, grants, signal, claim codes and budgets.
- The game-facing parts are thin and walled off in their own `Skyfall*.cs` files.
- Normal shot handling is touched at a few marked points, each a no-op unless the arrow is a Skyfall release:
  - `Wire.cs` (the claim field);
  - `Rules.cs` (`ServerCheck` clears it);
  - `Claims.cs` (request, pose record, server decision, relay);
  - `Draws.cs` (`Release` decides Skyfall first);
  - `Volley.cs` (`Fire` branches);
  - guards in `HolyArrow.cs` and `Quickdraw4.cs`.
- The server deals all damage from a due-time queue. Clients draw everything from the shot, the relayed claim and [shared] settings.

**Tech Stack:** C# (net472, LangVersion latest, Nullable on), MelonLoader 0.7.3 with HarmonyX, Unity 2020.3 (Mono), and the game's own assemblies (Root.Township, Alta.*). The tests are a console exe that links the Unity-free files (`dotnet run --project QuickshotRing.Tests -c Release`).

**Spec:** `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-11-19-skyfall-admin-skill-design.md`, approved by the owner on 2026-11-19, including the one invisible-message exception.

**Research (read the file for your task before you start):** `docs/superpowers/specs/skyfall-research/`.
- `plan-map/` holds exact signatures and file:line for the code each task touches: `tests-and-rules.md`, `claim-and-seam.md`, `server-systems.md`, `client-looks.md`, `tools-and-docs.md`.
- The five fact checks hold the reasons.
- The look rules are in `QuickshotRing/Art/admin_skill/game-native-kit.md` and its "Standard swaps" S1-S18 (`idea-check.md`).
- The target look is in `QuickshotRing/Art/drafts/admin_skill/`.

**Not a git repo.** "Commit" in this plan means the task's snapshot, taken by the controller with `.superpowers/sdd/2026-11-19-skyfall/snap.sh N`. It is the BASE for the next task's review diff; `pkg.sh` makes review packages. snap.sh covers `QuickshotRing/`, `QuickshotRing.Tests/` and `CLAUDE.md` only, so a task that changes `tools/` lists its files in its report.

## Global Constraints

- **Scope.** Skyfall is given and taken ONLY by console command. It never changes the skill tree, the build block, any orb, or any shot by a player who does not hold it. It never changes a holder's shot that is not a Skyfall cast: below 25 degrees, released before the arm time, or not at full draw. (owner)
- **The no-new-traffic rule has exactly one exception** (owner-approved). On give, take, festival on/off and rejoin, the server sends the holder's game the game's own player text message carrying only `QSR_SKYFALL_V1|...`. The client swallows it, so nothing ever shows on screen. Nothing is ever sent per shot, and nothing goes to other players.
- **Players.** In normal mode, a player inside a dome when its arrow lands is killed instantly by the game's normal death (the Downed state): "Instantly kill them" (owner). This includes the World-Nail's dome. The caster is never hit, whatever the server's PvP setting. Players take no damage and no shove anywhere else. Festival mode never kills; a caught player gets "OUT!" on their own screen only.
- **Creatures** take heavy damage and are knocked down by cripple in normal mode. In festival mode they are knocked over with cripple only, no damage. No explosion force is applied to anything.
- **The server decides all damage.** The headless server never plays a sound or a pooled effect; gate every one with `!ApplicationManager.IsHeadless`.
- **Every machine draws the same storm** from the arrow id, the relayed sigil centre (claim marks) and the [shared] `[Skyfall]` settings. The pattern uses its own named hash (`SkyfallRules.Unit`), never `LookSeed`, which is "looks only".
- **Default numbers** (all `[Skyfall]` settings; pattern and timing ones are `[shared]`):
  - MinStrength 0.9; MinElevationDegrees 25; ArmSeconds 1.0;
  - SigilHeight 30 m; MinDistance 15 m; MaxDistance 60 m; SigilRadius 17.5 m; RainRadius 10 m;
  - BuildSeconds 1.75; Waves 3; ArrowsPerWave 8; WaveGapSeconds 1.0;
  - DomeRadius 2.5 m; DomeWarningSeconds 1.5; MinImpactSpacing 1.5 m; CasterClearance 3 m; ArrowLength 5 m; FallSeconds 0.6;
  - ImpactDamage 80; ImpactCripple 80; BlastRadius 4 m;
  - NailLength 46 m; NailGapSeconds 1.0; NailSlideSeconds 2.5; NailDomeRadius 10 m; NailBlastRadius 12 m; NailDamage 400; NailStandSeconds 20;
  - MaxLiveExplosions 12;
  - SkyDimMetres 150; SkySun 0.5; SkyFade 0.6 (never above 0.95); SkyFadeSeconds 2;
  - FestivalCareRadius 4 m;
  - SpectatorTest false (not shared).
- **Look rules** (game-native kit):
  - Colours: skill gold #ffce00 / #ffd872 / #fff285; ruins cyan #3adeff / #66f6ff; fire orange only for danger and explosions. Never purple, magenta or violet.
  - Rings are the game's `SpriteSheet_Combat` cells on quads, never thin perfect circles. No hexagrams.
  - The game's real arrow meshes.
  - Bursts of a few dozen chunky sprites.
  - The sky is only dimmed, untinted.
  - Vinque font for any text.
  - At most 2-4 `Fx.Flash` lights for the whole of Skyfall.
  - No camera shake. No more than 3 bright flashes a second.
- **VR.** Never move a player's camera. Big sky art goes on layer 1 'TransparentFX', so it shows beyond 100 m.
- **Project rules (CLAUDE.md):**
  - every new patch, tick and look gets a `Timing.Section` and `using var _t = Timing.Begin(T);` as its first statement;
  - every patch body is wrapped in try/catch;
  - new value holders go into `SelfCheck.Run`, and none reads another value holder;
  - log lines start `[Archery] Skyfall:`; per-shot detail only under `if (Logs.Debug)`;
  - no scene-wide search per shot;
  - server ticks go in `Main.OnUpdate` BEFORE the Quickdraw-ring return at Main.cs:205; client looks go in `OnLateUpdate`;
  - state is kept per arrow id or per bow string.
- **The build gate** (tools/stage-build.sh): the tests end "ALL PASS (N)", and `dotnet build QuickshotRing -c Release --no-incremental` gives 0 warnings and 0 errors. Unity-free code is also compiled into the mod, so a nullable or CS0649 warning in it fails the gate.
- **Install.** Nothing is installed until the owner has switched the game back to their QuickshotRing pack (owner: "Restore my pack first"). Task 1's preflight must refuse first.
- **Model roles.** Opus builds. Fable reviews; until 2026-11-20 1pm PT, reviews run on Opus at max effort (owner).

## Review Focus

1. **A holder's shot right at a boundary, seen by two machines.** The server sees the pull 60-380 ms late, and elevation comes from the wire pose. Expected: never half a cast. Either every machine shows the storm, or every machine shows a normal shot; a refusal is a fizzle that every other machine sees. The caster's own game, which no message reaches after its release, still draws its storm unless the refusal was a take that reached it within 1 s (Task 11's TakenWindowSeconds). Pinned in Task 8 by the decision-table rows at claimed 999 / 1000 / 8190 ms, with the server's clock ahead and behind.
2. **A friend whose [shared] Skyfall numbers differ from the server's.** Expected: only that friend's looks differ. Damage and kills follow the server's domes, and the holder's game logs the mismatch once, using the check byte in the message. Pinned in Task 6 (the looks-only test) and Task 7 (a check-byte mismatch is detected).
3. **A storm over a drop, or a spectator without the ground loaded.** Expected: the server never deals or kills where it cannot find ground. Clients skip ground looks (domes, scorches) but still draw the sky. Pinned in Task 9 (the ground-search fallback) and Task 11 (no dome without ground).
4. **Several storms at once** (`skyfall give all`, then everyone casts). Expected: the explosion budget and the server queue cap hold, the World-Nail is never the one dropped, and no frame stalls. Pinned in Task 3 (the budget across 6 storms) and Task 9 (the Nail is queued first).
5. **A settings reload or a damaged grant file.** Expected: a running storm never changes, because its plan is fixed at its start; bad grant lines are skipped; a scene change or disconnect restores the sky. Pinned in Task 4 (Parse with junk lines), Task 8 (SkyStorm keeps its Impacts) and Task 3 ("skyfall sky: no storm, no dim").

## File Structure

New files (QuickshotRing/, top level, block-scoped `namespace QuickshotRing { ... }`, `internal` types):

| File | Responsibility |
|---|---|
| `SkyfallRules.cs` | **Unity-free, tested** (the fourteenth file the tests compile). `SkyfallValues`, `SkyCode`, `ShotPose`, `SkyfallRules` (Unit hash, trigger, aim, centre quantising), `SkyImpact`, `SkyStorm`, `SkyfallPattern`, `SkyfallGrants`, `SkyfallSignal`, `SkyfallNames`, `SkyfallCaught`, `ExplosionBudget`, `SkyDial`. |
| `Skyfall.cs` | The game-facing hub: the `[Skyfall]` settings and the cached `Values`; the server's grants (file load/save) and festival flag; the client latch; per-arrow pose records and storms; the held real arrow (`Skyfall.Held`: hidden at release, destroyed by the server 1 s later); the seam entry points every other file calls. |
| `SkyfallCommands.cs` | The `skyfall` console module (status, give, give all, take, festival), registered in `OnInitializeMelon`. |
| `SkyfallLink.cs` | The one message. Server: a send queue, flushed from `Main.OnUpdate`. Client: a `PlayerTextMessage.Serialize` postfix (latch) and a `PlayerMessageDisplay.Display` prefix (swallow). |
| `SkyfallServer.cs` | The server's storm: the impact queue, creature blasts, player kills, festival care. |
| `PlayerCare.cs` | Heal and Feed, moved out of `TestRangeHelpers.cs` so the range and Skyfall share them. |
| `SkyfallSigil.cs` | Client: builds rings, rune bands and glyph quads from the game's `SpriteSheet_Combat`, ruins glyphs and Lightbeam glyph. Pooled. |
| `SkyfallHold.cs` | Client, caster only: the hold looks (rings climbing the arrow, faint sigil, ground glyph, hum, arm thump). |
| `SkyfallLook.cs` | Client: a storm's timeline (sigil unfold, domes, rain arrows, explosions with budget and spacing, scorches, festival effects, "OUT!"). |
| `SkyfallNail.cs` | Client: the World-Nail (real Arrow LOD0 copies scaled along their own axes, seven bands, slide, impact, stand, crumble) and the sigil's shatter. |
| `SkyfallSky.cs` | Client: the cave dial (`DayTimeManager` LightIntensityModifier and skyColorFade), driven by `SkyDial`, restored on every exit. |

New tests (QuickshotRing.Tests/, picked up by the SDK glob, registered in Program.cs): `SkyfallRulesTests.cs`, `SkyfallPatternTests.cs`, `SkyfallTablesTests.cs`, `SkyfallWireTests.cs`.

Changed files:
- `Wire.cs`, `Rules.cs`, `Claims.cs`, `Draws.cs`, `Volley.cs`, `HolyArrow.cs`, `Quickdraw4.cs`, `Main.cs`, `SelfCheck.cs`, `TestRangeHelpers.cs`;
- `QuickshotRing.Tests/QuickshotRing.Tests.csproj` (one Compile line) and `Program.cs` (four Run lines);
- `tools/install-when-closed.sh`;
- `CLAUDE.md`.

## Shared contract (every task uses exactly these names)

Unity-free, in `SkyfallRules.cs`:

```csharp
internal sealed class SkyfallValues {               // every [Skyfall] number, defaults = Global Constraints
    public float MinStrength = 0.9f, MinElevationDegrees = 25f, ArmSeconds = 1.0f;
    public float SigilHeight = 30f, MinDistance = 15f, MaxDistance = 60f, SigilRadius = 17.5f, RainRadius = 10f;
    public float BuildSeconds = 1.75f; public int Waves = 3, ArrowsPerWave = 8; public float WaveGapSeconds = 1.0f;
    public float DomeRadius = 2.5f, DomeWarningSeconds = 1.5f, MinImpactSpacing = 1.5f, CasterClearance = 3f, ArrowLength = 5f, FallSeconds = 0.6f;
    public float ImpactDamage = 80f, ImpactCripple = 80f, BlastRadius = 4f;
    public float NailLength = 46f, NailGapSeconds = 1.0f, NailSlideSeconds = 2.5f, NailDomeRadius = 10f, NailBlastRadius = 12f, NailDamage = 400f, NailStandSeconds = 20f;
    public int MaxLiveExplosions = 12;
    public float SkyDimMetres = 150f, SkySun = 0.5f, SkyFade = 0.6f, SkyFadeSeconds = 2f, FestivalCareRadius = 4f;
    public static SkyfallValues Default => new SkyfallValues();
    public SkyfallValues Clamped();                  // a copy held to safe ranges (counts >= 1, SkyFade <= 0.95, MinDistance <= MaxDistance, ...)
}
internal enum SkyCode { None = 0, Skyfall = 1, Festival = 2, Fizzled = 3 }   // the claim's window bits when unsteered; a shooter only ever requests Skyfall
internal struct ShotPose { public HVec Pos; public HVec Dir; public float Strength; }   // the release as it went on the wire
internal static class SkyfallRules {
    public static float Unit(uint seed, int index, int salt);                 // [0,1), Skyfall's own hash (counts: decides damage spots)
    public static double ElevationDegrees(HVec dir);
    public static bool Aimed(ShotPose pose, SkyfallValues v);                 // strength >= MinStrength && elevation >= MinElevationDegrees
    public static bool Armed(ShotPose pose, double heldSeconds, SkyfallValues v);
    public static bool ServerAccepts(bool granted, bool casterBusy, ShotPose pose, int? claimedMs, int? effectiveMs, SkyfallValues v);
    public static HVec SigilCentre(HVec release, HVec dir, SkyfallValues v); // y = release.Y + SigilHeight; horizontal distance clamped to [MinDistance, MaxDistance]
    public const int MarkBits = 17; public const double MarkRange = 64.0;  // centre offset per axis in metres, 1/1024 m steps
    public static uint QuantizeOffset(double metres); public static double OffsetOf(uint mark);
}
internal struct SkyImpact { public int Index; public int Wave; public double X, Z; public float DomeAt, LandAt; public bool Nail; }   // times in seconds after the release
internal sealed class SkyStorm {
    public uint ArrowId; public SkyCode Code; public HVec Release; public double CX, CY, CZ; public float StartTime;
    public SkyImpact[] Impacts = new SkyImpact[0]; public int CasterAccount;
    public bool Festival => Code == SkyCode.Festival;
}
internal static class SkyfallPattern {
    public static SkyImpact[] Plan(uint arrowId, double cx, double cz, double casterX, double casterZ, SkyfallValues v);   // rain then the Nail last; deterministic
    public static float EndsAt(SkyfallValues v);     // seconds after release until the Nail has stood and crumbled
}
internal sealed class SkyfallGrants {                // account -> name, the server file's content
    public bool Give(int account, string name); public bool Take(int account); public int? FindByName(string name);
    public IReadOnlyList<KeyValuePair<int, string>> All { get; }
    public string Serialize(); public static SkyfallGrants Parse(string text);   // tolerant: bad lines skipped
}
internal static class SkyfallSignal {
    public const string Tag = "QSR_SKYFALL_V1";
    public static string Format(bool holds, bool festival, byte check);
    public static bool TryParse(string? text, out bool holds, out bool festival, out byte check);
}
internal static class SkyfallNames { public static string? Match(IEnumerable<string> online, string query, out string refusal); }   // one case-insensitive exact match, else null + refusal text
internal static class SkyfallCaught { public static bool Inside(HVec head, double cx, double groundY, double cz, double radius); }
internal sealed class ExplosionBudget { public ExplosionBudget(int cap); public bool TryTake(float now, float lifetime); public int Live(float now); }
internal static class SkyDial { public static float Amount(bool active, bool inCave, float sinceStart, float untilEnd, float fadeSeconds); }   // 0 = no dim .. 1 = full dim
```

Changes to `Wire.cs` (Task 5):
- `ShotClaim` gains `public SkyCode Sky;` and `public List<uint> Marks = new List<uint>();`.
- Both are carried by `Copy` and printed by `ToString` only when non-default.
- `Sky` rides the 2 window bits ONLY when the claim is unsteered. `Marks` (0-3 x 17 bits) ride the mark slot.
- `ClaimRules.ServerCheck` clears both.

Game-facing hub, `Skyfall.cs` (Task 6 creates it; later tasks add the members marked with their task):

```csharp
internal static class Skyfall {
    internal static SkyfallValues Values { get; }                    // T6: cached per settings load, the Seek.Values pattern
    internal static bool SpectatorTest { get; }                      // T6: [Skyfall] SpectatorTest
    internal static bool LocalHolds, LocalFestival;                  // T7: the client latch, set by SkyfallLink
    internal static byte LocalCheck;                                 // T7: the server's shared-settings short check, from the message
    internal static bool Festival { get; set; }                      // T7: server, memory only, starts off
    internal static SkyfallGrants Grants { get; }                    // T7: server, loaded from UserData/QuickshotRing/skyfall-grants.txt
    internal static bool Holds(int account);                         // T7: server
    internal static void RecordPose(uint arrowId, ShotPose pose);    // T8: every machine, from Claims.Bits
    internal static bool TryPose(uint arrowId, out ShotPose pose);   // T8
    internal static bool IsCast(uint arrowId);                       // T8: this machine treats the arrow as a Skyfall release (cast or fizzle)
    internal static bool CasterBusy(int account, float now);         // T8: one storm per caster
    internal static void Begin(SkyStorm storm, bool caster);         // T8: registers the storm; server -> SkyfallServer.Start, client -> SkyfallLook.Start
}
```
- **Server side:** `SkyfallServer.Start(SkyStorm s)`, `SkyfallServer.Tick()`, `SkyfallServer.Reset()`.
- **Client side:**

  - `SkyfallSky.Frame()` and `SkyfallSky.Restore()`;

  - `SkyfallSigil.Build(...)`: Task 10 defines its exact signature in its Produces block.
- **`PlayerCare.Heal(Player p)` and `PlayerCare.Feed(Player p)`:** Task 9 creates these, returning bool (true if changed).

## Controller's rulings (made while assembling the drafts, 2026-11-19)

Each ruling gives what was decided, why, and what it costs if wrong.

1. **`SkyfallRules.ServerAccepts` accepts when both `claimedMs` and `effectiveMs` are at least ArmSeconds.** It does not require `effectiveMs == claimedMs`.
   - Why: the strict rule fizzles every hold over 8.19 s (the claim saturates) and every cast whose client clock runs behind. An untrusted time is still never used, because `effectiveMs` is what TrustMs picks.
   - Cost if wrong: a client that lies about its time can cast up to TrustMs (1 s) early.
2. **The held real arrow is destroyed by Task 8** (`Skyfall.Held`, 1 s after the release, server), because a fizzle has no server storm. Task 9 must not queue arrow destroys.
   - Cost if wrong: none, as long as there is one owner.
3. **The arm rule is the draw clock.** At least ArmSeconds since the 68% crossing; the aim and strength are judged on the wire pose at release. The design's "hold it at the sky for 1 s" becomes "drawn for 1 s, released at the sky".
   - Cost if wrong: a holder who draws level for 1 s and flicks up at release casts. That is acceptable for an admin toy.
4. **The sky dims until the Nail lands plus 1 s,** then eases back over SkyFadeSeconds. It does not stay dim through the Nail's 20 s stand. Why: the game-feel check caps dimming at 10-15 s. Task 14's play-test text follows this.
   - Cost if wrong: the owner may want it darker for longer; that is one setting.
5. **Each wave gets ceil(MaxLiveExplosions / Waves) full explosions** (4 at defaults). The other impacts show sparks, so wave 3 is never left without a blast.
6. **`Skyfall.Tick()` is added to Main.OnUpdate by Task 8 only.** Task 6 adds none. Tasks 7 and 9 add their own `SkyfallLink.Tick()` / `SkyfallServer.Tick()` lines, all before the Quickdraw-ring return at Main.cs:205.
7. **`Skyfall.Begin` is created by Task 8** with two marked lines. Task 9 fills the `SkyfallServer.Start` line (Skyfall and Festival only); Task 11 fills the `SkyfallLook.Start` line (fizzles included).
8. **Constants rather than settings:** the edge share of 0.5, the 1 s arrow destroy, and the 80 m ground search (YAGNI). Each can become a setting later.
9. **Outside snap.sh, so every report lists these files:**
   - `tools/install-when-closed.sh` and `tools/test-installer.sh` (Task 1);
   - `QuickshotRing/Resources/Skyfall_*.png` and `QuickshotRing/Art/admin_skill/make_skyfall_glyphs.py` (Task 10).
   `QuickshotRing/QuiverGather.cs` also changes (Task 8), on top of the skeleton's list.
10. **Proportion:** this plan is long, about 4,350 lines after review; most of the code in it is tests, whose assertions the format wants written out. Function bodies the tests determine were left to the implementer.
11. **An armed Holy Arrow casts at any pull.** `HolyArrow.ReleaseStrength` sends strength 1.0 while Holy Arrow is armed, so `MinStrength` is always met on the wire, and every machine agrees on it.
   - Why: the game itself treats an armed beam as a full draw, and the wire pose is the one thing every machine judges alike.
   - Cost if wrong: a Holy Arrow owner who holds Skyfall, with Holy Arrow armed, casts with any pull past the 68% crossing (not a full draw) at 25 degrees or more. The fix then is a shooter-only check in `Skyfall.Finalise` that the draw clock's last raw pull (`DrawRecord.LastPull`) is at least `MinStrength`; the server needs no change.
12. **The ghost streak is the plain Arrow (Task 11's `PlainArrow`); the shot arrow's meshes are not copied.** The design's "held after its meshes are copied for the ghost" becomes "held at release".
   - Why: Tasks 11 and 12 build every arrow look from `SkyfallLook.PlainArrow()`, so a per-cast copy on the riskiest path would never be read.
   - Cost if wrong: a crafted arrow's 0.35 s ghost looks like the plain Arrow.
13. **One busy rule: Task 8's `SkyfallSeam.BusyUntil`** (the caster's game busy `BusySlackSeconds` 0.5 s past `EndsAt`, the server 0.5 s short of it), worked out once per storm in `Begin`. Task 3 has no `Busy`.
   - Why: two tested rules for one decision; the 1 s total margin covers the 60-380 ms network trip either way.
   - Cost if wrong: none, as long as there is one rule.

---


### Task 1: Installer preflight: refuse before copying anything when a target folder is missing

Why: today `tools/install-when-closed.sh` never checks that the three install folders exist (its only target check is the md5 compare at :46). With the client on the other pack (`<client>\Modpacks` missing on 2026-11-19), an install backs up, copies into the RevampedSkills pack's `Mods`, FAILs on `Modpacks/My mods` and exits 3: a half install whose newest backup has no client DLL, so a later `--rollback` fails too. Also, any unknown first argument does a REAL install today (only `--rollback` is recognised, :34), so a typo of the new flag must be refused.

**Files:**
- Modify: `tools/install-when-closed.sh`: header comment :4-7, insert after :18, delete :24, insert after :44.
- Create: `tools/test-installer.sh` (the installer's test, kept in `tools/` beside it).
- Test: `bash tools/test-installer.sh`.
- `tools/` is not covered by `snap.sh`: the report lists both files.

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: nothing.
- Produces (Task 13 documents them, Task 14's gate uses `--preflight`):
  - `bash tools/install-when-closed.sh --preflight`: checks only, before the one-installer lock, writes nothing (no log, no status, no backup, no `waiting.pid`). Prints exactly `PREFLIGHT OK: all three install folders exist` and exits 0, or `PREFLIGHT REFUSED: missing <folder>` (the first missing of `<client>/Mods`, `<client>/Modpacks/My mods`, `<server>/Mods`, in that order) and exits 4.
  - Install and `--rollback`: when a folder is missing, the log line `INSTALL REFUSED: missing <folder>` (through `say`, so time-stamped and also on stdout), the status file rewritten to two lines (the existing `installed=` line kept, else `installed=unknown`; then `refused=INSTALL REFUSED: missing <folder> at <yyyy-mm-dd HH:MM>`; the `waiting=` line dropped, because no installer is waiting any more), exit 4. Checked once before the lock and the wait, and once more after the wait, both before any backup (:49-55) or copy (:58-62).
  - Any other first argument: prints exactly `unknown option '<arg>': use no option, --rollback or --preflight`, exit 6, nothing else done.
  - `bash tools/test-installer.sh` ends `INSTALLER TEST ALL PASS (25)`, or `INSTALLER TEST ALL PASS (22)` after the line `SKIP installer install: the game or the server is running, so the full scratch install was not tried`.

Safety rule for this task: never run `tools/install-when-closed.sh` without `--preflight`, except through `tools/test-installer.sh`, which points all four `QR_*` overrides at its own `mktemp -d` folder.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the test.** Create `tools/test-installer.sh` with exactly this content (LF line endings). It was dry-run on 2026-11-19 against a scratch copy of the patched installer (25 of 25) and of today's installer (22 of 25 fail).

```bash
#!/usr/bin/env bash
# tools/test-installer.sh : the test of tools/install-when-closed.sh. It runs the installer ONLY on scratch folders made
# here (the QR_CLIENT / QR_SERVER / QR_STATE / QR_BACKUPS overrides) and never touches the game, the server or tools/.install.
# Checks: a missing pack or server Mods folder is refused at once with exit 4, before any backup or copy, in install,
# --rollback and --preflight; --preflight passes when all three folders exist and writes nothing; an unknown option is refused.
# When neither the game nor the server is running it also does one full scratch install, as before the preflight.
set -u
SCRIPT="${QR_SCRIPT:-/c/Users/dmand/OneDrive/Desktop/attmod/tools/install-when-closed.sh}"
R=$(mktemp -d) && [ -n "$R" ] && [ -d "$R" ] || { echo "no scratch folder"; exit 1; }
export QR_CLIENT="$R/game (client" QR_SERVER="$R/game(server" QR_STATE="$R/state" QR_BACKUPS="$R/bk"
CL="$QR_CLIENT"; SV="$QR_SERVER"; ST="$SV/QuickshotRing-install-status.txt"; LOG="$R/state/install.log"
PACK="$CL/Modpacks/My mods"
pass=0; fail=0
check() { local name="$1"; shift; if "$@"; then echo "PASS $name"; pass=$((pass+1)); else echo "FAIL $name"; fail=$((fail+1)); fi; }
run() { local t0=$SECONDS; out=$(timeout "$TO" bash "$SCRIPT" "$@" 2>&1); rc=$?; secs=$((SECONDS-t0)); }
untouched() { [ "$(cat "$CL/Mods/QuickshotRing.dll")" = old ] && [ "$(cat "$SV/Mods/QuickshotRing.dll")" = old ] \
  && [ "$(ls -1d "$R/bk"/auto-* | wc -l)" = 1 ] && [ ! -e "$R/state/waiting.pid" ]; }
last_is() { [[ "$(printf '%s\n' "$out" | tail -1)" == *" $1" ]]; }
refusals() { grep -cF "INSTALL REFUSED: missing $1" "$LOG" 2>/dev/null; }

# the scene: client Mods and server Mods hold "old", the launcher pack folder is MISSING, one old backup, one staged build
mkdir -p "$CL/Mods" "$SV/Mods" "$R/state/staged" "$R/bk/auto-2026-01-01-000000-v0.0.1/client"
echo old > "$CL/Mods/QuickshotRing.dll"; echo old > "$SV/Mods/QuickshotRing.dll"
echo backup > "$R/bk/auto-2026-01-01-000000-v0.0.1/client/QuickshotRing.dll"
printf 'installed=0.0.1 at 2026-01-01 00:00\nwaiting=9.9.9\n' > "$ST"
echo new > "$R/state/staged/QuickshotRing.dll"
printf 'version=9.9.9\nmd5=%s\nfriends=no\nchanged=scratch\ntest=scratch\n' "$(md5sum "$R/state/staged/QuickshotRing.dll" | cut -c1-32)" > "$R/state/staged/manifest.txt"
TO=20

run --preflight
check "installer preflight: a missing pack folder gives exit 4" [ "$rc" = 4 ]
check "installer preflight: it prints only the missing pack folder" [ "$out" = "PREFLIGHT REFUSED: missing $PACK" ]
check "installer preflight: it writes no log, status, backup or copy" eval '[ ! -e "$LOG" ] && untouched && [ "$(cat "$ST")" = "$(printf "installed=0.0.1 at 2026-01-01 00:00\nwaiting=9.9.9")" ]'

run
check "installer install: a missing pack folder gives exit 4" [ "$rc" = 4 ]
check "installer install: it refuses before the wait (under 5 s)" [ "$secs" -lt 5 ]
check "installer install: its last line is the refusal" last_is "INSTALL REFUSED: missing $PACK"
check "installer install: nothing was backed up or copied" untouched
check "installer install: the log holds the refusal once" [ "$(refusals "$PACK")" = 1 ]
check "installer install: the status keeps the installed line" [ "$(sed -n 1p "$ST")" = "installed=0.0.1 at 2026-01-01 00:00" ]
check "installer install: the status names the refusal, no waiting line" eval '[ "$(wc -l < "$ST")" = 2 ] && [[ "$(sed -n 2p "$ST")" == "refused=INSTALL REFUSED: missing $PACK at "* ]]'

run --rollback
check "installer rollback: a missing pack folder gives exit 4" [ "$rc" = 4 ]
check "installer rollback: it refuses before the wait (under 5 s)" [ "$secs" -lt 5 ]
check "installer rollback: nothing was backed up or copied" untouched
check "installer rollback: the log holds the second refusal" [ "$(refusals "$PACK")" = 2 ]

mkdir -p "$PACK"; echo old > "$PACK/QuickshotRing.dll"; mv "$SV/Mods" "$SV/Mods.away"
run --preflight
check "installer server: a missing server Mods folder gives exit 4" [ "$rc" = 4 ]
check "installer server: it prints only the missing server folder" [ "$out" = "PREFLIGHT REFUSED: missing $SV/Mods" ]
mv "$SV/Mods.away" "$SV/Mods"

run --preflight
check "installer preflight: all three folders present give exit 0" [ "$rc" = 0 ]
check "installer preflight: it prints the ok line only" [ "$out" = "PREFLIGHT OK: all three install folders exist" ]
check "installer preflight: the ok check writes nothing" eval 'untouched && [ "$(refusals "$PACK")" = 2 ] && [ "$(cat "$PACK/QuickshotRing.dll")" = old ]'

run --prefight
check "installer option: an unknown option gives exit 6" [ "$rc" = 6 ]
check "installer option: it names the option and the three valid ones" [ "$out" = "unknown option '--prefight': use no option, --rollback or --preflight" ]
check "installer option: nothing was backed up or copied" untouched

if tasklist 2>/dev/null | grep -i -q "township"; then
  echo "SKIP installer install: the game or the server is running, so the full scratch install was not tried"
else
  TO=90; run
  check "installer install: with all three folders it installs as before" eval '[ "$rc" = 0 ] && last_is "test: scratch"'
  check "installer install: all three targets hold the staged file" eval '[ "$(cat "$CL/Mods/QuickshotRing.dll")" = new ] && [ "$(cat "$PACK/QuickshotRing.dll")" = new ] && [ "$(cat "$SV/Mods/QuickshotRing.dll")" = new ]'
  check "installer install: the status reads installed=9.9.9" [ "$(sed -n 's/^installed=\([^ ]*\).*/\1/p' "$ST")" = 9.9.9 ]
fi

rm -rf "$R"
if [ $fail = 0 ]; then echo "INSTALLER TEST ALL PASS ($pass)"; else echo "INSTALLER TEST FAILED $fail of $((pass+fail))"; exit 1; fi
```

- [ ] **Step 4: The check, the option parse and the refusal before the lock.** Insert after :18 (`md5() { md5sum "$1" 2>/dev/null | cut -c1-32; }`), keeping a blank line before the existing `# one waiting installer at a time` comment, exactly:

```bash

# every target's folder must exist: a missing one (the launcher on another pack has no "Modpacks/My mods") would give a half
# install, so this refuses before anything is backed up or copied, at start and again after the wait
first_missing() { for t in "${TARGETS[@]}"; do [ -d "$(dirname "$t")" ] || { dirname "$t"; return 0; }; done; return 1; }
refuse_missing() {
  say "INSTALL REFUSED: missing $1"
  if [ -d "$S" ]; then
    INST=$(grep -m1 '^installed=' "$ST" 2>/dev/null || echo "installed=unknown")
    { echo "$INST"; echo "refused=INSTALL REFUSED: missing $1 at $(date '+%Y-%m-%d %H:%M')"; } > "$ST"
  fi
  exit 4
}
MODE="${1:-install}"
case "$MODE" in
  install|--rollback) ;;
  --preflight) if d=$(first_missing); then echo "PREFLIGHT REFUSED: missing $d"; exit 4; fi; echo "PREFLIGHT OK: all three install folders exist"; exit 0;;
  *) echo "unknown option '$MODE': use no option, --rollback or --preflight"; exit 6;;
esac
if d=$(first_missing); then refuse_missing "$d"; fi
```

Then delete the old :24 `MODE="${1:-install}"` (now parsed above). Why this shape: the `installed=` line must survive, because :50 (`WAS=`) and `tools/stage-build.sh:37` read it; `--preflight` and an unknown option return before the lock at :20-22, so a waiting installer never makes them exit early and they never leave a `waiting.pid`; the status file is written only when the server folder exists (no error line when it is the server folder that is missing).

It must stay before `B="$BK/auto-..."; mkdir -p ...`: a refusal after that mkdir would leave an empty newest `auto-*` folder, which breaks `--rollback` (:35-36).

### Task 2: SkyfallRules A: values, codes, pose, hash, trigger, aim, centre quantising

**Files:**
- Create: `QuickshotRing/SkyfallRules.cs` (new, Unity-free; the fourteenth file the tests compile)
- Create: `QuickshotRing.Tests/SkyfallRulesTests.cs` (picked up by the SDK glob; no csproj entry)
- Modify: `QuickshotRing.Tests/QuickshotRing.Tests.csproj`: one line after line 19 (`<Compile Include="..\QuickshotRing\RangeBuild.cs" />`)
- Modify: `QuickshotRing.Tests/Program.cs`: one line after line 92 (`        RangeBuildTests.Run();`)
- Test: `dotnet run --project QuickshotRing.Tests -c Release`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes:
  - `HVec` (Homing.cs:700-712): `X, Y, Z`, `Finite`, `operator *(HVec, double)`. It has no unary minus, no `double * HVec`, no division.
  - Tests only:
    - `ShotClaimWire.TimeMax` (8190) and `ShotClaimWire.EntityBits` (17) (Wire.cs:41-42);
    - `ClaimTrust.EffectiveMs(int? serverMs, int? claimedMs, int trustMs)` and `ClaimTrust.DefaultTrustMs` (Rules.cs:567-576);
    - `LookSeed.Unit` (Spectral.cs:120-129).
- Produces (the contract, plus the additions marked NEW):
  - `internal sealed class SkyfallValues`:
    - every field exactly as in the Shared contract, and `Default`;
    - `SkyfallValues Clamped()`;
    - NEW consts `MaxWaves = 10`, `MaxArrowsPerWave = 32`, `MaxExplosionsCap = 64` (int); `MaxSkyFade = 0.95f`, `MaxArmSeconds = 8f`, `MaxReachMetres = 60f` (float).
  - `internal enum SkyCode { None = 0, Skyfall = 1, Festival = 2, Fizzled = 3 }`
  - `internal struct ShotPose { public HVec Pos; public HVec Dir; public float Strength; }`
  - `internal static class SkyfallRules`:
    - `Unit(uint seed, int index, int salt)`;
    - `ElevationDegrees(HVec dir)`;
    - `Aimed(ShotPose, SkyfallValues)`;
    - `Armed(ShotPose, double heldSeconds, SkyfallValues)`;
    - `ServerAccepts(bool granted, bool casterBusy, ShotPose pose, int? claimedMs, int? effectiveMs, SkyfallValues v)`;
    - `SigilCentre(HVec release, HVec dir, SkyfallValues v)`;
    - `MarkBits = 17`, `MarkRange = 64.0`, `QuantizeOffset(double)`, `OffsetOf(uint)`;
    - NEW: `MarkSteps = 1024` (int), `MarkMax = 131071u` (uint), `AngleSlackDegrees = 1e-6` (double), `StrengthSlack = 1e-4f`, `HeldSlackSeconds = 1e-4` (double);
    - NEW: `public static SkyCode Relay(SkyCode requested, bool accepted, bool festival)`. The server's relayed code from the shooter's request; Task 8 calls it after `ServerAccepts`.

**What this task decides.** Everything below is a pure function. Tasks 5-12 call these functions and never re-derive the rules.

**ServerAccepts differs from the strict "claim trusted" rule.** The strict rule (`effectiveMs == claimedMs`) fizzles a real cast in two cases:
- **A hold over 8.19 s.** The claim saturates at `ShotClaimWire.TimeMax`, and `ClaimTrust.EffectiveMs` then always returns the server's own time (Rules.cs:572). So every long hold fizzles.
- **The client behind the server.** `EffectiveMs` again returns the server's own time, which is longer than the claim.

This task's rule instead accepts when BOTH:
- the claimed time is at least `ArmSeconds`, AND
- the time the server decides with (`effectiveMs`, which is exactly what the existing `[Claim] TrustMs` rule picks) is at least `ArmSeconds`.

The server therefore never acts on an untrusted client time: a claim far ahead is still refused unless the server's own clock also saw `ArmSeconds`. Checks "skyfall accept: ..." pin every row.

```csharp
// no System.Linq. The pattern decides damage spots, so it uses Skyfall's own hash (SkyfallRules.Unit), never LookSeed (looks only).
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Write the failing tests.** Create `QuickshotRing.Tests/SkyfallRulesTests.cs`:

```csharp
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Reflection;
using QuickshotRing;
using static Program;

// Skyfall (2026-11-19, the owner's admin skill, given only by console command): the trigger, the aim, the sigil's point and its
// marks on the wire. The pattern is in SkyfallPatternTests, the tables in SkyfallTablesTests.
internal static class SkyfallRulesTests {
    static readonly SkyfallValues V = SkyfallValues.Default;

    internal static void Run() {
        Values();
        Codes();
        UnitHash();
        Aim();
        Accepts();
        Centre();
        Marks();
    }

    static HVec Dir(double elevationDegrees, double yawDegrees = 0) {
        double e = elevationDegrees * Math.PI / 180.0, y = yawDegrees * Math.PI / 180.0;
        return new HVec(Math.Cos(e) * Math.Sin(y), Math.Sin(e), Math.Cos(e) * Math.Cos(y));
    }

    static ShotPose Pose(double elevationDegrees, float strength) =>
        new ShotPose { Pos = new HVec(10, 200, -40), Dir = Dir(elevationDegrees, 30), Strength = strength };

    static bool Same(SkyfallValues a, SkyfallValues b) {
        foreach (FieldInfo f in typeof(SkyfallValues).GetFields(BindingFlags.Public | BindingFlags.Instance))
            if (!Equals(f.GetValue(a), f.GetValue(b))) return false;
        return true;
    }

    static double Ground(HVec from, HVec to) { double x = to.X - from.X, z = to.Z - from.Z; return Math.Sqrt(x * x + z * z); }

    // ---------------------------------------------------------------- the values
    static void Values() {
        Check("skyfall values: the trigger and aim defaults are the design's",
            V.MinStrength == 0.9f && V.MinElevationDegrees == 25f && V.ArmSeconds == 1f && V.SigilHeight == 30f && V.MinDistance == 15f
            && V.MaxDistance == 60f && V.SigilRadius == 17.5f && V.RainRadius == 10f);
        Check("skyfall values: the storm defaults are the design's",
            V.BuildSeconds == 1.75f && V.Waves == 3 && V.ArrowsPerWave == 8 && V.WaveGapSeconds == 1f && V.DomeRadius == 2.5f
            && V.DomeWarningSeconds == 1.5f && V.MinImpactSpacing == 1.5f && V.CasterClearance == 3f && V.ArrowLength == 5f
            && V.FallSeconds == 0.6f && V.ImpactDamage == 80f && V.ImpactCripple == 80f && V.BlastRadius == 4f);
        Check("skyfall values: the Nail and sky defaults are the design's",
            V.NailLength == 46f && V.NailGapSeconds == 1f && V.NailSlideSeconds == 2.5f && V.NailDomeRadius == 10f && V.NailBlastRadius == 12f
            && V.NailDamage == 400f && V.NailStandSeconds == 20f && V.MaxLiveExplosions == 12 && V.SkyDimMetres == 150f && V.SkySun == 0.5f
            && V.SkyFade == 0.6f && V.SkyFadeSeconds == 2f && V.FestivalCareRadius == 4f);
        Check("skyfall values: Clamped keeps every default as it is", Same(V, V.Clamped()));
        Check("skyfall values: Clamped returns a copy", !ReferenceEquals(V, V.Clamped()));
        Check("skyfall values: SkyFade is held to 0.95", new SkyfallValues { SkyFade = 2f }.Clamped().SkyFade == 0.95f);
        var low = new SkyfallValues { Waves = 0, ArrowsPerWave = -5, MaxLiveExplosions = 0 }.Clamped();
        Check("skyfall values: counts are at least 1", low.Waves == 1 && low.ArrowsPerWave == 1 && low.MaxLiveExplosions == 1);
        var high = new SkyfallValues { Waves = 1000, ArrowsPerWave = 1000, MaxLiveExplosions = 1000 }.Clamped();
        Check("skyfall values: counts are held to 10 waves, 32 arrows and 64 explosions", high.Waves == 10 && high.ArrowsPerWave == 32 && high.MaxLiveExplosions == 64);
        var crossed = new SkyfallValues { MinDistance = 50f, MaxDistance = 20f }.Clamped();
        Check("skyfall values: MaxDistance is raised to MinDistance", crossed.MinDistance == 50f && crossed.MaxDistance == 50f);
        var far = new SkyfallValues { MaxDistance = 500f, SigilHeight = 500f }.Clamped();
        Check("skyfall values: the distances and the sigil height fit the 64 m mark range", far.MaxDistance == 60f && far.SigilHeight == 60f);
        Check("skyfall values: BuildSeconds is at least DomeWarningSeconds", new SkyfallValues { BuildSeconds = 0.5f, DomeWarningSeconds = 1.5f }.Clamped().BuildSeconds == 1.5f);
        var nan = new SkyfallValues { RainRadius = float.NaN, ArmSeconds = float.NaN }.Clamped();
        Check("skyfall values: a NaN setting falls back to its default", nan.RainRadius == 10f && nan.ArmSeconds == 1f);
        var arm = new SkyfallValues { ArmSeconds = 30f }.Clamped();
        Check("skyfall values: ArmSeconds stays under the claim's 8.19 s time cap", arm.ArmSeconds == 8f && arm.ArmSeconds * 1000f < ShotClaimWire.TimeMax);
    }

    // ---------------------------------------------------------------- the claim's codes
    static void Codes() {
        Check("skyfall codes: the wire values are pinned",
            (int)SkyCode.None == 0 && (int)SkyCode.Skyfall == 1 && (int)SkyCode.Festival == 2 && (int)SkyCode.Fizzled == 3);
        Check("skyfall codes: a refused request fizzles",
            SkyfallRules.Relay(SkyCode.Skyfall, false, false) == SkyCode.Fizzled && SkyfallRules.Relay(SkyCode.Skyfall, false, true) == SkyCode.Fizzled);
        Check("skyfall codes: an accepted request is Skyfall, or Festival while festival mode is on",
            SkyfallRules.Relay(SkyCode.Skyfall, true, false) == SkyCode.Skyfall && SkyfallRules.Relay(SkyCode.Skyfall, true, true) == SkyCode.Festival);
        Check("skyfall codes: no request is no storm, whatever the server would accept", SkyfallRules.Relay(SkyCode.None, true, true) == SkyCode.None);
        Check("skyfall codes: only Skyfall counts as a request",
            SkyfallRules.Relay(SkyCode.Festival, true, false) == SkyCode.None && SkyfallRules.Relay(SkyCode.Fizzled, true, false) == SkyCode.None);
    }

    // ---------------------------------------------------------------- the hash
    static void UnitHash() {
        bool inRange = true; double sum = 0; int n = 0;
        for (uint s = 0; s < 40; s++)
            for (int i = 0; i < 50; i++) { float u = SkyfallRules.Unit(s * 2654435761u, i, 1); if (!(u >= 0f && u < 1f)) inRange = false; sum += u; n++; }
        Check("skyfall unit: always in [0, 1)", inRange);
        Check("skyfall unit: the same inputs give the same number", SkyfallRules.Unit(123456789u, 7, 3) == SkyfallRules.Unit(123456789u, 7, 3));
        var distinct = new HashSet<float>();
        for (int i = 0; i < 200; i++) distinct.Add(SkyfallRules.Unit(42u, i, 1));
        Check("skyfall unit: 200 indices give at least 190 different numbers", distinct.Count >= 190);
        double mean = sum / n;
        Check("skyfall unit: the mean of 2000 numbers is near 0.5", mean > 0.47 && mean < 0.53);
        int saltDiffers = 0, seedDiffers = 0, sameAsLook = 0;
        for (int i = 0; i < 200; i++) {
            if (SkyfallRules.Unit(99u, i, 1) != SkyfallRules.Unit(99u, i, 2)) saltDiffers++;
            if (SkyfallRules.Unit(99u, i, 1) != SkyfallRules.Unit(100u, i, 1)) seedDiffers++;
            if (SkyfallRules.Unit(99u, i, 1) == LookSeed.Unit(99u, i, 1)) sameAsLook++;
        }
        Check("skyfall unit: the salt and the seed change the number", saltDiffers >= 195 && seedDiffers >= 195);
        Check("skyfall unit: it is its own hash, not LookSeed's", sameAsLook <= 5);
        Check("skyfall unit: the hash is pinned (a change moves every storm and needs a new DLL on every machine)",
            SkyfallRules.Unit(0u, 0, 0) == 6497268f / 16777216f && SkyfallRules.Unit(4242u, 5, 1) == 6128271f / 16777216f);
    }

    // ---------------------------------------------------------------- the trigger
    static void Aim() {
        Check("skyfall aim: elevation is measured from level",
            Math.Abs(SkyfallRules.ElevationDegrees(Dir(40, 75)) - 40) < 1e-9 && Math.Abs(SkyfallRules.ElevationDegrees(new HVec(0, -1, 1)) + 45) < 1e-9);
        Check("skyfall aim: a direction need not be unit length", Math.Abs(SkyfallRules.ElevationDegrees(Dir(40) * 7) - 40) < 1e-9);
        Check("skyfall aim: a zero or broken direction has no elevation",
            double.IsNaN(SkyfallRules.ElevationDegrees(new HVec(0, 0, 0))) && double.IsNaN(SkyfallRules.ElevationDegrees(new HVec(double.NaN, 1, 0))));
        Check("skyfall aim: 24.9 degrees is not aimed", !SkyfallRules.Aimed(Pose(24.9, 1f), V));
        Check("skyfall aim: 25 degrees is aimed", SkyfallRules.Aimed(Pose(25, 1f), V));
        Check("skyfall aim: strength 0.899 is not a full draw", !SkyfallRules.Aimed(Pose(40, 0.899f), V));
        Check("skyfall aim: strength 0.9 is a full draw", SkyfallRules.Aimed(Pose(40, 0.9f), V));
        Check("skyfall aim: straight up is aimed", SkyfallRules.Aimed(Pose(90, 1f), V));
        Check("skyfall aim: a NaN strength is never aimed", !SkyfallRules.Aimed(Pose(40, float.NaN), V));
        Check("skyfall arm: held 0.99 s is not armed", !SkyfallRules.Armed(Pose(40, 1f), 0.99, V));
        Check("skyfall arm: held 1.0 s is armed", SkyfallRules.Armed(Pose(40, 1f), 1.0, V));
        Check("skyfall arm: a long hold below the angle is not armed", !SkyfallRules.Armed(Pose(24.9, 1f), 5.0, V));
        Check("skyfall arm: a NaN hold is not armed", !SkyfallRules.Armed(Pose(40, 1f), double.NaN, V));
    }

    // ---------------------------------------------------------------- the server's decision
    static void Accepts() {
        ShotPose p = Pose(40, 1f);
        int trust = ClaimTrust.DefaultTrustMs;
        Check("skyfall accept: a granted, free, aimed caster with a trusted 1.2 s hold is accepted", SkyfallRules.ServerAccepts(true, false, p, 1200, 1200, V));
        Check("skyfall accept: not granted is refused", !SkyfallRules.ServerAccepts(false, false, p, 1200, 1200, V));
        Check("skyfall accept: a caster whose storm still runs is refused", !SkyfallRules.ServerAccepts(true, true, p, 1200, 1200, V));
        int? ahead = ClaimTrust.EffectiveMs(300, 1500, trust);
        Check("skyfall accept: a claim too far ahead of the server's own 0.3 s is refused", ahead == 300 && !SkyfallRules.ServerAccepts(true, false, p, 1500, ahead, V));
        Check("skyfall accept: a trusted hold of 0.999 s is refused", !SkyfallRules.ServerAccepts(true, false, p, 999, 999, V));
        Check("skyfall accept: a trusted hold of exactly 1.0 s is accepted", SkyfallRules.ServerAccepts(true, false, p, 1000, 1000, V));
        Check("skyfall accept: a release pose under 25 degrees is refused", !SkyfallRules.ServerAccepts(true, false, Pose(24.9, 1f), 1200, 1200, V));
        Check("skyfall accept: a release under full draw is refused", !SkyfallRules.ServerAccepts(true, false, Pose(40, 0.899f), 1200, 1200, V));
        Check("skyfall accept: no claimed time is refused", !SkyfallRules.ServerAccepts(true, false, p, null, 1500, V));
        int? none = ClaimTrust.EffectiveMs(null, 1500, trust);
        Check("skyfall accept: no time to decide with is refused", none == null && !SkyfallRules.ServerAccepts(true, false, p, 1500, none, V));
        int? capped = ClaimTrust.EffectiveMs(7900, ShotClaimWire.TimeMax, trust);
        Check("skyfall accept: a claim at the 8190 ms cap is accepted on the server's own 7.9 s",
            capped == 7900 && SkyfallRules.ServerAccepts(true, false, p, ShotClaimWire.TimeMax, capped, V));
        int? behind = ClaimTrust.EffectiveMs(1250, 1100, trust);
        Check("skyfall accept: a claim behind the server's own time is accepted", behind == 1250 && SkyfallRules.ServerAccepts(true, false, p, 1100, behind, V));
        int? farOk = ClaimTrust.EffectiveMs(1200, 2600, trust), farShort = ClaimTrust.EffectiveMs(900, 2600, trust);
        Check("skyfall accept: an untrusted claim is accepted only on the server's own time",
            farOk == 1200 && SkyfallRules.ServerAccepts(true, false, p, 2600, farOk, V) && farShort == 900 && !SkyfallRules.ServerAccepts(true, false, p, 2600, farShort, V));
        int? shortClaim = ClaimTrust.EffectiveMs(1300, 800, trust);
        Check("skyfall accept: a claim under ArmSeconds is refused even when the server's own time is longer",
            shortClaim == 1300 && !SkyfallRules.ServerAccepts(true, false, p, 800, shortClaim, V));
    }

    // ---------------------------------------------------------------- the sigil's point
    static void Centre() {
        var r = new HVec(100, 214, -300);
        HVec c45 = SkyfallRules.SigilCentre(r, Dir(45, 60), V), c80 = SkyfallRules.SigilCentre(r, Dir(80, 60), V), c26 = SkyfallRules.SigilCentre(r, Dir(26, 60), V);
        Check("skyfall centre: 45 degrees puts it 30 m out", Math.Abs(Ground(r, c45) - 30) < 1e-9);
        Check("skyfall centre: 80 degrees is held to 15 m", Math.Abs(Ground(r, c80) - 15) < 1e-9);
        Check("skyfall centre: 26 degrees is held to 60 m", Math.Abs(Ground(r, c26) - 60) < 1e-9);
        Check("skyfall centre: it is SigilHeight above the release", c45.Y == 244 && c80.Y == 244 && c26.Y == 244);
        double s60 = Math.Sin(60 * Math.PI / 180), k60 = Math.Cos(60 * Math.PI / 180);
        Check("skyfall centre: the aim's direction along the ground is kept",
            Math.Abs((c45.X - r.X) / 30 - s60) < 1e-9 && Math.Abs((c45.Z - r.Z) / 30 - k60) < 1e-9
            && Math.Abs((c80.X - r.X) / 15 - s60) < 1e-9 && Math.Abs((c26.Z - r.Z) / 60 - k60) < 1e-9);
        HVec up = SkyfallRules.SigilCentre(r, new HVec(0, 1, 0), V);
        Check("skyfall centre: straight up goes MinDistance toward +Z", up.X == r.X && Math.Abs(up.Z - r.Z - 15) < 1e-9);
        Check("skyfall centre: a level or downward aim goes MaxDistance out", Math.Abs(Ground(r, SkyfallRules.SigilCentre(r, Dir(-10, 0), V)) - 60) < 1e-9);
    }

    // ---------------------------------------------------------------- the centre's marks on the wire
    static void Marks() {
        Check("skyfall marks: MarkBits matches the claim's 17-bit slots and MarkRange is 64 m",
            SkyfallRules.MarkBits == ShotClaimWire.EntityBits && SkyfallRules.MarkRange == 64.0);
        bool half = true;
        foreach (double x in new[] { -64.0, -63.9, -30.123456, -0.0004, 0.0, 0.0004, 12.3456789, 59.9999, 63.998 })
            if (!(Math.Abs(SkyfallRules.OffsetOf(SkyfallRules.QuantizeOffset(x)) - x) <= 1.0 / 2048 + 1e-12)) half = false;
        Check("skyfall marks: an offset comes back within half a 1/1024 m step", half);
        bool fits = true;
        for (double x = -100; x <= 100; x += 0.37) if (SkyfallRules.QuantizeOffset(x) >= (1u << 17)) fits = false;
        Check("skyfall marks: every mark fits in 17 bits", fits);
        Check("skyfall marks: zero is the middle mark", SkyfallRules.QuantizeOffset(0) == 65536u && SkyfallRules.OffsetOf(65536u) == 0.0);
        Check("skyfall marks: +64 m and beyond hold to the top mark",
            SkyfallRules.QuantizeOffset(100) == 131071u && SkyfallRules.QuantizeOffset(64) == 131071u && SkyfallRules.OffsetOf(131071u) == 64.0 - 1.0 / 1024);
        Check("skyfall marks: -64 m and beyond hold to the bottom mark",
            SkyfallRules.QuantizeOffset(-100) == 0u && SkyfallRules.QuantizeOffset(-64) == 0u && SkyfallRules.OffsetOf(0u) == -64.0);
        Check("skyfall marks: NaN is the middle mark", SkyfallRules.QuantizeOffset(double.NaN) == 65536u);
        Check("skyfall marks: OffsetOf ignores bits above the 17th", SkyfallRules.OffsetOf(65536u | (1u << 20)) == 0.0);
        var rng = new Random(29);
        bool survives = true;
        for (int i = 0; i < 200; i++) {
            var r = new HVec(rng.NextDouble() * 4000 - 2000, rng.NextDouble() * 500, rng.NextDouble() * 4000 - 2000);
            HVec c = SkyfallRules.SigilCentre(r, Dir(25 + rng.NextDouble() * 65, rng.NextDouble() * 360), V);
            foreach (double o in new[] { c.X - r.X, c.Y - r.Y, c.Z - r.Z })
                if (!(Math.Abs(SkyfallRules.OffsetOf(SkyfallRules.QuantizeOffset(o)) - o) <= 1.0 / 2048 + 1e-9)) survives = false;
        }
        Check("skyfall marks: a sigil centre survives the wire within half a millimetre", survives);
    }
}
```

- [ ] **Step 6: SkyfallValues.** In `SkyfallRules.cs`, inside the namespace, add `internal sealed class SkyfallValues`:
  - the fields and `Default` exactly as the Shared contract;
  - the six NEW consts from Produces;
  - `public SkyfallValues Clamped()`:
    - It starts from `(SkyfallValues)MemberwiseClone()`, so a field added later is copied too.
    - It holds each field with a private `static float C(float x, float lo, float hi, float fallback)`: NaN gives that field's `Default` value, ±Infinity gives the bound.
    - Ints use a plain hold. net472 has no `Math.Clamp`, so write the holds by hand as Homing.cs:38 does.
    - The order matters for the four cross-field rules (marked "then").

| Field | Held to | | Field | Held to |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| MinStrength | [0.5, 1] | | ImpactDamage, ImpactCripple, NailDamage | [0, 100000] |
| MinElevationDegrees | [10, 89] | | BlastRadius | [0.5, 20] |
| ArmSeconds | [0.25, `MaxArmSeconds` 8] | | NailLength | [1, 200] |
| SigilHeight | [5, `MaxReachMetres` 60] | | NailGapSeconds | [0, 10] |
| MinDistance, MaxDistance | [0, 60], then MaxDistance raised to MinDistance | | NailSlideSeconds | [0.1, 10] |
| SigilRadius | [1, 100] | | NailDomeRadius | [0.5, 30] |
| RainRadius | [1, 40] | | NailBlastRadius | [0.5, 40] |
| DomeWarningSeconds | [0.5, 10] | | NailStandSeconds | [0, 120] |
| BuildSeconds | [0, 10], then raised to DomeWarningSeconds | | MaxLiveExplosions | [1, `MaxExplosionsCap` 64] |
| Waves | [1, `MaxWaves` 10] | | SkyDimMetres | [0, 1000] |
| ArrowsPerWave | [1, `MaxArrowsPerWave` 32] | | SkySun | [0, 1] |
| WaveGapSeconds | [0.1, 10] | | SkyFade | [0, `MaxSkyFade` 0.95] |
| DomeRadius | [0.5, 10] | | SkyFadeSeconds | [0, 10] |
| MinImpactSpacing | [0, 10] | | FestivalCareRadius | [0, 20] |
| CasterClearance | [0, 20] | | ArrowLength | [0.5, 50] |
| FallSeconds | [0.05, 5], then lowered to BuildSeconds | | | |

Why these ceilings:
- `MaxArmSeconds` 8 stays under the claim's 8190 ms time cap.
- `MaxReachMetres` 60 keeps every sigil-centre offset inside the marks' ±64 m (`MarkRange`).
- BuildSeconds ≥ DomeWarningSeconds makes the first wave's warning possible.
- FallSeconds ≤ BuildSeconds means no arrow starts falling before the release.

- [ ] **Step 8: The hash.** Add `internal static class SkyfallRules` with the consts from Produces and `Unit`. The tests check its range, spread and two pinned values; they do not determine the algorithm. Write exactly this: the murmur3 finaliser over a mixed start, a different mix from LookSeed's at Spectral.cs:124-125:

```csharp
        /// <summary>[0, 1): Skyfall's own repeatable number from a seed (the release's arrow id), an index and a salt. It COUNTS: the
        /// pattern's damage spots come from it, so it must be bit-identical on every machine (integer arithmetic, one exact scale).</summary>
        public static float Unit(uint seed, int index, int salt) {
            unchecked {
                uint h = seed * 0xCC9E2D51u + (uint)index * 0x1B873593u + (uint)salt * 0xE6546B64u + 0x5F1E7A11u;
                h ^= h >> 16; h *= 0x85EBCA6Bu; h ^= h >> 13; h *= 0xC2B2AE35u; h ^= h >> 16;
                return (h >> 8) * (1f / 16777216f);
            }
        }
```

```csharp
        public static bool ServerAccepts(bool granted, bool casterBusy, ShotPose pose, int? claimedMs, int? effectiveMs, SkyfallValues v) {
            if (!granted || casterBusy || !Aimed(pose, v) || !claimedMs.HasValue || !effectiveMs.HasValue) return false;
            double arm = v.ArmSeconds - HeldSlackSeconds;
            return claimedMs.Value / 1000.0 >= arm && effectiveMs.Value / 1000.0 >= arm;   // both clocks; effectiveMs is ClaimTrust's pick
        }

        /// <summary>The server's relayed code: only a shooter's Skyfall request counts; refused = Fizzled.</summary>
        public static SkyCode Relay(SkyCode requested, bool accepted, bool festival) =>
            requested != SkyCode.Skyfall ? SkyCode.None : !accepted ? SkyCode.Fizzled : festival ? SkyCode.Festival : SkyCode.Skyfall;
```

- [ ] **Step 10: The sigil's point and its marks.** Add these to `SkyfallRules`:
  - `SigilCentre`. The degenerate cases are decisions, so write exactly:

```csharp
        /// <summary>Where the aim meets the plane SigilHeight above the release, its distance along the ground held to
        /// [MinDistance, MaxDistance]. Straight up (or a broken direction) goes along +Z; level or downward goes MaxDistance out.</summary>
        public static HVec SigilCentre(HVec release, HVec dir, SkyfallValues v) {
            double h = Math.Sqrt(dir.X * dir.X + dir.Z * dir.Z);
            double gx = 0, gz = 1;
            if (dir.Finite && h >= 1e-9) { gx = dir.X / h; gz = dir.Z / h; }
            double d = dir.Finite && dir.Y > 0 ? v.SigilHeight * h / dir.Y : v.MaxDistance;
            if (d < v.MinDistance) d = v.MinDistance;
            if (d > v.MaxDistance) d = v.MaxDistance;
            return new HVec(release.X + gx * d, release.Y + v.SigilHeight, release.Z + gz * d);
        }
```

  - `public static uint QuantizeOffset(double metres)`:
    - NaN counts as 0 m;
    - `s = Math.Floor((metres + MarkRange) * MarkSteps + 0.5)`;
    - `!(s > 0)` gives 0, `s > MarkMax` gives `MarkMax`, else `(uint)s`.
  - `public static double OffsetOf(uint mark) => (mark & MarkMax) / (double)MarkSteps - MarkRange;`.
  - Zero is mark 65536. The top mark (131071) is +63.9990234375 m: +64 m itself does not fit in 17 bits.

### Task 3: SkyfallRules B: the pattern, storm end, explosion budget, sky dial

**Files:**
- Modify: `QuickshotRing/SkyfallRules.cs`: append after the closing `}` of `internal static class SkyfallRules` (the last type Task 2 wrote), before the namespace's closing `}`.
- Create: `QuickshotRing.Tests/SkyfallPatternTests.cs`
- Modify: `QuickshotRing.Tests/Program.cs`: one line after `        SkyfallRulesTests.Run();` (line 93 after Task 2).
- Test: `dotnet run --project QuickshotRing.Tests -c Release`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes (Task 2):
  - `SkyfallValues` (fields, `Default`, `Clamped()`, `MaxWaves`, `MaxArrowsPerWave`);
  - `SkyfallRules.Unit(uint, int, int)`;
  - `SkyfallRules.SigilCentre(HVec, HVec, SkyfallValues)`, used by the tests only;
  - `SkyCode`;
  - `HVec` (Homing.cs:700).
- Produces (the contract, plus the additions marked NEW):
  - `internal struct SkyImpact { public int Index; public int Wave; public double X, Z; public float DomeAt, LandAt; public bool Nail; }`, times in seconds after the release.
  - `internal sealed class SkyStorm`, exactly as the contract (`ArrowId, Code, Release, CX, CY, CZ, StartTime, Impacts, CasterAccount, Festival`).
  - `internal static class SkyfallPattern`:
    - `public static SkyImpact[] Plan(uint arrowId, double cx, double cz, double casterX, double casterZ, SkyfallValues v)`;
    - `public static float EndsAt(SkyfallValues v)`;
    - NEW `public static float NailLandAt(SkyfallValues v)`: v must already be clamped. Task 12 starts the slide at `NailLandAt(v) - v.NailSlideSeconds`.
    - NEW consts `TriesPerSpot = 64`, `SaltX = 1`, `SaltZ = 2` (int).
    - The one-storm-per-caster rule is NOT here: it is Task 8's `SkyfallSeam.BusyUntil` (ruling 13).
  - `internal sealed class ExplosionBudget { public ExplosionBudget(int cap); public bool TryTake(float now, float lifetime); public int Live(float now); }`
  - `internal static class SkyDial { public static float Amount(bool active, bool inCave, float sinceStart, float untilEnd, float fadeSeconds); }`

**What this task decides** (the tests check the outcomes; the algorithm below is the plan's decision):
- **All of a wave lands at once.** Rain arrow `i` is in wave `w = i / ArrowsPerWave` and lands at `BuildSeconds + w * WaveGapSeconds`. Staggering a wave's eight explosions would make eight bright flashes inside a fraction of a second, breaking the "no more than 3 bright flashes a second" rule. One wave a second is one flash a second.
- **Every rain dome burns in during the build-up,** one after another in arrow order, over `burn = max(0, min(BuildSeconds - DomeWarningSeconds, WaveGapSeconds))` (0.25 s by default):
  - `DomeAt = burn * i / rainCount`.
  - Capping `burn` at `WaveGapSeconds` makes every arrow of a later wave get a longer warning than every arrow of an earlier wave, for any settings `Clamped` allows.
- **The Nail is not a wave:**
  - It lands at `NailLandAt = BuildSeconds + (Waves - 1) * WaveGapSeconds + NailGapSeconds + NailSlideSeconds` (7.25 s by default).
  - Its 10 m dome shows when the last wave lands: `DomeAt = min(lastWaveLandAt, NailLandAt - DomeWarningSeconds)`. That is 3.5 s of warning by default, with the Nail visibly sliding for 2.5 s of it.
  - It is NOT held to "longer than every rain warning". Wave 3's earliest dome shows about 3.58 s ahead. Beating that would put the 10 m dome over the storm while the rain still falls: by 0.08 s at default settings, and by up to 7 s with 10 waves.
- **EndsAt = NailLandAt + NailStandSeconds** (27.25 s by default). `Plan` and `EndsAt` both work on `v.Clamped()`: one small allocation, so call each once per storm, never per frame. This keeps a bad cfg from flooding (at most 10 x 32 + 1 impacts).
- **The pattern depends only on positions relative to the centre.** The offsets come from the arrow id and the [shared] values. The caster enters only as `(casterX - cx, casterZ - cz)`.
- **Seeded rejection sampling with a crowded fallback** (exact code in Step 6):
  - Each rain spot takes up to `TriesPerSpot` 64 candidates from `SkyfallRules.Unit`, uniform in the square around the centre.
  - Hard rules, which no candidate may break: inside the `RainRadius` disc, and at least `DomeRadius + CasterClearance` (5.5 m) from the caster.
  - It takes the first candidate also at least `MinImpactSpacing` from every spot already placed. The Nail's centre is placed first, so no rain lands on the Nail's spot.
  - If no candidate keeps the spacing, it takes the passing candidate with the widest gap. From then on the pattern is "crowded": later spots take their first candidate that passes the hard rules, without the spacing search.
  - If no candidate passes the hard rules at all, the spot goes to the disc's far edge from the caster (+Z when the caster stands on the centre).
  - Measured on a scratch copy over 20,000 random default casts (480,000 spots): never crowded, never on the fallback, at most 22 of the 64 tries.
  - Cost: about 4 µs per default plan. The crowded rule keeps the largest allowed settings (320 spots) at about 0.1 ms; without it they took 10 ms, a frame stall on every machine.
- **Determinism.** Only integer hashing, IEEE `+ - * /` and comparisons in doubles; there is no trig in `Plan`. The test pins two spots of arrow 4242. Any change to the algorithm changes the storm, and friends then need the new DLL.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing tests.** Create `QuickshotRing.Tests/SkyfallPatternTests.cs`:

```csharp
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Linq;
using QuickshotRing;
using static Program;

// Skyfall: the storm's pattern (where and when every giant arrow and the World-Nail land, and when each danger dome shows), the
// storm's end, the client's explosion budget and the sky dial. Every machine plans the same storm
// from the arrow id and the relayed centre, so these spots decide damage: Skyfall's own hash, never LookSeed.
internal static class SkyfallPatternTests {
    static readonly SkyfallValues V = SkyfallValues.Default;

    internal static void Run() {
        Pattern();
        RandomAims();
        Crowded();
        Budget();
        Dial();
    }

    static double Flat(double ax, double az, double bx, double bz) { double x = ax - bx, z = az - bz; return Math.Sqrt(x * x + z * z); }

    static bool Spaced(SkyImpact[] p, double gap) {
        for (int a = 0; a < p.Length; a++)
            for (int b = a + 1; b < p.Length; b++)
                if (Flat(p[a].X, p[a].Z, p[b].X, p[b].Z) < gap - 1e-9) return false;
        return true;
    }

    static bool SameImpacts(SkyImpact[] a, SkyImpact[] b) {
        if (a.Length != b.Length) return false;
        for (int i = 0; i < a.Length; i++)
            if (a[i].Index != b[i].Index || a[i].Wave != b[i].Wave || a[i].X != b[i].X || a[i].Z != b[i].Z
                || a[i].DomeAt != b[i].DomeAt || a[i].LandAt != b[i].LandAt || a[i].Nail != b[i].Nail) return false;
        return true;
    }

    // ---------------------------------------------------------------- one storm: release at the origin, aimed 45 degrees along +Z
    static void Pattern() {
        const uint id = 4242u;
        const double cx = 0, cz = 30;     // SigilCentre(origin, 45 degrees along +Z) is 30 m out
        SkyImpact[] p = SkyfallPattern.Plan(id, cx, cz, 0, 0, V);
        SkyImpact[] rain = p.Where(i => !i.Nail).ToArray();
        SkyImpact nail = p[p.Length - 1];
        Check("skyfall pattern: the same arrow gives the same storm", SameImpacts(p, SkyfallPattern.Plan(id, cx, cz, 0, 0, V)));
        SkyImpact[] other = SkyfallPattern.Plan(id + 1, cx, cz, 0, 0, V);
        int moved = 0;
        for (int i = 0; i < 24; i++) if (other[i].X != p[i].X || other[i].Z != p[i].Z) moved++;
        Check("skyfall pattern: another arrow gives another storm", moved >= 20);
        Check("skyfall pattern: 3 waves of 8 rain arrows and then one Nail",
            p.Length == 25 && rain.Length == 24 && p.Count(i => i.Nail) == 1 && nail.Nail);
        Check("skyfall pattern: each impact's Index is its place in the array", Enumerable.Range(0, p.Length).All(i => p[i].Index == i));
        Check("skyfall pattern: the Nail lands at the centre", nail.X == cx && nail.Z == cz);
        Check("skyfall pattern: the rain's waves are 0, 1 and 2 in order, eight each, and the Nail's is 3",
            Enumerable.Range(0, 24).All(i => p[i].Wave == i / 8) && nail.Wave == 3);
        Check("skyfall pattern: the Nail lands last", rain.All(i => i.LandAt < nail.LandAt));
        Check("skyfall pattern: each wave lands together, WaveGapSeconds after the one before",
            rain.All(i => Near(i.LandAt, V.BuildSeconds + i.Wave * V.WaveGapSeconds)));
        Check("skyfall pattern: the first arrow lands at BuildSeconds", Near(p.Min(i => i.LandAt), 1.75f) && p.All(i => i.LandAt >= V.BuildSeconds));
        Check("skyfall pattern: the Nail lands NailGapSeconds plus NailSlideSeconds after the last wave", Near(nail.LandAt, 3.75f + 1f + 2.5f));
        Check("skyfall pattern: every dome shows at least DomeWarningSeconds before its arrow",
            p.All(i => i.DomeAt <= i.LandAt - V.DomeWarningSeconds + 1e-5f));
        Check("skyfall pattern: no dome shows before the release", p.All(i => i.DomeAt >= 0f));
        bool longer = true;
        foreach (SkyImpact a in rain) foreach (SkyImpact b in rain)
            if (a.LandAt < b.LandAt && b.LandAt - b.DomeAt < a.LandAt - a.DomeAt) longer = false;
        Check("skyfall pattern: a later wave never gets a shorter warning", longer);
        Check("skyfall pattern: the Nail's dome shows when the last wave lands", Near(nail.DomeAt, 3.75f));
        Check("skyfall pattern: every rain spot is within RainRadius of the centre", rain.All(i => Flat(i.X, i.Z, cx, cz) <= V.RainRadius + 1e-9));
        Check("skyfall pattern: every two impacts are at least MinImpactSpacing apart", Spaced(p, V.MinImpactSpacing));
        Check("skyfall pattern: EndsAt is the Nail's landing plus NailStandSeconds",
            Near(SkyfallPattern.EndsAt(V), nail.LandAt + V.NailStandSeconds) && Near(SkyfallPattern.EndsAt(V), 27.25f));
        Check("skyfall pattern: NailLandAt is the Nail's LandAt", SkyfallPattern.NailLandAt(V) == nail.LandAt);
        SkyImpact[] shifted = SkyfallPattern.Plan(id, cx + 500, cz - 250, 500, -250, V);
        bool follows = true;
        for (int i = 0; i < p.Length; i++)
            if (Math.Abs((shifted[i].X - 500) - p[i].X) > 1e-9 || Math.Abs((shifted[i].Z + 250) - p[i].Z) > 1e-9) follows = false;
        Check("skyfall pattern: moving the whole scene moves the pattern with it", follows);
        Check("skyfall pattern: arrow 4242's first and last rain spots are pinned (a change needs a new DLL on every machine)",
            Math.Abs(p[0].X - 5.2505779266357422) < 1e-9 && Math.Abs(p[0].Z - 29.996129274368286) < 1e-9
            && Math.Abs(p[23].X - 8.39145302772522) < 1e-9 && Math.Abs(p[23].Z - 32.264765501022339) < 1e-9);
    }

    // ---------------------------------------------------------------- 500 random casts (fixed seed)
    static void RandomAims() {
        var rng = new Random(19);
        bool clear = true, nailClear = true, inDisc = true, spaced = true;
        for (int s = 0; s < 500; s++) {
            uint id = (uint)rng.Next(int.MinValue, int.MaxValue);
            double e = (25 + rng.NextDouble() * 64.9) * Math.PI / 180, y = rng.NextDouble() * 2 * Math.PI;
            var release = new HVec(rng.NextDouble() * 4000 - 2000, rng.NextDouble() * 500, rng.NextDouble() * 4000 - 2000);
            HVec c = SkyfallRules.SigilCentre(release, new HVec(Math.Cos(e) * Math.Sin(y), Math.Sin(e), Math.Cos(e) * Math.Cos(y)), V);
            SkyImpact[] p = SkyfallPattern.Plan(id, c.X, c.Z, release.X, release.Z, V);
            foreach (SkyImpact i in p) {
                double fromCaster = Flat(i.X, i.Z, release.X, release.Z);
                if (!i.Nail && fromCaster < V.DomeRadius + V.CasterClearance - 1e-9) clear = false;
                if (i.Nail && fromCaster < V.NailDomeRadius + V.CasterClearance - 1e-9) nailClear = false;
                if (!i.Nail && Flat(i.X, i.Z, c.X, c.Z) > V.RainRadius + 1e-9) inDisc = false;
            }
            if (!Spaced(p, V.MinImpactSpacing)) spaced = false;
        }
        Check("skyfall pattern: over 500 random aims no rain dome reaches within CasterClearance of the caster", clear);
        Check("skyfall pattern: over 500 random aims the Nail's dome stays CasterClearance from the caster", nailClear);
        Check("skyfall pattern: over 500 random aims every rain spot is inside the rain disc", inDisc);
        Check("skyfall pattern: over 500 random aims every two impacts keep MinImpactSpacing", spaced);
    }

    // ---------------------------------------------------------------- settings that leave no room
    static void Crowded() {
        SkyImpact[] most = SkyfallPattern.Plan(7u, 0, 30, 0, 0, new SkyfallValues { Waves = 1000, ArrowsPerWave = 1000 });
        Check("skyfall pattern: Plan holds its counts to 10 waves of 32, every spot in the disc and clear of the caster",
            most.Length == 321 && most.Where(i => !i.Nail).All(i => Flat(i.X, i.Z, 0, 30) <= 10 + 1e-9 && Flat(i.X, i.Z, 0, 0) >= 5.5 - 1e-9));
        var tight = new SkyfallValues { RainRadius = 1f, MinImpactSpacing = 10f };
        SkyImpact[] t = SkyfallPattern.Plan(7u, 0, 30, 0, 0, tight);
        Check("skyfall pattern: crowded settings still give every arrow a spot in the disc",
            t.Length == 25 && t.Where(i => !i.Nail).All(i => Flat(i.X, i.Z, 0, 30) <= 1 + 1e-9));
        SkyImpact[] quick = SkyfallPattern.Plan(7u, 0, 30, 0, 0, new SkyfallValues { NailGapSeconds = 0f, NailSlideSeconds = 0.5f });
        SkyImpact qn = quick[quick.Length - 1];
        Check("skyfall pattern: a quick Nail still shows its dome DomeWarningSeconds before it lands", Near(qn.LandAt, 4.25f) && Near(qn.DomeAt, 2.75f));
        var cornered = new SkyfallValues { RainRadius = 2f };
        SkyImpact[] f = SkyfallPattern.Plan(7u, 0, 0, 0, -1, cornered);
        Check("skyfall pattern: with no room outside the caster's clearance every spot goes to the disc's far edge",
            f.Where(i => !i.Nail).All(i => Math.Abs(i.X) < 1e-12 && Math.Abs(i.Z - 2) < 1e-12));
    }

    // ---------------------------------------------------------------- the client's explosion budget
    static void Budget() {
        var b = new ExplosionBudget(12);
        bool all = true;
        for (int i = 0; i < 12; i++) if (!b.TryTake(0f, 3f)) all = false;
        Check("skyfall budget: twelve explosions fit at once", all);
        Check("skyfall budget: the thirteenth is refused", !b.TryTake(0.5f, 3f) && b.Live(0.5f) == 12);
        Check("skyfall budget: nothing frees before its lifetime ends", !b.TryTake(2.99f, 3f) && b.Live(2.99f) == 12);
        Check("skyfall budget: every slot frees at its lifetime's end", b.Live(3f) == 0 && b.TryTake(3f, 3f) && b.Live(3f) == 1);
        var two = new ExplosionBudget(2);
        two.TryTake(0f, 1f); two.TryTake(0.5f, 1f);
        Check("skyfall budget: lifetimes expire one by one", two.Live(0.9f) == 2 && two.Live(1f) == 1 && two.Live(1.5f) == 0);
        Check("skyfall budget: a cap of 0 refuses every explosion", !new ExplosionBudget(0).TryTake(0f, 1f) && new ExplosionBudget(-3).Live(0f) == 0);
        var one = new ExplosionBudget(1);
        Check("skyfall budget: an explosion with no lifetime takes no slot",
            one.TryTake(0f, 0f) && one.Live(0f) == 0 && one.TryTake(0f, float.NaN) && one.Live(0f) == 0 && one.TryTake(0f, 2f) && one.Live(0f) == 1);
        var six = new ExplosionBudget(V.MaxLiveExplosions);
        int started = 0, most = 0;
        for (int w = 0; w < V.Waves; w++) {
            float at = V.BuildSeconds + w * V.WaveGapSeconds;
            for (int k = 0; k < 6 * V.ArrowsPerWave; k++) if (six.TryTake(at, 6f)) started++;
            most = Math.Max(most, six.Live(at));
        }
        Check("skyfall budget: six storms landing together never have more than MaxLiveExplosions explosions alive",
            started == 12 && most == 12 && six.Live(V.BuildSeconds + 6f) == 0);
    }

    // ---------------------------------------------------------------- the sky dial
    static void Dial() {
        Check("skyfall sky: no storm, no dim", SkyDial.Amount(false, false, 5f, 10f, 2f) == 0f);
        Check("skyfall sky: in a cave, no dim", SkyDial.Amount(true, true, 5f, 10f, 2f) == 0f);
        Check("skyfall sky: it starts at 0", SkyDial.Amount(true, false, 0f, 10f, 2f) == 0f);
        Check("skyfall sky: it ramps in over fadeSeconds",
            Near(SkyDial.Amount(true, false, 0.5f, 10f, 2f), 0.15625f) && Near(SkyDial.Amount(true, false, 1f, 10f, 2f), 0.5f) && SkyDial.Amount(true, false, 2f, 10f, 2f) == 1f);
        Check("skyfall sky: it ramps out over the last fadeSeconds",
            Near(SkyDial.Amount(true, false, 20f, 1f, 2f), 0.5f) && Near(SkyDial.Amount(true, false, 20f, 0.5f, 2f), 0.15625f));
        Check("skyfall sky: it is 0 after the end", SkyDial.Amount(true, false, 30f, 0f, 2f) == 0f && SkyDial.Amount(true, false, 30f, -1f, 2f) == 0f);
        bool bounded = true;
        foreach (float fade in new[] { 2f, 0.01f, 0f })
            for (float s = -1f; s < 40f; s += 0.1f) { float a = SkyDial.Amount(true, false, s, 27.25f - s, fade); if (!(a >= 0f && a <= 1f)) bounded = false; }
        Check("skyfall sky: it never goes below 0 or above 1", bounded);
        Check("skyfall sky: a fade of 0 switches at once",
            SkyDial.Amount(true, false, 0f, 10f, 0f) == 1f && SkyDial.Amount(true, false, 0.001f, 10f, 0f) == 1f && SkyDial.Amount(true, false, 0.001f, 0f, 0f) == 0f);
        Check("skyfall sky: broken times give no dim", SkyDial.Amount(true, false, float.NaN, 10f, 2f) == 0f && SkyDial.Amount(true, false, 5f, float.NaN, 2f) == 0f);
    }
}
```
