- [ ] **Step 6: Plan.** Add to `SkyfallPattern` exactly this. The tests check its properties; this text pins the algorithm and the golden values:

```csharp
        /// <summary>The storm's impacts: Waves * ArrowsPerWave rain spots (wave-major, Index = place in the array), then the Nail at
        /// the centre, last. Deterministic from the arrow id, the [shared] values and the caster's offset from the centre.</summary>
        public static SkyImpact[] Plan(uint arrowId, double cx, double cz, double casterX, double casterZ, SkyfallValues v) {
            v = v.Clamped();
            int rain = v.Waves * v.ArrowsPerWave;
            var result = new SkyImpact[rain + 1];
            double r = v.RainRadius, keep = v.DomeRadius + v.CasterClearance, gap = v.MinImpactSpacing;
            double ox = casterX - cx, oz = casterZ - cz;                       // the caster, seen from the centre
            var px = new List<double>(rain + 1) { 0 };                          // placed offsets; the Nail's spot first
            var pz = new List<double>(rain + 1) { 0 };
            float lastRain = v.BuildSeconds + (v.Waves - 1) * v.WaveGapSeconds;
            float burn = Math.Max(0f, Math.Min(v.BuildSeconds - v.DomeWarningSeconds, v.WaveGapSeconds));
            bool crowded = false;
            for (int i = 0; i < rain; i++) {
                bool found = false, any = false; double bx = 0, bz = 0, bestGap2 = -1;
                for (int t = 0; t < TriesPerSpot; t++) {
                    double dx = (2.0 * SkyfallRules.Unit(arrowId, i * TriesPerSpot + t, SaltX) - 1.0) * r;
                    double dz = (2.0 * SkyfallRules.Unit(arrowId, i * TriesPerSpot + t, SaltZ) - 1.0) * r;
                    if (dx * dx + dz * dz > r * r) continue;                    // outside the rain disc
                    double ex = dx - ox, ez = dz - oz;
                    if (ex * ex + ez * ez < keep * keep) continue;              // its dome would reach within CasterClearance of the caster
                    if (crowded) { bx = dx; bz = dz; found = true; break; }     // no room for spacing any more: first legal spot
                    double g2 = double.MaxValue;
                    for (int k = 0; k < px.Count; k++) { double ax = dx - px[k], az = dz - pz[k]; double d2 = ax * ax + az * az; if (d2 < g2) g2 = d2; }
                    if (g2 >= gap * gap) { bx = dx; bz = dz; found = true; break; }
                    if (g2 > bestGap2) { bestGap2 = g2; bx = dx; bz = dz; any = true; }
                }
                if (!found) crowded = true;
                if (!found && !any) {                                           // nothing legal: the disc's far edge from the caster
                    double l = Math.Sqrt(ox * ox + oz * oz);
                    if (l < 1e-9) { bx = 0; bz = r; } else { bx = -ox / l * r; bz = -oz / l * r; }
                }
                px.Add(bx); pz.Add(bz);
                int w = i / v.ArrowsPerWave;
                result[i] = new SkyImpact { Index = i, Wave = w, X = cx + bx, Z = cz + bz,
                                            LandAt = v.BuildSeconds + w * v.WaveGapSeconds, DomeAt = burn * i / rain, Nail = false };
            }
            float nail = NailLandAt(v);
            result[rain] = new SkyImpact { Index = rain, Wave = v.Waves, X = cx, Z = cz, LandAt = nail,
                                           DomeAt = Math.Min(lastRain, nail - v.DomeWarningSeconds), Nail = true };
            return result;
        }
```

- [ ] **Step 7: ExplosionBudget.** Append `internal sealed class ExplosionBudget`:
  - one `readonly float[]` of expiry times, length `cap` (a negative cap gives 0), each starting at `float.NegativeInfinity`. No allocation per call.
  - `TryTake(now, lifetime)`:
    - a lifetime that is not `> 0` (NaN included) counts as 0;
    - it takes the first slot whose expiry is not `> now`, sets it to `now + lifetime` and returns true;
    - with no free slot it returns false;
    - a zero lifetime still needs a free slot, and is free again at once.
  - `Live(now)` counts the expiries `> now`, so a slot expires exactly at `now == start + lifetime`.
  - Task 11 makes one `new ExplosionBudget(Values.MaxLiveExplosions)` per client and passes the effect's lifetime.

- [ ] **Step 8: SkyDial.** Append `internal static class SkyDial` with `Amount`, 0 = no dim, 1 = full dim:
  - 0 when `!active || inCave`;
  - 0 when `sinceStart` is NaN or `< 0`, or `!(untilEnd > 0)`;
  - 1 when `!(fadeSeconds > 0)`;
  - otherwise `a = Math.Min(1f, Math.Min(sinceStart, untilEnd) / fadeSeconds)`, returning the smoothstep `a * a * (3f - 2f * a)`.
  - Task 10 maps the amount onto the game's dial: sun `1 - amount * (1 - SkySun)`, `skyColorFade = amount * SkyFade` (already ≤ 0.95 by `Clamped`). SkyfallSky passes each dim request's `until - now`. Task 11 asks until `StartTime + NailLandAt + 1 + SkyFadeSeconds` (ruling 4); the hold asks until `now + SkyFadeSeconds + 0.3`.

### Task 4: SkyfallRules C: grants table, signal format, name match, caught rule

**Files:**
- Modify: `QuickshotRing/SkyfallRules.cs`: append after the closing `}` of `internal static class SkyDial` (the last type Task 3 wrote), before the namespace's closing `}`.
- Create: `QuickshotRing.Tests/SkyfallTablesTests.cs`
- Modify: `QuickshotRing.Tests/Program.cs`: one line after `        SkyfallPatternTests.Run();` (line 94 after Task 3).
- Test: `dotnet run --project QuickshotRing.Tests -c Release`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `HVec` (Homing.cs:700; `Finite`).
- Produces (the contract, plus the additions marked NEW):
  - `internal sealed class SkyfallGrants`:
    - `public bool Give(int account, string name)`: true when the table changed.
    - `public bool Take(int account)`.
    - `public int? FindByName(string name)`.
    - `public IReadOnlyList<KeyValuePair<int, string>> All { get; }`: sorted by account, a new list each call.
    - `public string Serialize()`.
    - `public static SkyfallGrants Parse(string? text)`. This widens the contract's `string` to `string?` (as `PathStore.Parse`, Path.cs:266), so Task 7 can pass "no file" as null.
    - NEW `public const string Header`.
  - `internal static class SkyfallSignal`:
    - `public const string Tag = "QSR_SKYFALL_V1"`;
    - `public static string Format(bool holds, bool festival, byte check)`;
    - `public static bool TryParse(string? text, out bool holds, out bool festival, out byte check)`;
    - NEW `public static bool IsOurs(string? text)`: true for anything that starts `QSR_SKYFALL_V1|`. `IsOurs` is TryParse's first test. Task 7's Display prefix swallows on its own wider `SkyfallSignal.Hidden` (any `QSR_SKYFALL_` version); its Serialize postfix latches only on `TryParse`.
  - `internal static class SkyfallNames { public static string? Match(IEnumerable<string> online, string query, out string refusal); }`: the refusal is `""` on a match.
  - `internal static class SkyfallCaught { public static bool Inside(HVec head, double cx, double groundY, double cz, double radius); }`
- Who calls them:
  - Task 7: `Grants` holds the grants file `UserData\QuickshotRing\skyfall-grants.txt`. The server sends `Format(holds, Festival, Cfg.SharedShort)` (Cfg.cs:30). The client compares the whole byte with its own `Cfg.SharedShort`; the build block compares only 6 bits (`BuildBits.CheckMask`, Build.cs:99). `skyfall give/take <player>` use `Match`, and `take` falls back to `FindByName` for an offline player.
  - Task 9: the server's kill test, `Inside` per online player's head per landing.
  - Task 11: the client's festival "OUT!" test.

**What this task decides:**
- **The grants file** is plain `account=name` lines, accounts ascending, under one comment line (exact text in Step 4).
  - Hand edits are read only at start-up; the next give or take rewrites the file.
  - Accounts must be positive ints. Names are trimmed, and must be non-empty and on one line.
  - A repeated account keeps its last line.
- **The signal** is `QSR_SKYFALL_V1|<holds 0/1>|<festival 0/1>|<check, 2 lowercase hex>`: 21 ASCII characters, no player name. `PlayerTextMessage` serialises as ASCII (Stream.cs:103).
  - `TryParse` wants exactly three fields after the tag. Hex may be either case.
  - On a refusal the outputs are false, false, 0.
  - Other mods' tags (`CUSTOM_INGOTS_CONFIG_V1|`, `PET_ROSTER|`, `PET_CLONE|`) are refused.
- **Names** match case-insensitively and exactly (never a prefix), after trimming the query. Blank entries in the online list are skipped.
  - Two online names that differ only in case are refused, never guessed between.
  - The online list in a refusal is sorted case-insensitively, ties ordinal; it reads `nobody` when nobody is online.
  - Task 7 may put its own prefix before the refusal text. It must not reword it, since the tests pin it.
- **Caught** is a cylinder:
  - horizontal distance ≤ radius;
  - head height from `groundY - 1` to `groundY + radius + 3`, both ends included.
  - It is false for a non-finite head, ground or centre, or a radius not > 0.
  - This keeps a player on a ledge high above the dome, or in a cave under it, uncaught.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing tests.** Create `QuickshotRing.Tests/SkyfallTablesTests.cs`:

```csharp
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Linq;
using QuickshotRing;
using static Program;

// Skyfall: the server's grant table (account -> name, the file UserData\QuickshotRing\skyfall-grants.txt), the one invisible
// message's text (the owner-approved exception to "no new traffic"), the console's player lookup and who a dome catches.
internal static class SkyfallTablesTests {
    internal static void Run() {
        Grants();
        Signal();
        Names();
        Caught();
    }

    static string Keys(SkyfallGrants g) => string.Join(",", g.All.Select(kv => kv.Key + ":" + kv.Value).ToArray());

    // ---------------------------------------------------------------- the grant table
    static void Grants() {
        Check("skyfall grants: an empty text is no grants", SkyfallGrants.Parse("").All.Count == 0 && SkyfallGrants.Parse(null).All.Count == 0);
        var g = new SkyfallGrants();
        Check("skyfall grants: give, then the table lists it", g.Give(123, "Alice") && Keys(g) == "123:Alice");
        Check("skyfall grants: giving the same name again changes nothing", !g.Give(123, "Alice") && Keys(g) == "123:Alice");
        Check("skyfall grants: give replaces the name", g.Give(123, "Alicia") && Keys(g) == "123:Alicia");
        g.Give(900, "Zed"); g.Give(5, "Bob"); g.Give(77, "Carol");
        Check("skyfall grants: All is sorted by account", Keys(g) == "5:Bob,77:Carol,123:Alicia,900:Zed");
        Check("skyfall grants: take removes one, and taking it again is refused", g.Take(77) && !g.Take(77) && Keys(g) == "5:Bob,123:Alicia,900:Zed");
        Check("skyfall grants: a blank or multi-line name is refused", !g.Give(1, "  ") && !g.Give(2, "a\nb") && !g.Give(3, "a\rb") && g.All.Count == 3);
        Check("skyfall grants: names are trimmed", g.Give(9, "  Dora  ") && g.All.First(kv => kv.Key == 9).Value == "Dora");
        Check("skyfall grants: FindByName is case-insensitive",
            g.FindByName("alicia") == 123 && g.FindByName("ALICIA") == 123 && g.FindByName("  Alicia ") == 123);
        Check("skyfall grants: FindByName of an unknown name is nothing", g.FindByName("Mallory") == null && g.FindByName("") == null);
        g.Give(500, "Twin"); g.Give(501, "twin");
        Check("skyfall grants: two grants with one name are not guessed between", g.FindByName("Twin") == null);
        Check("skyfall grants: the file round-trips", Keys(SkyfallGrants.Parse(g.Serialize())) == Keys(g));
        var pinned = new SkyfallGrants(); pinned.Give(123, "Alice"); pinned.Give(5, "Bob");
        Check("skyfall grants: the file text is pinned",
            pinned.Serialize() == "# Skyfall grants (QuickshotRing): account=name, one per line. Edit only while the server is stopped.\n5=Bob\n123=Alice\n");
        Check("skyfall grants: bad lines are skipped",
            Keys(SkyfallGrants.Parse("# a comment\n\nnot a line\n=NoAccount\nabc=Name\n12=\n 34 = Carol \n-5=Neg\n0=Zero\n99999999999=Big\n56=Dave=Two\n")) == "34:Carol,56:Dave=Two");
        Check("skyfall grants: a repeated account keeps the last line", Keys(SkyfallGrants.Parse("7=Old\n7=New\n")) == "7:New");
        Check("skyfall grants: Windows line ends are read", Keys(SkyfallGrants.Parse("8=Eve\r\n9=Finn\r\n")) == "8:Eve,9:Finn");
        Check("skyfall grants: accounts must be positive", !new SkyfallGrants().Give(0, "Zed") && !new SkyfallGrants().Give(-4, "Zed"));
    }

    // ---------------------------------------------------------------- the message
    static void Signal() {
        Check("skyfall signal: the tag is pinned", SkyfallSignal.Tag == "QSR_SKYFALL_V1");
        Check("skyfall signal: the text is pinned",
            SkyfallSignal.Format(true, false, 0xA5) == "QSR_SKYFALL_V1|1|0|a5" && SkyfallSignal.Format(false, true, 0x07) == "QSR_SKYFALL_V1|0|1|07");
        bool round = true;
        foreach (bool h in new[] { false, true })
            foreach (bool f in new[] { false, true })
                for (int c = 0; c < 256; c++)
                    if (!SkyfallSignal.TryParse(SkyfallSignal.Format(h, f, (byte)c), out bool h2, out bool f2, out byte c2) || h2 != h || f2 != f || c2 != c) round = false;
        Check("skyfall signal: every combination round-trips", round);
        Check("skyfall signal: other mods' messages are refused",
            !SkyfallSignal.TryParse("CUSTOM_INGOTS_CONFIG_V1|abc", out _, out _, out _) && !SkyfallSignal.TryParse("PET_ROSTER|x|y", out _, out _, out _)
            && !SkyfallSignal.TryParse("PET_CLONE|1", out _, out _, out _));
        bool junk = true;
        foreach (string? s in new[] { null, "", "QSR_SKYFALL_V1", "QSR_SKYFALL_V1|", "QSR_SKYFALL_V1|1|0", "QSR_SKYFALL_V1|2|0|a5",
                                      "QSR_SKYFALL_V1|1|0|a", "QSR_SKYFALL_V1|1|0|zz", "QSR_SKYFALL_V1|1|0|a5|extra", "qsr_skyfall_v1|1|0|a5",
                                      " QSR_SKYFALL_V1|1|0|a5", "QSR_SKYFALL_V10|1|0|a5", "QSR_SKYFALL_V1|1|0|+5", "Hello there" })
            if (SkyfallSignal.TryParse(s, out _, out _, out _)) junk = false;
        Check("skyfall signal: junk is refused", junk);
        bool refusedH = true, refusedF = true; byte refusedC = 9;
        bool ok = SkyfallSignal.TryParse("junk", out refusedH, out refusedF, out refusedC);
        Check("skyfall signal: a refused text leaves the outputs false and 0", !ok && !refusedH && !refusedF && refusedC == 0);
        Check("skyfall signal: upper-case hex is read", SkyfallSignal.TryParse("QSR_SKYFALL_V1|1|1|A5", out bool uh, out bool uf, out byte uc) && uh && uf && uc == 0xA5);
        Check("skyfall signal: IsOurs knows its own messages, even broken ones",
            SkyfallSignal.IsOurs("QSR_SKYFALL_V1|junk") && SkyfallSignal.IsOurs(SkyfallSignal.Format(true, true, 1)) && !SkyfallSignal.IsOurs("QSR_SKYFALL_V1")
            && !SkyfallSignal.IsOurs("QSR_SKYFALL_V10|1|0|a5") && !SkyfallSignal.IsOurs("CUSTOM_INGOTS_CONFIG_V1|x") && !SkyfallSignal.IsOurs("PET_ROSTER|x")
            && !SkyfallSignal.IsOurs(null));
        string longest = SkyfallSignal.Format(true, true, 255);
        Check("skyfall signal: the text is plain ASCII and short", longest.All(ch => ch < 128) && longest.Length <= 32);
    }

    // ---------------------------------------------------------------- the console's player lookup
    static void Names() {
        string[] online = { "Carol", "Alice", "bob" };
        Check("skyfall names: one exact match comes back as spelled online", SkyfallNames.Match(online, "Bob", out string r1) == "bob" && r1 == "");
        Check("skyfall names: case and spaces round the name do not matter", SkyfallNames.Match(online, "  ALICE ", out _) == "Alice");
        Check("skyfall names: a part of a name is not a match",
            SkyfallNames.Match(online, "Ali", out string r2) == null && r2 == "no player named 'Ali' is online (online: Alice, bob, Carol)");
        Check("skyfall names: an unknown name lists who is online",
            SkyfallNames.Match(online, "Dave", out string r3) == null && r3 == "no player named 'Dave' is online (online: Alice, bob, Carol)");
        Check("skyfall names: two players whose names differ only in case are not guessed between",
            SkyfallNames.Match(new[] { "sam", "Zoe", "Sam" }, "SAM", out string r4) == null
            && r4 == "'SAM' matches more than one online player: Sam, sam (online: Sam, sam, Zoe)");
        Check("skyfall names: nobody online says so",
            SkyfallNames.Match(new string[0], "Bob", out string r5) == null && r5 == "no player named 'Bob' is online (online: nobody)");
        Check("skyfall names: an empty name asks for one", SkyfallNames.Match(online, "  ", out string r6) == null && r6 == "name a player (online: Alice, bob, Carol)");
        Check("skyfall names: blank entries in the online list are skipped",
            SkyfallNames.Match(new[] { "", "Alice", null! }, "alice", out _) == "Alice"
            && SkyfallNames.Match(new[] { " ", "Alice" }, "x", out string r7) == null && r7 == "no player named 'x' is online (online: Alice)");
    }

    // ---------------------------------------------------------------- who a dome catches
    static void Caught() {
        static HVec H(double x, double y, double z) => new HVec(x, y, z);
        Check("skyfall caught: a head over the middle is caught", SkyfallCaught.Inside(H(10, 201.7, -5), 10, 200, -5, 2.5));
        Check("skyfall caught: exactly at the dome's edge is caught", SkyfallCaught.Inside(H(12.5, 201.7, -5), 10, 200, -5, 2.5));
        Check("skyfall caught: just past the edge is not", !SkyfallCaught.Inside(H(12.51, 201.7, -5), 10, 200, -5, 2.5));
        Check("skyfall caught: only the distance along the ground counts",
            SkyfallCaught.Inside(H(11.5, 201.7, -3), 10, 200, -5, 2.5) && !SkyfallCaught.Inside(H(11.5, 201.7, -2.99), 10, 200, -5, 2.5));
        Check("skyfall caught: a head down to a metre below the ground is caught",
            SkyfallCaught.Inside(H(10, 199, -5), 10, 200, -5, 2.5) && !SkyfallCaught.Inside(H(10, 198.99, -5), 10, 200, -5, 2.5));
        Check("skyfall caught: a head up to the radius plus 3 m above the ground is caught",
            SkyfallCaught.Inside(H(10, 205.5, -5), 10, 200, -5, 2.5) && !SkyfallCaught.Inside(H(10, 205.51, -5), 10, 200, -5, 2.5));
        Check("skyfall caught: the Nail's 10 m dome reaches 13 m up and 10 m out",
            SkyfallCaught.Inside(H(17, 213, -5), 10, 200, -5, 10) && SkyfallCaught.Inside(H(20, 201, -5), 10, 200, -5, 10)
            && !SkyfallCaught.Inside(H(10, 213.01, -5), 10, 200, -5, 10));
        Check("skyfall caught: a broken head, ground or radius catches nobody",
            !SkyfallCaught.Inside(H(double.NaN, 201, -5), 10, 200, -5, 2.5) && !SkyfallCaught.Inside(H(10, 201, -5), 10, 200, -5, 0)
            && !SkyfallCaught.Inside(H(10, 201, -5), 10, 200, -5, double.NaN) && !SkyfallCaught.Inside(H(10, 201, -5), 10, double.NaN, -5, 2.5));
    }
}
```

- [ ] **Step 4: SkyfallGrants.** Append `internal sealed class SkyfallGrants`. Model it on `PathStore` (Path.cs:263-303), with a `Dictionary<int, string>` inside.
  - `public const string Header = "# Skyfall grants (QuickshotRing): account=name, one per line. Edit only while the server is stopped.";`
  - `Give`:
    - false for `account <= 0`, a null name, or a name whose `Trim()` is empty or contains `'\n'` or `'\r'`;
    - false when the stored name is already exactly the trimmed name;
    - otherwise it stores the trimmed name and returns true. That one rule covers both adding and replacing.
  - `Take`: `Remove`.
  - `FindByName`:
    - the trimmed query, compared `StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase` against each stored name;
    - exactly one hit gives its account;
    - none, an empty query, or two or more hits give null.
  - `All`: a new `List<KeyValuePair<int, string>>`, sorted by account ascending.
  - `Serialize`: `Header + "\n"`, then `account.ToString(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture) + "=" + name + "\n"` per entry, in `All`'s order.
  - `Parse(string? text)`:
    - null or empty gives an empty table;
    - split on `'\n'` and `Trim()` each line; skip empty lines and lines starting `#`;
    - split at the FIRST `'='`, at index > 0 (`56=Dave=Two` is account 56, name `Dave=Two`);
    - the key must pass `int.TryParse(key.Trim(), NumberStyles.Integer, CultureInfo.InvariantCulture, out a)`;
    - then `Give(a, rest)`, which applies the name rules and makes a later line win.

- [ ] **Step 5: SkyfallSignal.** Append `internal static class SkyfallSignal`:
  - `Format`: `Tag + "|" + (holds ? "1" : "0") + "|" + (festival ? "1" : "0") + "|" + check.ToString("x2", CultureInfo.InvariantCulture)`.
  - `IsOurs`: `text != null && text.StartsWith(Tag + "|", StringComparison.Ordinal)`.
  - `TryParse`:
    - it sets the outputs to false, false, 0 first;
    - it needs `IsOurs`, then `Split('|')` of the rest giving exactly 3 parts;
    - parts 0 and 1 must each be exactly `"0"` or `"1"`;
    - part 2 must be exactly two characters from `[0-9a-fA-F]`. Check the characters by hand: `byte.TryParse` with `HexNumber` would accept spaces;
    - only then does it set the outputs and return true.

- [ ] **Step 6: SkyfallNames.** Append `internal static class SkyfallNames` with `Match`:

  - `q = (query ?? "").Trim()`. Hits are the sorted names whose `Trim()` equals `q` under `OrdinalIgnoreCase`.
  - Exactly one hit: return it as spelled, with `refusal = ""`.
  - Otherwise return null with exactly one of these texts:

```
name a player (online: {list})                                                     // q is empty
no player named '{q}' is online (online: {list})                                   // no hit
'{q}' matches more than one online player: {hits joined by ", "} (online: {list})  // two or more hits
```

### Task 5: The claim: `ShotClaim.Sky` and `Marks` on the wire, `ServerCheck` clears them, old layout unchanged

**Files:**
- Modify: `QuickshotRing/Wire.cs`
  - `ShotClaim`, lines 9-31: two fields, plus `Copy` (23-26) and `ToString` (28-30);
  - the block's doc comment, lines 33-39;
  - constants, lines 41-42;
  - `Write`, lines 53-68;
  - `TryRead`, lines 81-95.
- Modify: `QuickshotRing/Rules.cs:214-219` (`ClaimRules.ServerCheck(ShotClaim)`).
- Create: `QuickshotRing.Tests/SkyfallWireTests.cs`.
- Modify: `QuickshotRing.Tests/Program.cs`: one Run line after the Skyfall Run lines that Tasks 2-4 added after line 92 (`RangeBuildTests.Run();`).

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes:
  - `internal enum SkyCode { None = 0, Skyfall = 1, Festival = 2, Fizzled = 3 }` (Task 2, `SkyfallRules.cs`);
  - `SkyfallRules.MarkBits` (= 17, Task 2).
- Produces:
  - `ShotClaim.Sky` (`public SkyCode Sky;`);
  - `ShotClaim.Marks` (`public List<uint> Marks = new List<uint>();`);
  - `ShotClaimWire.MaxMarks = 3`;
  - the text format: `... picks[...] sky:<Code> marks[a,b,c]`. Each part prints only when it is not the default.
  - Wire rule: `Sky` rides the 2 window bits only when the claim goes out unsteered (no steered bit on the wire). Marks ride the mark slot at `EntityBits` (17) each, at most 3, all or none.
  - `ClaimRules.ServerCheck` (both overloads) and `LockClaim.Server` return `Sky == None` and no marks.

**Why the layout keeps Version 1.** An old reader ignores the window when the steered bit is 0 (Wire.cs:81-83) and skips marks at 17 bits each (92-93). So a Skyfall claim still parses on old DLLs, and every claim without Skyfall stays bit for bit what 0.17.15 writes.

**Old senders.** Every sender since 0.16.0 writes an unsteered window as 0. A 0.15.x sender could set it only through the deleted Snap Draw orb. Say this in the doc comment.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the test file** `QuickshotRing.Tests/SkyfallWireTests.cs` (25 checks):

```csharp
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Linq;
using QuickshotRing;
using static Program;

// Skyfall (admin skill, 2026-11-19). Task 5: the claim's Skyfall code rides the window bits of an unsteered claim, and the
// sigil centre rides the mark slot. Task 8 adds the seam's pure rules below.
internal static class SkyfallWireTests {
    internal static void Run() {
        RoundTrips();
        Steered();
        OldLayout();
        Text();
        Cleared();
    }

    static ShotClaim Sky(SkyCode code, params uint[] marks) {
        var c = new ShotClaim { Kind = ShotKind.Early, TimeMs = 1500, Stage = 0, Sky = code };
        c.Marks.AddRange(marks);
        return c;
    }

    static ShotClaim? RoundTrip(ShotClaim c, out int bits) {
        var w = new FakeBits(); bits = ShotClaimWire.Write(w, c); w.Seal();
        return ShotClaimWire.TryRead(w, out ShotClaim? got) == WireRead.Ok ? got : null;
    }

    static string Bits(ShotClaim c) { var w = new FakeBits(); ShotClaimWire.Write(w, c); return w.Dump(); }

    // ---------------------------------------------------------------- round trips
    static void RoundTrips() {
        bool all = true;
        foreach (SkyCode code in new[] { SkyCode.Skyfall, SkyCode.Festival, SkyCode.Fizzled }) {
            ShotClaim? g = RoundTrip(Sky(code, 65536, 1024, 131071), out _);
            all &= g != null && g.Sky == code && g.Marks.SequenceEqual(new uint[] { 65536, 1024, 131071 })
                   && g.TimeMs == 1500 && g.Kind == ShotKind.Early && !g.Steered;
        }
        Check("skyfall wire: each Skyfall code and three marks round trip", all);
        RoundTrip(Sky(SkyCode.Skyfall, 1, 2, 3), out int bits);
        Check("skyfall wire: a Skyfall claim with three marks is 32 + 3 x 17 = 83 bits", bits == 83);
        var raw = new FakeBits(); ShotClaimWire.Write(raw, Sky(SkyCode.Festival)); raw.Seal(); raw.Read(4); raw.Read(2);
        Check("skyfall wire: the code is the window bits of an unsteered claim", raw.Read(2) == (uint)SkyCode.Festival && raw.Read(1) == 0);
        ShotClaim? plainMarks = RoundTrip(Sky(SkyCode.None, 1, 2), out _);
        Check("skyfall wire: marks without a code round trip", plainMarks != null && plainMarks.Sky == SkyCode.None && plainMarks.Marks.SequenceEqual(new uint[] { 1, 2 }));
        ShotClaim? four = RoundTrip(Sky(SkyCode.Skyfall, 1, 2, 3, 4), out _);
        Check("skyfall wire: at most three marks, the first three", four != null && four.Marks.SequenceEqual(new uint[] { 1, 2, 3 }) && ShotClaimWire.MaxMarks == 3);
        ShotClaim? wide = RoundTrip(Sky(SkyCode.Skyfall, 5, 200000, 7), out int wideBits);
        Check("skyfall wire: a mark over 17 bits drops every mark, never shifts them", wide != null && wide.Marks.Count == 0 && wide.Sky == SkyCode.Skyfall && wideBits == 32);
        var withTargets = new ShotClaim { Kind = ShotKind.Held, TimeMs = 900, Stage = 2, Targets = { 7, 9 }, Picks = { 1, 0 }, Sky = SkyCode.Festival };
        withTargets.Marks.AddRange(new uint[] { 10, 11, 12 });
        ShotClaim? gt = RoundTrip(withTargets, out _);
        Check("skyfall wire: an unsteered claim with targets carries its code and marks too", gt != null && gt.ToString() == withTargets.ToString());
        Check("skyfall wire: writes are repeatable", Bits(Sky(SkyCode.Skyfall, 1, 2, 3)) == Bits(Sky(SkyCode.Skyfall, 1, 2, 3)));
        var al = new FakeBits(); al.Write(0x3FF, 10); ShotClaimWire.Write(al, Sky(SkyCode.Skyfall, 1, 2, 3)); al.Write(0x2A, 6); al.Seal(); al.Read(10);
        Check("skyfall wire: the reader stays aligned after the marks", ShotClaimWire.TryRead(al, out ShotClaim? ga) == WireRead.Ok && ga!.Marks.Count == 3 && al.Read(6) == 0x2A);
        Check("skyfall wire: a mark is as wide as the old mark slot", SkyfallRules.MarkBits == ShotClaimWire.EntityBits);
    }

    // ---------------------------------------------------------------- the steered bit wins
    static void Steered() {
        var s = new ShotClaim { Kind = ShotKind.Held, Steered = true, Locks = 2, TimeMs = 1500, Stage = 15, Targets = { 7, 8 }, Sky = SkyCode.Skyfall };
        ShotClaim? g = RoundTrip(s, out _);
        Check("skyfall wire: a steered claim ignores Sky (the window holds its locks)", g != null && g.Steered && g.Locks == 2 && g.Sky == SkyCode.None);
        var noSky = s.Copy(); noSky.Sky = SkyCode.None;
        Check("skyfall wire: a steered claim's bits are the same with or without Sky", Bits(s) == Bits(noSky));
        var raw = new FakeBits(); raw.Write(1, 4); raw.Write(2, 2); raw.Write(2, 2); raw.Write(1, 1); raw.Write(300, 13); raw.Write(0, 5); raw.Write(0, 3); raw.Write(0, 2); raw.Seal();
        Check("skyfall wire: a steered bit with no target reads unsteered and no Sky",
              ShotClaimWire.TryRead(raw, out ShotClaim? rg) == WireRead.Ok && !rg!.Steered && rg.Locks == 0 && rg.Sky == SkyCode.None);
        ShotClaim? ng = RoundTrip(new ShotClaim { Steered = true, Locks = 2, Sky = SkyCode.Skyfall }, out _);
        Check("skyfall wire: a lock with no target is written unsteered, so its Sky rides", ng != null && !ng.Steered && ng.Sky == SkyCode.Skyfall);
    }

    // ---------------------------------------------------------------- every claim without Skyfall: the 0.17.15 bits (the HomingTests.Unchanged idea)
    static void OldLayout() {
        var min = new ShotClaim { Kind = ShotKind.Early, TimeMs = null, Stage = 0 };
        var rm = new FakeBits(); rm.Write(1, 4); rm.Write(0, 2); rm.Write(0, 2); rm.Write(0, 1); rm.Write(8191, 13); rm.Write(0, 5); rm.Write(0, 3); rm.Write(0, 2);
        Check("skyfall wire: a plain minimal claim is the old layout bit for bit", Bits(min) == rm.Dump());
        var held = new ShotClaim { Kind = ShotKind.Held, TimeMs = 1700, Stage = 3, Targets = { 7, 9 }, Picks = { 1, 0, 7 } };
        var rh = new FakeBits(); rh.Write(1, 4); rh.Write(2, 2); rh.Write(0, 2); rh.Write(0, 1); rh.Write(1700, 13); rh.Write(3, 5); rh.Write(2, 3); rh.Write(7, 17); rh.Write(9, 17);
        rh.Write(1, 3); rh.Write(0, 3); rh.Write(7, 3); rh.Write(0, 2);
        Check("skyfall wire: a claim with targets and picks is the old layout bit for bit", Bits(held) == rh.Dump());
        var steer = new ShotClaim { Kind = ShotKind.Charged, Steered = true, Locks = 2, TimeMs = 2400, Stage = 1, Targets = { 5, 6 }, Picks = { 1 } };
        var rs = new FakeBits(); rs.Write(1, 4); rs.Write(3, 2); rs.Write(1, 2); rs.Write(1, 1); rs.Write(2400, 13); rs.Write(1, 5); rs.Write(2, 3); rs.Write(5, 17); rs.Write(6, 17);
        rs.Write(1, 3); rs.Write(0, 2);
        Check("skyfall wire: a steered claim is the H1 layout bit for bit", Bits(steer) == rs.Dump());
    }

    // ---------------------------------------------------------------- text and copy
    static void Text() {
        Check("skyfall wire: without Sky or marks the text is unchanged",
              new ShotClaim { Kind = ShotKind.Held, TimeMs = 1234, Stage = 5 }.ToString() == "Held steer0 t1234ms stage5 targets[] picks[]");
        Check("skyfall wire: Sky and marks print after the picks",
              Sky(SkyCode.Festival, 1, 2, 3).ToString() == "Early steer0 t1500ms stage0 targets[] picks[] sky:Festival marks[1,2,3]");
        Check("skyfall wire: marks alone print without a code",
              Sky(SkyCode.None, 4).ToString() == "Early steer0 t1500ms stage0 targets[] picks[] marks[4]");
        var o = Sky(SkyCode.Skyfall, 4, 5, 6); var cp = o.Copy(); cp.Marks.Add(9);
        Check("skyfall wire: a copy keeps Sky and its own list of marks", cp.Sky == SkyCode.Skyfall && cp.Marks.Count == 4 && o.Marks.SequenceEqual(new uint[] { 4, 5, 6 }));
    }

    // ---------------------------------------------------------------- the server never relays a client's code or marks
    static void Cleared() {
        var got = new ShotClaim { Kind = ShotKind.Held, TimeMs = 1500, Stage = 15, Targets = { 7 }, Sky = SkyCode.Festival };
        got.Marks.AddRange(new uint[] { 1, 2, 3 });
        ShotClaim c = ClaimRules.ServerCheck(got);
        Check("skyfall wire: the server's check clears Sky and marks", c.Sky == SkyCode.None && c.Marks.Count == 0 && c.Kind == ShotKind.Held && c.TimeMs == 1500 && c.Stage == 15);
        Check("skyfall wire: the server's check leaves its input untouched", got.Sky == SkyCode.Festival && got.Marks.Count == 3);
        Func<uint, TargetFacts> facts = id => new TargetFacts(true, true, false, true, 10f);
        ShotClaim kept = ClaimRules.ServerCheck(got, facts, 60f);
        Check("skyfall wire: the target check clears them too", kept.Sky == SkyCode.None && kept.Marks.Count == 0 && kept.Targets.SequenceEqual(new uint[] { 7 }));
        ShotClaim locked = LockClaim.Server(got, new uint[] { 7 }, false, facts, 60f);
        Check("skyfall wire: the lock check clears them too", locked.Sky == SkyCode.None && locked.Marks.Count == 0);
    }
}
```

**Files:**

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the looks-only test.** Add to `QuickshotRing.Tests/SkyfallPatternTests.cs` (add `using System.Linq;` and `using System.Reflection;` if missing), and call `LooksOnlyKeepThePattern();` as the last line of its `Run()`:

```csharp
// Task 6: the eight looks-only [Skyfall] settings are per client, so none may change what the server or the pattern decides.
static void LooksOnlyKeepThePattern() {
    Check("skyfall looks-only: eight names, each a number field of SkyfallValues",
        SkyfallValues.LooksOnly.Length == 8 && SkyfallValues.LooksOnly.All(n => {
            FieldInfo? f = typeof(SkyfallValues).GetField(n, BindingFlags.Public | BindingFlags.Instance);
            return f != null && (f.FieldType == typeof(float) || f.FieldType == typeof(int));
        }));
    SkyfallValues d = SkyfallValues.Default.Clamped();
    var pose = new ShotPose { Pos = new HVec(3, 214, -7), Dir = new HVec(0.3, 0.8, 0.52).Normalized, Strength = 0.95f };
    HVec centre = SkyfallRules.SigilCentre(pose.Pos, pose.Dir, d);
    SkyImpact[] plan = SkyfallPattern.Plan(4242u, centre.X, centre.Z, pose.Pos.X, pose.Pos.Z, d);
    foreach (string name in SkyfallValues.LooksOnly) {
        SkyfallValues c = SkyfallValues.Default;
        FieldInfo? f = typeof(SkyfallValues).GetField(name, BindingFlags.Public | BindingFlags.Instance);
        object? old = f?.GetValue(c);
        if (f != null && old is int i) f.SetValue(c, i + 3);
        else if (f != null && old is float x) f.SetValue(c, x * 1.7f + 0.3f);
        c = c.Clamped();
        HVec cc = SkyfallRules.SigilCentre(pose.Pos, pose.Dir, c);
        bool same = Same(plan, SkyfallPattern.Plan(4242u, cc.X, cc.Z, pose.Pos.X, pose.Pos.Z, c))
            && SkyfallPattern.EndsAt(c) == SkyfallPattern.EndsAt(d)
            && cc.X == centre.X && cc.Y == centre.Y && cc.Z == centre.Z
            && SkyfallRules.Aimed(pose, c) == SkyfallRules.Aimed(pose, d)
            && SkyfallRules.Armed(pose, 1.2, c) == SkyfallRules.Armed(pose, 1.2, d)
            && SkyfallRules.ServerAccepts(true, false, pose, 1200, 1200, c) == SkyfallRules.ServerAccepts(true, false, pose, 1200, 1200, d);
        Check($"skyfall looks-only: {name} changes nothing the pattern, the trigger or the centre reads", same);
    }
}

static bool Same(SkyImpact[] a, SkyImpact[] b) {
    if (a.Length != b.Length) return false;
    for (int k = 0; k < a.Length; k++)
        if (a[k].Index != b[k].Index || a[k].Wave != b[k].Wave || a[k].X != b[k].X || a[k].Z != b[k].Z ||
            a[k].DomeAt != b[k].DomeAt || a[k].LandAt != b[k].LandAt || a[k].Nail != b[k].Nail) return false;
    return true;
}
```
(If Task 3's class already has a `Same` helper with this meaning, use it and drop this one.)

```csharp
};
```

  The descriptions, exact. Cfg.Shared adds the "[shared] " prefix itself.

| Key | Description |
|---|---|
| MinStrength | The trigger: the bow string must be drawn at least this far (the game's shot strength, 0 to 1) for a Skyfall cast. Below it every shot is a normal shot. |
| MinElevationDegrees | The trigger: the arrow must point at least this many degrees above level. Below it every shot is a normal shot. |
| ArmSeconds | Seconds the full draw at the sky must be held before a release casts Skyfall (the bow hand feels a thump when it is armed). The server checks the shot's time since the 68% crossing against it. A release before then is a normal shot. |
| SigilHeight | The sigil (the storm's centre) is on a level plane this many metres above the release point, where the aim meets it. |
| MinDistance | The sigil is never nearer than this many metres from the archer along the ground (aim steeper: nearer, down to this). |
| MaxDistance | The sigil is never farther than this many metres from the archer along the ground (aim flatter: farther, up to this). |
| SigilRadius | Clients: the sigil's radius in the sky, in metres (looks only). |
| RainRadius | The giant arrows land within this many metres of the point under the sigil. |
| BuildSeconds | Seconds from the release to the first landing: the sigil unfolds and every dome burns in (everyone's moment to run). |
| Waves | How many waves of giant arrows fall before the World-Nail. |
| ArrowsPerWave | Giant arrows in each wave. |
| WaveGapSeconds | Seconds between one wave and the next. |
| DomeRadius | Each landing's danger dome, in metres. Normal mode: a player whose head is inside it when its arrow lands dies (never the archer), and a creature inside it takes the full damage. Festival mode: a caught player sees OUT! instead. |
| DomeWarningSeconds | Every dome shows at least this many seconds before its arrow lands (later waves longer, never shorter). |
| MinImpactSpacing | Landing spots are at least this many metres apart (the game's explosion merges closer ones). |
| CasterClearance | Metres of clear ground the landing spots leave round where the archer released. |
| ArrowLength | Clients: each giant arrow's length in metres (looks only). |
| FallSeconds | Seconds a giant arrow takes to fall from the sigil to its dome. |
| ImpactDamage | Normal mode: damage to a creature inside a landing's dome, falling to half at BlastRadius. Players never take blast damage: they die only inside a dome. |
| ImpactCripple | Cripple (the game's knock-down damage) to a creature inside a landing's dome, falling as the damage does. In festival mode a creature takes only this, no damage. |
| BlastRadius | How far a landing reaches creatures, in metres: full inside the dome, half at this distance, nothing beyond. |
| NailLength | Clients: the World-Nail's length in metres (looks only). |
| NailGapSeconds | Seconds after the last wave before the World-Nail starts to fall. |
| NailSlideSeconds | Seconds the World-Nail takes to slide down through the sigil into the ground. |
| NailDomeRadius | The World-Nail's danger dome, in metres (the same rules as DomeRadius). |
| NailBlastRadius | How far the World-Nail's impact reaches creatures, in metres. |
| NailDamage | Normal mode: damage to a creature inside the World-Nail's dome, falling to half at NailBlastRadius. |
| NailStandSeconds | Seconds the World-Nail stands before it crumbles. The storm runs until then: the archer's next sky shot is a normal shot until it has crumbled (one storm per archer). |
| MaxLiveExplosions | Clients: at most this many explosion effects play at once; beyond that a landing shows only its spark and sound. |
| SkyDimMetres | Clients: the sky dims only for a player within this many metres of the sigil. |
| SkySun | Clients: the sun's strength while the sky is dimmed (1 = unchanged). |
| SkyFade | Clients: how far the sky fades while dimmed (0 = not at all; at most 0.95, above which the sky turns black). |
| SkyFadeSeconds | Clients: seconds the sky takes to dim, and to come back. |
| FestivalCareRadius | Festival mode: players whose head is within this many metres of a landing are healed and fed. |
| SpectatorTest | Debug, this machine only: the caster's own release also runs through the spectator path, and both landing patterns are logged, so one client can check that a spectator rebuilds the same storm. Off for play. |

### Task 7: Commands, the grant file, and the message (server send queue, client latch and swallow)

**Files:**
- Create: `QuickshotRing/SkyfallCommands.cs`, `QuickshotRing/SkyfallLink.cs`
- Modify: `QuickshotRing/SkyfallRules.cs`:
  - add `SkyfallSignal.Hidden` and `SkyfallSignal.Differs` inside Task 4's `SkyfallSignal`;
  - append `SkyfallOutbox`, `SkyfallSeen` and `SkyfallConsole` at the end of the file.
- Modify: `QuickshotRing/Skyfall.cs` (grants, festival, latch members)
- Modify: `QuickshotRing/TestRange.cs:176` (`WriteWhole` becomes `internal`)
- Modify: `QuickshotRing/Main.cs`: the section list after line 150; the console registration after `RangePhoto.Started();` (line 113 before Task 6); one tick line after the Seek tick at lines 197-198.
- Test: `QuickshotRing.Tests/SkyfallTablesTests.cs` (Task 4's class: four new methods, called last from its `Run()`)

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes:
  - contract: `SkyfallGrants` (`Give`, `Take`, `All`, `Serialize`, `Parse`), `SkyfallSignal.Tag/Format/TryParse`, `SkyfallNames.Match`;
  - Task 6: `Skyfall.cs`, `Cfg.SharedShort` (Cfg.cs:30);
  - `TestRange.WriteWhole` (TestRange.cs:176-193), `Builds.NameOf` (Builds.cs:30).
- Produces:
  - Unity-free (SkyfallRules.cs):
    - `SkyfallSignal.Hidden(string? text)`, `SkyfallSignal.Differs(byte serverCheck, byte localCheck)`;
    - `SkyfallOutbox` (`GiveUpSeconds`, `Waiting`, `Want`, `Done`, `Expired`; `Expired` = waited too long, warn once, never dropped while online);
    - `SkyfallSeen.Changed(IEnumerable<KeyValuePair<int, uint>>)`;
    - `SkyfallConsole` (texts and the take rule; signatures in step 6).
  - Hub (contract): `Skyfall.LocalHolds`, `LocalFestival`, `LocalCheck`, `Festival`, `Grants`, `Holds(int)`.
  - Hub, new members:
    - `Skyfall.GrantsFile` (string);
    - `Skyfall.SaveGrants()` (`string?`: null when written, else the error);
    - `Skyfall.LocalTakenAt` (`float`, -1 when none): `Time.time` when a "does not hold" message arrived after a "holds" one. This is for the design's fizzle rule, "The caster's game drops its storm when the 'taken' message reaches it" (Task 8 / 11 read it).
  - `SkyfallLink.Queue(int account)`, `SkyfallLink.QueueHolders()` (int: how many queued), `SkyfallLink.Tick()`.
  - `SkyfallCommands.Register()`.

**Decisions this task makes:**
- **Commands are registered only on the dedicated server** (`ApplicationManager.IsHeadless`). On a client the module would be hidden by `[ServerOnly]` anyway. Registering there would also make the client collect Root.Township's command list at start-up for nothing.
- **Grants load lazily, on the server's first `SkyfallLink.Tick`** (the getter `Skyfall.Grants`). Client code never reads them, so a client never touches the file.
- **The message is computed when it is sent, not when it is queued.** It carries `Holds(account)`, `Festival` and `Cfg.SharedShort` as they are then, so a queued message is never stale. There is one queue entry per account.
- **A message to a player without a `PlayerController`** (or before `PlayerCommunicationManager.Instance` exists) waits and is retried every frame for as long as the player is online (the design: "queued until the player's controller exists"). After `SkyfallOutbox.GiveUpSeconds` (60, as CustomIngots' ServerConfigSync) it warns once per account and keeps waiting. A player who goes offline is dropped; their next join is a rejoin.
- **A rejoin** is a new `Player` entity id for an account (`SkyfallSeen`), polled once a second over every online player. A holder is queued, and nobody else. A give to someone already online is not sent twice, because they were already seen.
- **`take` never stops a storm already falling.** The server and every client have already decided it, so stopping only the server's half would leave the looks out of step. `festival` changes only storms cast after it, because a storm's mode is fixed by its relayed code.
- **The client swallows any message starting `QSR_SKYFALL_`**, any version, so a later version's message never shows on this client. It latches only what `SkyfallSignal.TryParse` reads.
- **The latch is dropped when `Player.Current` changes away from a player that was set:** null, destroyed, or another player. It is not dropped on the first one being set, because the message can be read in the frame before the tick sees the new player.
- **No resend when the server's settings reload.** The constraint allows exactly give, take, festival and rejoin. A holder's game compares the check again at the next one.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the tests.** Add to `QuickshotRing.Tests/SkyfallTablesTests.cs` (add `using System.Collections.Generic;` and `using System.Linq;` if missing), and call `Hidden(); Outbox(); Seen(); ConsoleText();` as the last lines of its `Run()`:

```csharp
// Task 7: the quiet signal's filter, the server's send queue and rejoin poll, and the console's words.
static void Hidden() {
    Check("skyfall signal: a Skyfall message is hidden", SkyfallSignal.Hidden(SkyfallSignal.Format(true, false, 0x2a)));
    Check("skyfall signal: a later version's message is hidden too", SkyfallSignal.Hidden("QSR_SKYFALL_V2|1|1|00"));
    Check("skyfall signal: no text is not hidden", !SkyfallSignal.Hidden(null) && !SkyfallSignal.Hidden(""));
    Check("skyfall signal: an ordinary message is shown", !SkyfallSignal.Hidden("Welcome back!"));
    Check("skyfall signal: the tag is matched case for case", !SkyfallSignal.Hidden("qsr_skyfall_v1|1|0|2a"));
    Check("skyfall signal: a check byte that differs only above the build block's 6 bits is a mismatch",
        SkyfallSignal.TryParse(SkyfallSignal.Format(true, false, 0x65), out _, out _, out byte got)
        && ((got ^ 0x25) & BuildBits.CheckMask) == 0 && SkyfallSignal.Differs(got, 0x25));
    Check("skyfall signal: the same check byte is no mismatch", !SkyfallSignal.Differs(0xA5, 0xA5));
}

static void Outbox() {
    var o = new SkyfallOutbox();
    o.Want(5, 0f); o.Want(3, 1f); o.Want(5, 2f); o.Want(0, 2f);
    Check("skyfall outbox: one entry per account, in the order first wanted, never account 0", o.Waiting.SequenceEqual(new[] { 5, 3 }));
    Check("skyfall outbox: wanting again restarts the give-up clock", !o.Expired(5, 61f) && o.Expired(5, 62.5f));
    Check("skyfall outbox: an entry gives up after 60 s", !o.Expired(3, 60.9f) && o.Expired(3, 61.5f));
    o.Done(5);
    Check("skyfall outbox: done removes it", o.Waiting.SequenceEqual(new[] { 3 }));
    Check("skyfall outbox: an account not waiting never expires", !o.Expired(5, 1000f));
    o.Want(5, 100f);
    Check("skyfall outbox: wanted again after done goes to the back", o.Waiting.SequenceEqual(new[] { 3, 5 }));
}

static void Seen() {
    var s = new SkyfallSeen();
    KeyValuePair<int, uint> P(int account, uint session) => new KeyValuePair<int, uint>(account, session);
    Check("skyfall seen: everyone online at the first poll is new", s.Changed(new[] { P(1, 10u), P(2, 20u) }).SequenceEqual(new[] { 1, 2 }));
    Check("skyfall seen: the same sessions again are not", s.Changed(new[] { P(1, 10u), P(2, 20u) }).Count == 0);
    Check("skyfall seen: a new session for an account is a rejoin", s.Changed(new[] { P(1, 11u), P(2, 20u) }).SequenceEqual(new[] { 1 }));
    Check("skyfall seen: leaving is not a change", s.Changed(new[] { P(2, 20u) }).Count == 0);
    Check("skyfall seen: coming back is, even with the same session id", s.Changed(new[] { P(1, 11u), P(2, 20u) }).SequenceEqual(new[] { 1 }));
    Check("skyfall seen: account 0 is never reported", s.Changed(new[] { P(0, 5u), P(2, 20u) }).Count == 0);
}

static void ConsoleText() {
    bool a, b, c, d;
    Check("skyfall console: on, yes, 1 and true (any case, spaces round it) mean on",
        SkyfallConsole.TryOnOff("on", out a) && a && SkyfallConsole.TryOnOff(" YES ", out b) && b &&
        SkyfallConsole.TryOnOff("1", out c) && c && SkyfallConsole.TryOnOff("True", out d) && d);
    Check("skyfall console: off, no, 0 and false mean off",
        SkyfallConsole.TryOnOff("off", out a) && !a && SkyfallConsole.TryOnOff("No", out b) && !b &&
        SkyfallConsole.TryOnOff("0", out c) && !c && SkyfallConsole.TryOnOff("false", out d) && !d);
    Check("skyfall console: anything else is not understood",
        !SkyfallConsole.TryOnOff("maybe", out _) && !SkyfallConsole.TryOnOff("", out _) && !SkyfallConsole.TryOnOff(null, out _));

    SkyfallGrants g = SkyfallGrants.Parse("");
    Check("skyfall console: status with no holders", SkyfallConsole.Status(g.All, new HashSet<int>(), false) ==
        "Skyfall: nobody holds it. Festival mode is off. Use: skyfall give <player>, or skyfall give all (players online now).");
    g.Give(2000000002, "VMan"); g.Give(2000000001, "Blue_XD");
    Check("skyfall console: status lists holders by name, with account and whether online", SkyfallConsole.Status(g.All, new HashSet<int> { 2000000001 }, true) ==
        "Skyfall: 2 holder(s): Blue_XD (2000000001, online), VMan (2000000002, offline). Festival mode is on.");

    Check("skyfall console: give", SkyfallConsole.Given("Blue_XD", 2000000001, false, null) ==
        "Skyfall given to Blue_XD (2000000001). Their game is told quietly; nothing shows on anyone's screen.");
    Check("skyfall console: give to a holder", SkyfallConsole.Given("Blue_XD", 2000000001, true, null) ==
        "Blue_XD (2000000001) already holds Skyfall; their game is told again.");
    Check("skyfall console: give when the file cannot be written", SkyfallConsole.Given("Blue_XD", 2000000001, false, "disk full") ==
        "Skyfall given to Blue_XD (2000000001), but skyfall-grants.txt could not be written (disk full): the grant lasts until the server restarts.");
    Check("skyfall console: give all with nobody online", SkyfallConsole.GivenAll(new string[0], new string[0], null) ==
        "Nobody is online. skyfall give all gives Skyfall to the players online now.");
    Check("skyfall console: give all when everyone holds it", SkyfallConsole.GivenAll(new string[0], new[] { "Blue_XD", "VMan" }, null) ==
        "Everyone online already holds Skyfall: Blue_XD, VMan.");
    Check("skyfall console: give all", SkyfallConsole.GivenAll(new[] { "VMan", "Foo" }, new[] { "Blue_XD" }, null) ==
        "Skyfall given to 2 player(s): VMan, Foo. Already held: Blue_XD.");
    Check("skyfall console: a player still joining", SkyfallConsole.NoAccount("VMan") ==
        "Not given: VMan has no account id yet (still joining?). Try again in a moment.");
    Check("skyfall console: no such online player", SkyfallConsole.NotGiven("No online player 'x'.") == "Not given. No online player 'x'.");

    var online = new[] { new KeyValuePair<int, string>(2000000001, "Blue_XD"), new KeyValuePair<int, string>(2000000003, "Foo") };
    Check("skyfall console: take finds a holder by their saved name, any case", SkyfallConsole.TakeTarget(g, "blue_xd", online, out _) == 2000000001);
    Check("skyfall console: take finds an offline holder", SkyfallConsole.TakeTarget(g, "VMan", online, out _) == 2000000002);
    Check("skyfall console: take refuses an online player who does not hold it",
        SkyfallConsole.TakeTarget(g, "foo", online, out string r1) == null && r1 == "Foo does not hold Skyfall.");
    Check("skyfall console: take refuses a name nobody has, naming the holders",
        SkyfallConsole.TakeTarget(g, "vamn", online, out string r2) == null && r2 == "Nobody called 'vamn' holds Skyfall. Holders: Blue_XD, VMan.");
    Check("skyfall console: take with no holders at all",
        SkyfallConsole.TakeTarget(SkyfallGrants.Parse(""), "vamn", online, out string r3) == null && r3 == "Nobody holds Skyfall.");
    SkyfallGrants renamed = SkyfallGrants.Parse(""); renamed.Give(2000000003, "OldFoo");
    Check("skyfall console: take finds an online holder saved under an older name", SkyfallConsole.TakeTarget(renamed, "Foo", online, out _) == 2000000003);

    Check("skyfall console: take from an online player", SkyfallConsole.Taken("Blue_XD", 2000000001, true, null) ==
        "Skyfall taken from Blue_XD (2000000001). Their game is told quietly.");
    Check("skyfall console: take from an offline player", SkyfallConsole.Taken("VMan", 2000000002, false, null) ==
        "Skyfall taken from VMan (2000000002). They are offline; nothing is sent.");
    Check("skyfall console: take when the file cannot be written", SkyfallConsole.Taken("VMan", 2000000002, false, "locked") ==
        "Skyfall taken from VMan (2000000002). They are offline; nothing is sent. But skyfall-grants.txt could not be written (locked): the grant comes back when the server restarts.");

    Check("skyfall console: festival with no word says the state", SkyfallConsole.FestivalNow(false) ==
        "Festival mode is off. Use: skyfall festival on, or skyfall festival off.");
    Check("skyfall console: festival on", SkyfallConsole.FestivalSet(true, true, 2) ==
        "Festival mode on: storms cast from now on heal, feed and knock over, and kill nobody. 2 online holder(s) told. Storms already falling keep their mode.");
    Check("skyfall console: festival off", SkyfallConsole.FestivalSet(false, true, 0) ==
        "Festival mode off: storms cast from now on kill players caught in a dome. 0 online holder(s) told. Storms already falling keep their mode.");
    Check("skyfall console: festival unchanged", SkyfallConsole.FestivalSet(true, false, 2) == "Festival mode is already on.");
    Check("skyfall console: a failed command", SkyfallConsole.Failed("boom") == "Skyfall: the command failed: boom. See the server's MelonLoader log.");
}
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Add `SkyfallOutbox`** at the end of `SkyfallRules.cs`. It is the server's send queue, at most one entry per account:
  - `public const float GiveUpSeconds = 60f;`
  - `public IReadOnlyList<int> Waiting { get; }`, in the order first wanted;
  - `public void Want(int account, float now)`: account 0 is ignored; a new account goes to the back; one already waiting keeps its place but its give-up clock restarts at `now`;
  - `public void Done(int account)`;
  - `public bool Expired(int account, float now)`: waiting, and `now - since > GiveUpSeconds`.

- [ ] **Step 5: Add `SkyfallSeen`** after it. `public List<int> Changed(IEnumerable<KeyValuePair<int, uint>> online)` takes the online players as (account, the `Player` entity's network id). It returns, in the given order, the accounts that are new since the last call: unseen, or seen with another id, or back after a poll without them. Account 0 is never returned. It then remembers exactly this call's list (anyone absent is forgotten).

  - `public const string GrantFile = "skyfall-grants.txt", GiveUsage = "Use: skyfall give <player>, or skyfall give all (players online now).", TakeUsage = "Use: skyfall take <player> (offline holders too; skyfall lists them).", FestivalUsage = "Use: skyfall festival on, or skyfall festival off.";`

  - `public static string GivenAll(IList<string> given, IList<string> already, string? saveError)`. When `given.Count > 0` and `saveError != null`, append `$" But {GrantFile} could not be written ({saveError}): these grants last until the server restarts."`.
  - `public static string NoAccount(string name)`, `public static string NotGiven(string refusal) => "Not given. " + refusal;`.
  - `public static int? TakeTarget(SkyfallGrants grants, string query, IEnumerable<KeyValuePair<int, string>> online, out string refusal)`, in this order (it compares names itself, so it does not depend on `FindByName`'s case rule):
    1. a grant whose saved name equals the trimmed query (`OrdinalIgnoreCase`) gives its account;
    2. else an online player whose name equals it: their account if it holds a grant, else the refusal `"{online name} does not hold Skyfall."`;
    3. else the refusal `"Nobody holds Skyfall."` when there are no grants, or `"Nobody called '{query}' holds Skyfall. Holders: {sorted names}."`.
    
    `refusal` is `""` when an account is returned.
  - `public static string Taken(string name, int account, bool online, string? saveError)`, `FestivalNow(bool on)`, `FestivalSet(bool on, bool changed, int told)`, `Failed(string message)`.

- [ ] **Step 10: Create `QuickshotRing/SkyfallLink.cs`, the server half.**
  - `internal static class SkyfallLink`. The header comment states:
    - the one exception to the no-new-traffic rule, quoting CLAUDE.md's wording from Task 13;
    - what the message holds;
    - when it is sent;
    - that it goes to the holder only.
  - State: `static readonly SkyfallOutbox outbox`, `static readonly SkyfallSeen seen`, `static readonly HashSet<int> warned` (accounts already warned about a long wait; an account leaves it at `Done`), `static float nextPoll`, `const float PollSeconds = 1f`, and the lists reused per poll.
  - `internal static void Queue(int account) => outbox.Want(account, Time.unscaledTime);`.
  - `internal static int QueueHolders()`: every online player (`Player.AllPlayers`, `p.UserInfo != null`) whose account `Skyfall.Holds` is queued. Returns how many.
  - `internal static void Tick()`: if `NetworkSceneManager.IsServer`, the server tick, else the client tick (step 11). The server tick:
    1. `_ = Skyfall.Grants;` (the first tick loads the file);
    2. when `Time.unscaledTime >= nextPoll` (every `PollSeconds`): `seen.Changed(...)` over every online player as `(p.UserInfo.Identifier, p.Identifier)` (players without `UserInfo` skipped); each changed account that `Skyfall.Holds` is queued;
    3. `if (outbox.Waiting.Count == 0) return;`, then for each account in a copy of `Waiting`, `Send(account, now)`.
  - `Send` has the section `SkyfallLink.Send (one signal)`:
    - not online (`Player.SafeGetPlayer(account, out Player p)` false): `outbox.Done`, plus a Debug line. This is the only way an entry is dropped unsent;
    - `p.PlayerController == null || PlayerCommunicationManager.Instance == null`: leave it waiting. When `outbox.Expired(account, now)` and `warned.Add(account)`, warn (below) once;
    - else `PlayerCommunicationManager.Instance.SendMessageToPlayer(p, SkyfallSignal.Format(Skyfall.Holds(account), Skyfall.Festival, Cfg.SharedShort), 0f)` (duration 0, as CustomIngots), `outbox.Done(account)`, and the Summary line below.
    - Every `outbox.Done(account)` also does `warned.Remove(account)`.
  - Log lines, exact:
    - Summary: `[Archery] Skyfall: told '{Builds.NameOf(p)}''s game quietly: {(holds ? "holds Skyfall" : "does not hold Skyfall")}, festival mode {(festival ? "on" : "off")}`
    - Warning: `[Archery] Skyfall: account {account}'s game is still waiting for the signal after {SkyfallOutbox.GiveUpSeconds:0} s ({(p.PlayerController == null ? "no body yet" : "the game's message manager is missing")}); it is sent as soon as it can be`

  - `internal static void Register()`:

```csharp
try {
    CommandService.CommandCollection.ProcessModule(typeof(SkyfallCommands), typeof(SkyfallCommands).Assembly);
```

  - The four commands are private static methods returning `string`. Each opens with its section (`command SkyfallCommands.<Name>`). Its whole body is in `try { ... } catch (Exception e) { MelonLogger.Warning($"[Archery] Skyfall: console command failed: {e}"); return SkyfallConsole.Failed(e.Message); }`, so none ever throws. A parameter with a default makes the word optional, so a bare `skyfall give` gets the usage text instead of the game's "Command not found".
  - The online list for each call is `Player.AllPlayers` as `Player` with `UserInfo != null`: name `Builds.NameOf(p)`, account `p.UserInfo.Identifier`.
    - `[Command(null, "Who holds Skyfall, and whether festival mode is on")] [Alias("status")] static string Status()`: `SkyfallConsole.Status(Skyfall.Grants.All, <online accounts>, Skyfall.Festival)`.
    - `[Command("give", "Gives Skyfall to an online player: skyfall give <player>, or skyfall give all")] static string Give(string player = "")`, where `q = player.Trim()`:
      - empty: `GiveUsage`;
      - `q` equal to `"all"` (`OrdinalIgnoreCase`): every online player with account != 0. Already held goes into `already`; else `Skyfall.Grants.Give(account, name)` and into `given`. Every one of them is `SkyfallLink.Queue`d. `SaveGrants()` runs only when `given.Count > 0`. Return `GivenAll(given, already, err)`;
      - else `SkyfallNames.Match(<online names>, q, out string refusal)`: null gives `NotGiven(refusal)`; the match is found again with `Player.GetPlayer(name)`; account 0 gives `NoAccount(name)`. Otherwise `bool held = Skyfall.Holds(account); bool changed = Skyfall.Grants.Give(account, name); string? err = changed ? Skyfall.SaveGrants() : null; SkyfallLink.Queue(account);` and return `Given(name, account, held, err)`.
    - `[Command("take", "Takes Skyfall from a player, online or not: skyfall take <player>")] static string Take(string player = "")`:
      - empty: `TakeUsage`;
      - else `SkyfallConsole.TakeTarget(Skyfall.Grants, q, <online (account, name)>, out string refusal)`: null gives `refusal`;
      - else take the saved name for the reply (from `Grants.All`, before taking), `Skyfall.Grants.Take(account)` and `err = Skyfall.SaveGrants()`. When the account is online, `SkyfallLink.Queue(account)`. Return `Taken(name, account, online, err)`.
    - `[Command("festival", "Festival mode for storms cast from now on: skyfall festival on, or skyfall festival off")] static string Festival(string state = "")`:
      - empty: `FestivalNow(Skyfall.Festival)`;
      - not `TryOnOff`: `FestivalUsage`;
      - the same as now: `FestivalSet(on, false, 0)`;
      - else `Skyfall.Festival = on; int told = SkyfallLink.QueueHolders();` and return `FestivalSet(on, true, told)`.
  - Commands only change state and queue sends. Every send happens in `SkyfallLink.Tick`. One Summary line per change, exact:
    - `[Archery] Skyfall: given to '{name}' ({account}) from the console`
    - `[Archery] Skyfall: given to {given.Count} player(s) from the console (give all): {string.Join(", ", given)}`
    - `[Archery] Skyfall: taken from '{name}' ({account}) from the console`
    - `[Archery] Skyfall: festival mode {(on ? "on" : "off")} from the console; {told} online holder(s) told`
    
    A failed file write has already warned in `SaveGrants`.

### Task 8: The seam: pose record, shooter request, server decision and relay, `Draws.Release` first, `Volley.Fire` branch, stand-aside guards, real-arrow hold and destroy, fizzle, SpectatorTest

This is the riskiest task. Every bow shot in the game passes through the code it touches. The rule it lives by: **a release that is not a Skyfall cast reaches exactly the code it reaches today**. Every new line on the common path is either:
- one dictionary lookup that returns at once (`IsCast`, `TryPose`, `ClaimedSky`, the `Decide*` calls on a plain release), or
- a record that nothing reads unless the arrow is a Skyfall release (`RecordPose`).
