    ```csharp
    internal enum SkyWhy { Plain = 0, Cast, NotRequested, NotGranted, Busy, NoPose, NotAimed, NotArmed, NoCentre, CentreFar }
    internal sealed class SkyVerdict { public SkyCode Code; public SkyWhy Why; public List<uint> Marks = new List<uint>(); }
    internal static class SkyfallSeam {
        public const int CentreMarks = 3;                 // x, y, z offsets of the sigil centre from the release point
        public const double CentreSlackMetres = 2.0;      // server: the shooter's centre is kept within this of its own
        public const float BusySlackSeconds = 0.5f;       // the caster's game stays busy this much longer, the server this much shorter
        public const float DestroySeconds = 1.0f;         // server: the held real arrow is destroyed this long after the release
        public const float ClientHoldSeconds = 5f;        // clients: a held arrow still there this long after the release is let go
        public static readonly HVec Unknown;              // (NaN, NaN, NaN): no centre / no release point
        public static readonly Resolution Neutral;        // the Release.cs:198 literal
        public static List<uint> MarksOf(HVec release, HVec centre);
        public static bool TryCentre(HVec release, IList<uint>? marks, out HVec centre);    // false (centre = Unknown) unless exactly 3 marks and a finite release
        public static bool ShooterAsks(bool holds, bool busy, int? elapsedMs, SkyfallValues v);
        public static bool Finalise(ShotClaim claim, ShotPose wire, int? elapsedMs, SkyfallValues v);   // true: the claim is now a request
        public static ShotClaim AsSky(ShotClaim c, SkyCode code, IList<uint> marks);        // in place; returns c
        public static SkyVerdict Server(bool requested, bool granted, bool busy, ShotPose? pose, int? claimedMs, int? effectiveMs,
                                        IList<uint>? marks, bool festival, SkyfallValues v);
        public static ShotClaim Relay(ShotClaim? checkedClaim, SkyVerdict verdict, int? effectiveMs);  // a new claim; the input untouched
        public static SkyCode Seen(ShotClaim? relay, HVec release, out HVec centre);
        public static SkyStorm Storm(uint arrowId, SkyCode code, HVec release, HVec centre, int casterAccount, float startTime, SkyfallValues v);
        public static float BusyUntil(float startTime, SkyfallValues v, bool server);
        public static bool SamePattern(SkyStorm a, SkyStorm b, double metres);
        public static string Describe(SkyStorm s);
    }
    ```
  - `Skyfall.cs`: the contract's `RecordPose`, `TryPose`, `IsCast`, `CasterBusy` and `Begin`, plus:
    ```csharp
    internal static ShotPose PoseOf(in ShootArguments a);                       // every machine: the release as it went on the wire
    internal static int OwnAccount { get; }                                     // client: Player.Current's UserInfo.Identifier, 0 unknown
    internal static void Ask(uint arrowId, int? elapsedMs);                     // shooter, Claims.Shot
    internal static void Finalise(uint arrowId, ShotClaim claim);               // shooter, Claims.Write
    internal static SkyCode DecideServer(NetworkEntity arrow, Player? owner, int? effectiveMs, int? claimedMs);
    internal static SkyCode DecideShooter(NetworkEntity arrow);
    internal static SkyCode DecideSeen(NetworkEntity arrow, ShotClaim? relay, Player? shooter);
    internal static void Fire(BowString bow, NetworkEntity arrow);              // Volley.Fire's branch
    internal static class Held { internal static void Hold(Arrow arrow, uint release); internal static void Tick(); internal static void Shutdown(); internal static int Count { get; } }
    internal static void Tick();                                                // Main.OnUpdate, every machine, before the headless return
    ```
  - `Claims.cs`:
    - `internal static SkyCode ClaimedSky(uint arrowId, out List<uint>? marks)`;
    - `internal static void PutSkyfall(uint arrowId, SkyVerdict verdict, int? effectiveMs)`.
  - **For Tasks 9, 11 and 12:**
    - **Calls into later tasks.** `Begin` can call neither `SkyfallServer.Start` nor `SkyfallLook.Start` yet, because those types do not exist until Tasks 9 and 11. `Begin` carries two marked lines, and Tasks 9 and 11 each replace theirs.
    - **Fizzled storms.** `Begin` is called for Fizzled storms too: `Impacts` is empty, and `CX/CY/CZ` are NaN when the centre is unknown. `SkyfallServer.Start` is called only for Skyfall and Festival. `SkyfallLook.Start` gets a fizzle too, and draws only the sigil's puff, or nothing when the centre is NaN.
    - **The held arrow is this task's.** Its hold and its destroy belong to Task 8 (`Skyfall.Held`), not `SkyfallServer`. Task 9 must not destroy it.
    - **No mesh copies** (ruling 12). Tasks 11 and 12 build every arrow look from `SkyfallLook.PlainArrow()`.
    - **Hold looks.** `Skyfall.Fire` does not end Task 10's hold looks; Task 10 ends them itself at the release.

**How a Skyfall shot runs** (verified in D Shooter.cs:45-131, D BowString.cs:155-158, D ExtraArrowUpdater.cs:63):
- **Shooter:**
  1. `Claims.Shot` builds today's claim, then `Skyfall.Ask` (latch, not busy, draw clock) marks the arrow as a candidate.
  2. `Arrow.Shoot` adds the deviation; `new ShootArguments` compresses the rotation.
  3. `Claims.Bits` Write runs `RecordPose` (the WIRE pose), then `Claims.Write` runs `Skyfall.Finalise`. `Armed` on the wire pose makes the claim a request (code 1, centre marks, its locks' cages let go), or drops it so the claim goes out exactly as built.
  4. OnShoot -> `Volley.Fire` -> `Draws.Release` -> `DecideShooter`.
  5. `Skyfall.Fire` holds the arrow and calls `Begin`.
- **Server:**
  1. `Claims.Bits` Read runs `RecordPose` (the decoded pose); `incoming` keeps the raw request.
  2. `Claims.Shot` -> `Check` -> `ServerCheck` clears Sky and marks from the claim it keeps.
  3. `Arrow.Shoot`, which sends the flight sync.
  4. OnShoot -> `Draws.Release` -> `DecideServer`. This reads the raw request (`ClaimedSky`), the grant, the busy test, the pose, and the claimed and effective times, decides `SkyfallSeam.Server`, and puts the relay (`PutSkyfall`).
  5. `Skyfall.Fire`: destroys any extras, `ForgetServer`, holds, `Begin`.
  6. `DestroyShotArrow` is skipped by its prefix.
  7. The relay is sent (D Shooter.cs:64).
  8. `Held.Tick` destroys the arrow 1 s later.
- **Spectator:**
  1. `Claims.Bits` Read runs `RecordPose` and `incoming.Put(relay)`.
  2. OnShoot -> `Draws.Release` -> `DecideSeen` reads the relay through `SkyfallSeam.Seen`.
  3. `Skyfall.Fire` holds the arrow and calls `Begin`.

**The map's gaps, settled:**
- **Shooter.Shoot prefix order (gap 3): no `[HarmonyPriority]` is needed.**
  - `Claims.Shot` only reads the draw clock and the latch.
  - The strength and pose that decide the cast are read from `ShootArguments` at Write. Write runs after every prefix (HolyArrow.ReleaseStrength's raise included) and after Arrow.Shoot's deviation.
  - The server and the spectators read the same quantised values.
- **Several prefixes on `Arrow.StartHandlingArrowDeviance` (gap 4): no priority is needed.**
  - The installed HarmonyX (`MelonLoader\net35\0Harmony.dll`, `HarmonyLib.Public.Patching.HarmonyManipulator.WritePrefixes`, decompiled 2026-11-19) calls EVERY prefix and ANDs their bool results into `__runOriginal`. So the original is skipped if any one of them returns false, whatever the order.
  - For a Skyfall-held arrow:
    - `Quickdraw4.Detect` returns true without acting (speed < 998);
    - `Seek.HeldFlight` returns true (the arrow is not in Seek's holds);
    - Skyfall's `HeldFlight` returns false and stops it.
- **Relay versus destroy (gap 5).**
  - The relay is sent in the same server frame, right after `Shoot` returns (D Shooter.cs:64). The destroy is `DestroySeconds` (1 s) later, from `Held.Tick`.
  - A flight sync that reaches a spectator before the relay starts a flight that the relayed `Shoot`'s hold stops at the bow.
  - One that arrives after the relay is refused by `HeldFlight`.
  - A destroy that overtook a relay by more than 1 s would leave that spectator with no storm, and no other harm.
- **Gap 8, `DestroyShotArrow` staying subscribed.** The new prefix does what the game's own method does, minus the destroy: `IsIgnoringCollisionOnShoot = true`, and it unsubscribes itself. Skyfall therefore does not depend on `ShotReset`'s `Reset(true)` unsubscribing it. If it stayed subscribed, it would sacrifice the caster's NEXT arrow.

---

**Part A: the pure rules (TDD)**

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the seam tests.** Add `Seam();` as the last call in `SkyfallWireTests.Run()`, then add these members to the class (41 checks):

```csharp
    // ================================================================ Task 8: the seam's pure rules (SkyfallSeam)
    static readonly SkyfallValues V = SkyfallValues.Default;
    static readonly HVec Release0 = new HVec(-835.6, 214.3, 386.9);   // the archery shrine's spawn point

    static ShotPose Pose(double elevationDegrees, float strength) {
        double e = elevationDegrees * Math.PI / 180.0;
        return new ShotPose { Pos = Release0, Dir = new HVec(0, Math.Sin(e), Math.Cos(e)), Strength = strength };
    }
    static bool Close(HVec a, HVec b, double metres) =>
        Math.Abs(a.X - b.X) <= metres && Math.Abs(a.Y - b.Y) <= metres && Math.Abs(a.Z - b.Z) <= metres;
    static HVec Centre45 => SkyfallRules.SigilCentre(Release0, Pose(45, 1f).Dir, V);
    static ShotClaim Normal() => new ShotClaim { Kind = ShotKind.Held, Steered = true, Locks = 1, TimeMs = 1500, Stage = 15, Targets = { 7, 9 }, Picks = { 1 } };
    static SkyVerdict Server(bool granted = true, bool busy = false, double elevation = 45, float strength = 1f, int? claimed = 1500, int? effective = 1500,
                             List<uint>? marks = null, bool festival = false, bool pose = true, bool requested = true) {
        ShotPose p = Pose(elevation, strength);
        List<uint> m = marks ?? SkyfallSeam.MarksOf(Release0, SkyfallRules.SigilCentre(Release0, p.Dir, V));
        return SkyfallSeam.Server(requested, granted, busy, pose ? p : (ShotPose?)null, claimed, effective, m, festival, V);
    }

    static void Seam() {
        SeamBasics();
        Asking();
        Finalising();
        Deciding();
        Relaying();
        Seeing();
        Storms();
        EndToEnd();
        Boundary();
    }

    static void SeamBasics() {
        Check("skyfall seam: the pinned numbers", SkyfallSeam.CentreMarks == 3 && SkyfallSeam.CentreSlackMetres == 2.0 && SkyfallSeam.BusySlackSeconds == 0.5f
              && SkyfallSeam.DestroySeconds == 1f && SkyfallSeam.ClientHoldSeconds == 5f);
        Check("skyfall seam: a cast is the resolver's empty release and never homes",
              SkyfallSeam.Neutral.ToString() == new Resolution(ShotKind.Early, UltimateUse.None, new ReleasePlan(0, 0, 0), 0).ToString()
              && SkyfallSeam.Neutral.ToString() == "Early ult:- ghosts 0 pellets 0 echoes 0 charge tier 0" && !LockRule.Steerable(SkyfallSeam.Neutral));
        var centre = new HVec(Release0.X + 12.3456, Release0.Y + 30, Release0.Z - 59.999);
        List<uint> m = SkyfallSeam.MarksOf(Release0, centre);
        Check("skyfall seam: the centre rides three marks to within a millimetre", m.Count == 3 && SkyfallSeam.TryCentre(Release0, m, out HVec back) && Close(back, centre, 0.001));
        Check("skyfall seam: anything but three marks is no centre",
              !SkyfallSeam.TryCentre(Release0, new List<uint> { 1, 2 }, out HVec none) && double.IsNaN(none.X) && !SkyfallSeam.TryCentre(Release0, null, out _));
        Check("skyfall seam: no release point is no centre", !SkyfallSeam.TryCentre(SkyfallSeam.Unknown, m, out _));
    }

    static void Asking() {
        Check("skyfall seam: the shooter asks after ArmSeconds of draw while it holds Skyfall and is not busy",
              SkyfallSeam.ShooterAsks(true, false, 1000, V) && SkyfallSeam.ShooterAsks(true, false, 8190, V));
        Check("skyfall seam: no grant, a running storm, no time or a short draw never asks",
              !SkyfallSeam.ShooterAsks(false, false, 1500, V) && !SkyfallSeam.ShooterAsks(true, true, 1500, V)
              && !SkyfallSeam.ShooterAsks(true, false, null, V) && !SkyfallSeam.ShooterAsks(true, false, 999, V));
    }

    static void Finalising() {
        ShotClaim c = Normal();
        bool cast = SkyfallSeam.Finalise(c, Pose(45, 1f), 1500, V);
        Check("skyfall seam: a full draw at 45 degrees becomes a request", cast && c.Sky == SkyCode.Skyfall && c.Kind == ShotKind.Early && c.Stage == 0
              && !c.Steered && c.Locks == 0 && c.Targets.Count == 0 && c.Picks.Count == 0 && c.TimeMs == 1500);
        Check("skyfall seam: the request's marks are the sigil centre over the wire pose",
              SkyfallSeam.TryCentre(Release0, c.Marks, out HVec at) && Close(at, Centre45, 0.001) && Close(at, new HVec(Release0.X, Release0.Y + 30, Release0.Z + 30), 0.001));
        ShotClaim low = Normal(); string before = low.ToString();
        Check("skyfall seam: below 25 degrees the request is dropped and the claim untouched", !SkyfallSeam.Finalise(low, Pose(24, 1f), 1500, V) && low.ToString() == before);
        ShotClaim weak = Normal();
        Check("skyfall seam: under MinStrength the request is dropped", !SkyfallSeam.Finalise(weak, Pose(45, 0.89f), 1500, V) && weak.Sky == SkyCode.None && weak.Steered);
        ShotClaim quick = Normal();
        Check("skyfall seam: a draw under ArmSeconds, or of unknown length, is dropped",
              !SkyfallSeam.Finalise(quick, Pose(45, 1f), 999, V) && !SkyfallSeam.Finalise(Normal(), Pose(45, 1f), null, V) && quick.Sky == SkyCode.None);
    }

    static void Deciding() {
        SkyVerdict cast = Server();
        Check("skyfall seam: the server casts a granted, armed, aimed request with a true centre", cast.Code == SkyCode.Skyfall && cast.Why == SkyWhy.Cast && cast.Marks.Count == 3);
        SkyVerdict fest = Server(festival: true);
        Check("skyfall seam: in festival mode the server relays Festival", fest.Code == SkyCode.Festival && fest.Why == SkyWhy.Cast);
        SkyVerdict none = Server(requested: false);
        Check("skyfall seam: no request is a plain shot with no marks", none.Code == SkyCode.None && none.Why == SkyWhy.NotRequested && none.Marks.Count == 0);
        SkyVerdict ungranted = Server(granted: false), busy = Server(busy: true);
        Check("skyfall seam: no grant or a running storm fizzles, naming why",
              ungranted.Code == SkyCode.Fizzled && ungranted.Why == SkyWhy.NotGranted && busy.Code == SkyCode.Fizzled && busy.Why == SkyWhy.Busy);
        SkyVerdict noPose = Server(pose: false), flat = Server(elevation: 20), weak = Server(strength: 0.85f);
        Check("skyfall seam: no wire pose, a flat aim or a weak pull fizzles, naming why",
              noPose.Code == SkyCode.Fizzled && noPose.Why == SkyWhy.NoPose && flat.Code == SkyCode.Fizzled && flat.Why == SkyWhy.NotAimed
              && weak.Code == SkyCode.Fizzled && weak.Why == SkyWhy.NotAimed);
        SkyVerdict shortHold = Server(claimed: 900, effective: 900), noTime = Server(claimed: null, effective: 1200), ahead = Server(claimed: 3000, effective: 900);
        Check("skyfall seam: a short hold, no claimed time, or an untrusted claim fizzles as not armed",
              shortHold.Why == SkyWhy.NotArmed && noTime.Why == SkyWhy.NotArmed && ahead.Why == SkyWhy.NotArmed
              && shortHold.Code == SkyCode.Fizzled && noTime.Code == SkyCode.Fizzled && ahead.Code == SkyCode.Fizzled);
        Check("skyfall seam: a hold past the claim's 8190 ms cap, or a server clock ahead of the claim, still casts",
              Server(claimed: 8190, effective: 8000).Why == SkyWhy.Cast && Server(claimed: 1200, effective: 1350).Why == SkyWhy.Cast);
        HVec right = Centre45;
        List<uint> near = SkyfallSeam.MarksOf(Release0, new HVec(right.X + 1.5, right.Y, right.Z));
        List<uint> far = SkyfallSeam.MarksOf(Release0, new HVec(right.X + 2.5, right.Y, right.Z));
        SkyVerdict kept = Server(marks: near);
        Check("skyfall seam: the shooter's centre is kept, and relayed, within 2 m of the server's", kept.Why == SkyWhy.Cast && kept.Marks.SequenceEqual(near));
        SkyVerdict tooFar = Server(marks: far), two = Server(marks: new List<uint> { 1, 2 });
        Check("skyfall seam: a centre over 2 m off, or no centre, fizzles",
              tooFar.Code == SkyCode.Fizzled && tooFar.Why == SkyWhy.CentreFar && two.Code == SkyCode.Fizzled && two.Why == SkyWhy.NoCentre);
        List<uint> own = SkyfallSeam.MarksOf(Release0, right);
        Check("skyfall seam: a fizzle relays the shooter's centre, else the server's own, else none",
              Server(granted: false, marks: near).Marks.SequenceEqual(near) && two.Marks.SequenceEqual(own)
              && Server(pose: false, marks: new List<uint> { 1, 2 }).Marks.Count == 0);
        bool agrees = true;
        foreach (bool granted in new[] { false, true })
        foreach (bool isBusy in new[] { false, true })
        foreach (double elevation in new[] { 20.0, 45.0 })
        foreach (float strength in new[] { 0.85f, 1f })
        foreach (int? claimed in new int?[] { null, 900, 1500, 8190 })
        foreach (int? effective in new int?[] { null, 900, 1500, 8000 }) {
            bool casts = Server(granted, isBusy, elevation, strength, claimed, effective).Why == SkyWhy.Cast;
            if (casts != SkyfallRules.ServerAccepts(granted, isBusy, Pose(elevation, strength), claimed, effective, V)) agrees = false;
        }
        Check("skyfall seam: with a true centre the server casts exactly when ServerAccepts says so", agrees);
    }

    static void Relaying() {
        List<uint> m = SkyfallSeam.MarksOf(Release0, Centre45);
        ShotClaim fresh = SkyfallSeam.Relay(null, new SkyVerdict { Code = SkyCode.Skyfall, Why = SkyWhy.Cast, Marks = m }, 1500);
        Check("skyfall seam: with no claim the relay is a fresh Early claim with the code, the marks and the server's time",
              fresh.Kind == ShotKind.Early && fresh.Stage == 0 && !fresh.Steered && fresh.Locks == 0 && fresh.Targets.Count == 0 && fresh.Picks.Count == 0
              && fresh.Sky == SkyCode.Skyfall && fresh.Marks.SequenceEqual(m) && fresh.TimeMs == 1500);
        var checkedClaim = new ShotClaim { Kind = ShotKind.Held, TimeMs = 1400, Stage = 30, Targets = { 7 }, Picks = { 1 } };
        ShotClaim fizz = SkyfallSeam.Relay(checkedClaim, new SkyVerdict { Code = SkyCode.Fizzled, Why = SkyWhy.NotGranted, Marks = m }, null);
        Check("skyfall seam: the relay is unsteered, Early, stage 0, no targets, and the checked claim is untouched",
              fizz.Kind == ShotKind.Early && fizz.Stage == 0 && fizz.Targets.Count == 0 && fizz.Picks.Count == 0 && fizz.Sky == SkyCode.Fizzled && fizz.TimeMs == null
              && checkedClaim.Kind == ShotKind.Held && checkedClaim.Targets.Count == 1 && checkedClaim.Sky == SkyCode.None);
        ShotClaim? wired = RoundTrip(fresh, out _);
        Check("skyfall seam: a relay survives the wire", wired != null && wired.ToString() == fresh.ToString());
    }

    static void Seeing() {
        HVec right = Centre45;
        List<uint> m = SkyfallSeam.MarksOf(Release0, right);
        Check("skyfall seam: no relay, or a relay with no code, is a plain shot",
              SkyfallSeam.Seen(null, Release0, out HVec c0) == SkyCode.None && double.IsNaN(c0.X) && SkyfallSeam.Seen(new ShotClaim(), Release0, out _) == SkyCode.None);
        bool both = true;
        foreach (SkyCode code in new[] { SkyCode.Skyfall, SkyCode.Festival }) {
            ShotClaim r = Sky(code); r.Marks.AddRange(m);
            both &= SkyfallSeam.Seen(r, Release0, out HVec c) == code && Close(c, right, 0.001);
        }
        Check("skyfall seam: a cast relay gives its code and the centre over this machine's release point", both);
        Check("skyfall seam: a cast relay without three marks is held as a fizzle with no centre",
              SkyfallSeam.Seen(Sky(SkyCode.Skyfall, 1, 2), Release0, out HVec c2) == SkyCode.Fizzled && double.IsNaN(c2.X));
        ShotClaim fz = Sky(SkyCode.Fizzled); fz.Marks.AddRange(m);
        ShotClaim sk = Sky(SkyCode.Skyfall); sk.Marks.AddRange(m);
        Check("skyfall seam: a fizzle keeps its centre for the puff; no release point is a fizzle",
              SkyfallSeam.Seen(fz, Release0, out HVec c3) == SkyCode.Fizzled && Close(c3, right, 0.001) && SkyfallSeam.Seen(sk, SkyfallSeam.Unknown, out _) == SkyCode.Fizzled);
    }

    static void Storms() {
        HVec right = Centre45;
        SkyStorm s = SkyfallSeam.Storm(4242, SkyCode.Skyfall, Release0, right, 77, 10f, V);
        SkyImpact[] plan = SkyfallPattern.Plan(4242, right.X, right.Z, Release0.X, Release0.Z, V);
        bool same = s.Impacts.Length == plan.Length && plan.Length > 0;
        for (int i = 0; same && i < plan.Length; i++) same = s.Impacts[i].X == plan[i].X && s.Impacts[i].Z == plan[i].Z && s.Impacts[i].LandAt == plan[i].LandAt;
        Check("skyfall seam: a cast's storm is the pattern over its centre and release point",
              same && s.ArrowId == 4242 && s.Code == SkyCode.Skyfall && s.CasterAccount == 77 && s.StartTime == 10f && s.CX == right.X && s.CY == right.Y && s.CZ == right.Z);
        Check("skyfall seam: a fizzle, or a storm with no centre, plans no impact",
              SkyfallSeam.Storm(4242, SkyCode.Fizzled, Release0, right, 77, 10f, V).Impacts.Length == 0
              && SkyfallSeam.Storm(4242, SkyCode.Skyfall, Release0, SkyfallSeam.Unknown, 77, 10f, V).Impacts.Length == 0);
        SkyStorm f = SkyfallSeam.Storm(4242, SkyCode.Festival, Release0, right, 77, 10f, V);
        Check("skyfall seam: festival plans the same storm",
              f.Festival && plan.Length > 0 && f.Impacts.Length == plan.Length && f.Impacts[0].X == plan[0].X && f.Impacts[0].Z == plan[0].Z);
        Check("skyfall seam: the caster's game stays busy a second longer than the server",
              Near(SkyfallSeam.BusyUntil(100f, V, true), 100f + SkyfallPattern.EndsAt(V) - 0.5f) && Near(SkyfallSeam.BusyUntil(100f, V, false), 100f + SkyfallPattern.EndsAt(V) + 0.5f));
        SkyStorm keep = SkyfallSeam.Storm(4242, SkyCode.Skyfall, Release0, right, 77, 10f, V);
        SkyImpact first = keep.Impacts[0], last = keep.Impacts[keep.Impacts.Length - 1]; int n = keep.Impacts.Length;
        SkyStorm reloaded = SkyfallSeam.Storm(4242, SkyCode.Skyfall, Release0, right, 77, 10f, new SkyfallValues { Waves = 5, RainRadius = 20f, NailGapSeconds = 3f });
        Check("skyfall seam: a running storm keeps the impacts it started with when the settings change",
              reloaded.Impacts.Length != n && !ReferenceEquals(keep.Impacts, reloaded.Impacts) && keep.Impacts.Length == n
              && keep.Impacts[0].X == first.X && keep.Impacts[0].Z == first.Z && keep.Impacts[n - 1].LandAt == last.LandAt);
    }

    static void EndToEnd() {
        // a Seeking Volley owner's claim (one lock), finalised with its wire pose, sent, decided, relayed, read
        ShotPose wire = Pose(45, 1f);
        ShotClaim claim = Normal();
        bool asked = SkyfallSeam.Finalise(claim, wire, 1500, V);
        ShotClaim? got = RoundTrip(claim, out _);
        SkyVerdict verdict = SkyfallSeam.Server(got != null && got.Sky != SkyCode.None, true, false, wire, got?.TimeMs, 1500, got?.Marks, false, V);
        ShotClaim relay = SkyfallSeam.Relay(got != null ? ClaimRules.ServerCheck(got) : null, verdict, 1500);
        ShotClaim? seen = RoundTrip(relay, out _);
        SkyCode code = SkyfallSeam.Seen(seen, wire.Pos, out HVec spectatorCentre);
        SkyfallSeam.TryCentre(wire.Pos, claim.Marks, out HVec casterCentre);
        SkyfallSeam.TryCentre(wire.Pos, verdict.Marks, out HVec serverCentre);
        SkyStorm caster = SkyfallSeam.Storm(4242, SkyCode.Skyfall, wire.Pos, casterCentre, 77, 0f, V);
        SkyStorm server = SkyfallSeam.Storm(4242, verdict.Code, wire.Pos, serverCentre, 77, 0f, V);
        SkyStorm spectator = SkyfallSeam.Storm(4242, code, wire.Pos, spectatorCentre, 77, 0f, V);
        Check("skyfall seam: caster, server and spectator plan the same storm",
              asked && verdict.Code == SkyCode.Skyfall && code == SkyCode.Skyfall && server.Impacts.Length > 0
              && SkyfallSeam.SamePattern(caster, server, 0) && SkyfallSeam.SamePattern(server, spectator, 0)
              && SkyfallSeam.Describe(server) == SkyfallSeam.Describe(spectator) && SkyfallSeam.Describe(server).StartsWith("Skyfall " + server.Impacts.Length + " impacts"));
        // the shooter's release point is raw; every reader's is to 1 mm (D LocationChunk.cs:252-255)
        var raw = new HVec(wire.Pos.X + 0.0004, wire.Pos.Y, wire.Pos.Z - 0.0003);
        SkyfallSeam.TryCentre(raw, claim.Marks, out HVec rawCentre);
        SkyStorm casterRaw = SkyfallSeam.Storm(4242, SkyCode.Skyfall, raw, rawCentre, 77, 0f, V);
        Check("skyfall seam: a release point 0.4 mm off moves the caster's storm by under a millimetre, and SamePattern tells 1 cm apart",
              SkyfallSeam.SamePattern(casterRaw, server, 0.001) && !SkyfallSeam.SamePattern(SkyfallSeam.Storm(4242, SkyCode.Skyfall,
                  new HVec(wire.Pos.X + 0.01, wire.Pos.Y, wire.Pos.Z), new HVec(serverCentre.X + 0.01, serverCentre.Y, serverCentre.Z), 77, 0f, V), server, 0.001));
    }

    // Review Focus 1: a holder's shot at the arm boundary, decided by the shooter and by the server with its clock 380 ms behind or ahead
    static void Boundary() {
        int t = ClaimTrust.DefaultTrustMs;
        Check("skyfall seam: at 999 ms the shooter never asks, so every machine sees a plain shot",
              !SkyfallSeam.Finalise(Normal(), Pose(45, 1f), 999, V)
              && Server(requested: false, claimed: 999, effective: ClaimTrust.EffectiveMs(619, 999, t)).Code == SkyCode.None
              && Server(requested: false, claimed: 999, effective: ClaimTrust.EffectiveMs(1379, 999, t)).Code == SkyCode.None);
        Check("skyfall seam: at 1000 ms the shooter asks and the server casts, its clock 380 ms behind or ahead",
              SkyfallSeam.Finalise(Normal(), Pose(45, 1f), 1000, V)
              && Server(claimed: 1000, effective: ClaimTrust.EffectiveMs(620, 1000, t)).Why == SkyWhy.Cast
              && Server(claimed: 1000, effective: ClaimTrust.EffectiveMs(1380, 1000, t)).Why == SkyWhy.Cast);
        Check("skyfall seam: at the 8190 ms cap the shooter asks and the server casts on its own clock, behind or ahead",
              SkyfallSeam.Finalise(Normal(), Pose(45, 1f), 8190, V)
              && Server(claimed: 8190, effective: ClaimTrust.EffectiveMs(7810, 8190, t)).Why == SkyWhy.Cast
              && Server(claimed: 8190, effective: ClaimTrust.EffectiveMs(8570, 8190, t)).Why == SkyWhy.Cast);
        Check("skyfall seam: a 999 ms request that reaches the server anyway fizzles, never casts",
              Server(claimed: 999, effective: ClaimTrust.EffectiveMs(1379, 999, t)).Code == SkyCode.Fizzled
              && Server(claimed: 999, effective: ClaimTrust.EffectiveMs(619, 999, t)).Code == SkyCode.Fizzled);
    }
```

  - `Unknown = new HVec(double.NaN, double.NaN, double.NaN)`.
  - `Neutral = new Resolution(ShotKind.Early, UltimateUse.None, new ReleasePlan(0, 0, 0), 0)`.
  - `MarksOf`: `[QuantizeOffset(c.X - r.X), QuantizeOffset(c.Y - r.Y), QuantizeOffset(c.Z - r.Z)]`.
  - `TryCentre` is its inverse with `OffsetOf`. It returns false with `Unknown` unless `marks.Count == CentreMarks` and `release.Finite`.
  - `ShooterAsks`: `holds && !busy && elapsedMs.HasValue && elapsedMs.Value / 1000.0 >= v.ArmSeconds`.
  - `Finalise`:
    - false (and the claim untouched) unless `elapsedMs.HasValue && SkyfallRules.Armed(wire, elapsedMs.Value / 1000.0, v)`;
    - otherwise `AsSky(claim, SkyCode.Skyfall, MarksOf(wire.Pos, SigilCentre(wire.Pos, wire.Dir, v)))` and true.
  - `AsSky`: sets `Kind = Early`, `Stage = 0`, `Steered = false`, `Locks = 0`, clears `Targets` and `Picks`, sets `Sky = code` and `Marks = new List<uint>(marks)`. It leaves `TimeMs` alone.
  - `Server`: the first failing check, in this order, names the reason. Every code comes from Task 2's `SkyfallRules.Relay`, the one relay-code rule:
    1. `!requested` -> `NotRequested`, code `Relay(SkyCode.None, false, festival)` (= `None`), no marks;
    2. `!granted` -> `NotGranted`;
    3. `busy` -> `Busy`;
    4. `pose == null` -> `NoPose`;
    5. `!Aimed(pose, v)` -> `NotAimed`;
    6. `!ServerAccepts(granted, busy, pose, claimedMs, effectiveMs, v)` -> `NotArmed`;
    7. `!TryCentre(pose.Pos, marks, out sc)` -> `NoCentre`;
    8. the 3-D distance from `sc` to `SigilCentre(pose.Pos, pose.Dir, v)` is over `CentreSlackMetres` -> `CentreFar`;
    9. otherwise `Cast`, with code `Relay(SkyCode.Skyfall, true, festival)` (= `festival ? Festival : Skyfall`) and a copy of `marks`.
    - Reasons 2-8 give `Relay(SkyCode.Skyfall, false, festival)` (= `Fizzled`). A fizzle's marks are a copy of `marks` when there are exactly 3; else `MarksOf(pose.Pos, SigilCentre(...))` when there is a pose; else none.
  - `Relay`: `(checkedClaim?.Copy() ?? new ShotClaim())`, then `AsSky(.., verdict.Code, verdict.Marks)` and `TimeMs = effectiveMs`.
  - `Seen`:
    - `None` (centre `Unknown`) for a null relay or `Sky == None`;
    - otherwise centre = `TryCentre(release, relay.Marks)`, and the result is `Fizzled` when the code is Fizzled or there is no centre, else the relayed code.
  - `Storm`:
    - `Impacts = SkyfallPattern.Plan(arrowId, centre.X, centre.Z, release.X, release.Z, v)` only for Skyfall or Festival with a finite centre and release; otherwise an empty array.
    - `CX/CY/CZ` = centre (NaN when unknown).
  - `BusyUntil`: `startTime + SkyfallPattern.EndsAt(v) + (server ? -BusySlackSeconds : BusySlackSeconds)`.
  - `SamePattern`: the same `Code` and `Impacts.Length`. Index for index: the same `Index`, `Wave` and `Nail`, `|dX|` and `|dZ|` at most `metres`, and `|dDomeAt|` and `|dLandAt|` at most 1e-6.
  - `Describe` (InvariantCulture): `"{Code} {n} impacts, centre {centre}, first ({x:0.00}, {z:0.00}) at {LandAt:0.00} s, last ({x:0.00}, {z:0.00}){(last.Nail ? " nail" : "")}"`. With no impacts, it is `"{Code} 0 impacts, centre {centre}"`.

  ```csharp
  [HarmonyPatch(typeof(LightBeamSkill), "DestroyShotArrow")]

      static readonly int T = Timing.Section("patch LightBeamSkill.DestroyShotArrow / HolyArrow.SkyfallKeepsArrow");
      static readonly MethodInfo? method = AccessTools.Method(typeof(LightBeamSkill), "DestroyShotArrow");
      static bool Prefix(LightBeamSkill __instance, BowString bowString, NetworkEntity shotEntity) {
          using var _t = Timing.Begin(T);
          bool cast = false;
          try {
              cast = shotEntity != null && bowString != null && Skyfall.IsCast(shotEntity.Identifier);
              if (!cast) return true;
              bowString.IsIgnoringCollisionOnShoot = true;
              if (method != null)
                  bowString.OnShootingEntity -= (Action<BowString, NetworkEntity, Vector3>)Delegate.CreateDelegate(typeof(Action<BowString, NetworkEntity, Vector3>), __instance, method);
              if (Logs.Debug) MelonLogger.Msg($"[Archery] Skyfall: arrow {shotEntity!.Identifier} kept from Holy Arrow's sacrifice (Skyfall holds it; the server destroys it after the relay)");
              return false;
          }
          catch (Exception e) { MelonLogger.Warning($"[Archery] Skyfall: Holy Arrow's sacrifice check failed: {e.Message}"); return !cast; }
      }
  }
  ```

### Task 9: The server's storm: landing queue, creature blasts, dome kills, festival care, `PlayerCare`

**Files:**
- Create: `QuickshotRing/SkyfallServer.cs`, `QuickshotRing/PlayerCare.cs`
- Modify: `QuickshotRing/SkyfallRules.cs` (append `SkyBody`, `SkyEffect`, `SkyfallStrike`)
- Modify: `QuickshotRing/Skyfall.cs` (add `GroundMask`, `GroundBelowMetres`, `GroundAt`; fill Task 8's marked `SkyfallServer.Start` line in `Begin`)
- Modify: `QuickshotRing/TestRangeHelpers.cs:66-99` (Heal and Feed call `PlayerCare`; every text unchanged)
- Modify: `QuickshotRing/Main.cs`: the section list after Task 7's `TSkyLink` line; `OnApplicationQuit` (lines 126-131); one tick line after Task 7's `SkyfallLink` tick
- Test: `QuickshotRing.Tests/SkyfallPatternTests.cs` (Task 3's class: one new method, called last from its `Run()`)

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes:
  - contract: `SkyStorm`, `SkyImpact`, `SkyfallValues`, `SkyfallCaught.Inside(HVec head, double cx, double groundY, double cz, double radius)`;
  - Task 6: `Skyfall.Values`;
  - `BounceQueue<T>` (Spectral.cs:555-585: `Cap` 512, `Add`, `TakeDue`, `Count`, `Clear`), `BlastMath.Fraction` (Rules.cs:61-70);
  - `Targets.Overlap` (Targets.cs:99-106), `Targets.IsCreature` (:111), `Targets.HeadOf` (:142-146), `Seek.H` (Seek.cs:129), `Hits.Loaded` (Hits.cs:111-117), `Hits.Dealing` (Hits.cs:29), `Builds.NameOf` (Builds.cs:30).
- Produces:
  - Unity-free (SkyfallRules.cs):
    - `struct SkyBody { public HVec At; public bool Player, Alive; public int Account; }`;
    - `struct SkyEffect { public int Body; public float Damage, Cripple; public bool Kill, Care; }`;
    - `static class SkyfallStrike`: `EdgeShare`, `Reach`, `Share`, `Decide`, `Lethal`, `QueueOrder`.
  - `PlayerCare` (server):
    - `bool Heal(Player p)` and `bool Feed(Player p)` (contract: true if changed);
    - `string? Heal(Player p, out float before, out float after, out float max)` and `string? Feed(...)`, the same forms with the reason (null = done) for the range's texts.
  - Hub, every machine: `Skyfall.GroundMask`, `Skyfall.GroundBelowMetres` (80, the one depth of every ground search), `Skyfall.GroundAt(double x, double z, double top, double bottom, out float y)`. The server's landing height comes from this; Tasks 10-12 use it with `GroundBelowMetres` so their ground matches.
  - Contract: `SkyfallServer.Start(SkyStorm s)`, `SkyfallServer.Tick()`, `SkyfallServer.Reset()`.

**Decisions this task makes:**
- **The queue:** its own `BounceQueue<Due>` instance (`Cap` 512 fits). A default storm queues 25 landings (3 x 8 + the World-Nail), so 20 storms can wait at once, and there is one storm per caster.
  - Start queues the World-Nail FIRST, then the rain in plan order (`SkyfallStrike.QueueOrder`, tested). A full queue then drops rain, never the finale. `TakeDue` still deals each one at its own time.
  - A drop warns once a session, as Ricochet.WaitingFull (Ricochet.cs:354-358).
- **Falloff:** a creature takes the full damage and cripple inside the landing's dome (`DomeRadius`, World-Nail `NailDomeRadius`). From there it falls in a straight line to `EdgeShare` = 0.5 at the blast radius (`BlastRadius`, `NailBlastRadius`): `BlastMath.Fraction`. The reach is the larger of dome and blast, so a creature inside a dome is never missed.
  - `SkyfallValues` has no edge-share field, so `EdgeShare` is a constant. If the owner wants it tuned, it moves into `SkyfallValues` as `BlastEdgeShare` (a contract change).
- **ImpactType.Explosive** is used for every Skyfall hit. These are exploding arrows, and some creatures take no Spiritual damage at all (Weight.SpiritualOf, Weight.cs:232-240).
- **The world height of a landing** is the server's own static-ground cast at its (X, Z), from the sigil plane (`CY` + 0.5) down to 80 m below the release. It is the highest non-loose surface, as TestRange's WorldProbe.Ground (TestRangeBuild.cs:170, 182-195), in loaded world only.
  - No ground, or ground not loaded: the landing deals nothing and is counted as skipped.
  - The 80 m is `Skyfall.GroundBelowMetres`, an `internal const` in the hub so the clients' ground looks (Tasks 10-12) use the same depth (ponytail: a setting if a storm is ever cast over a deeper drop).
- **Players:**
  - In normal mode a player is killed when `SkyfallCaught.Inside(head, X, groundY, Z, dome radius)` and they are not the caster. When `CasterAccount` is 0 (unknown), nobody is killed: never risk the caster.
  - They never take blast damage or cripple.
  - In festival mode, a player within `FestivalCareRadius` (the same `Inside` rule) is healed and fed, the caster too. Nobody is killed.
- **The kill** is a plain `ImpactHit` of `SkyfallStrike.Lethal(maxHealth)` health damage, no cripple, no wielder, `ImpactType.Explosive` (exact constructor call in Step 10) through `ph.DetectImpact`. That is the Holy recipe (HolyArrow.cs:500-502), so the game's normal Downed death follows. There is no wielder, so no PvP rule applies. It is never `float.MaxValue`: `DamageX` > 1 overflows that to Infinity, and Infinity x a 0 multiplier is NaN, which `Stat.Base`'s clamp keeps.
  - **Known blockers, by design, not worked around:**
    - spawn and cave invincibility (the design wants it);
    - the immortals switch;
    - `IsIgnoringDamage`;
    - locked controls (`BlockDamageIfControlsAreLocked`);
    - `DamageX` or `PlayerDamageTakenMultiplier` at 0;
    - the player's Explosive multiplier at 0;
    - damage protection.

    The kill line then says "but not killed" with the health before and after.
- **Creatures** get one batched hit per creature per tick: the summed damage and cripple of every landing due that tick. The wielder is the caster's `PlayerController` from the storm's start, as the Holy blast names its shooter. There is no explosion force, and no XP (a plain hit).
- **The held real arrow is not this task's** (ruling 2): `Skyfall.Held` (Task 8) destroys it 1 s after the release, fizzles included. Nothing here queues or destroys an arrow.
- **`StartTime`** is `Time.time` on this machine at the release (Task 8). A landing is due at `StartTime + LandAt`.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the strike tests.** Add to `QuickshotRing.Tests/SkyfallPatternTests.cs` (`using System.Collections.Generic;`, `using System.Linq;`), and call `Strike();` as the last line of its `Run()`:

```csharp
// Task 9: who a landing strikes, from plain positions (server). Players die only inside a dome, never the caster; festival kills nobody.
static void Strike() {
    SkyfallValues v = SkyfallValues.Default;
    var landing = new SkyImpact { Index = 0, Wave = 0, X = 10, Z = 20, Nail = false };
    SkyBody C(double x, bool alive = true) => new SkyBody { At = new HVec(x, 5, 20), Player = false, Alive = alive };
    SkyBody P(double x, int account, bool alive = true) => new SkyBody { At = new HVec(x, 6.5, 20), Player = true, Alive = alive, Account = account };
    // 0..5 creatures at 0, 2, 3.25, 4, 4.1 m and a dead one; 6 player 7 at the landing, 7 the caster (9) there, 8 player 8 at 3 m, 9 a dead player
    var bodies = new List<SkyBody> { C(10), C(12), C(13.25), C(14), C(14.1), C(10, false), P(10, 7), P(10, 9), P(13, 8), P(10, 6, false) };
    var into = new List<SkyEffect>();

    SkyfallStrike.Decide(landing, 5, false, 9, bodies, v, into);
    Check("skyfall strike: in normal mode four creatures and one player are struck", into.Select(e => e.Body).SequenceEqual(new[] { 0, 1, 2, 3, 6 }));
    Check("skyfall strike: full damage and cripple at the landing and inside the dome",
        Near(into[0].Damage, 80f) && Near(into[0].Cripple, 80f) && Near(into[1].Damage, 80f) && Near(into[1].Cripple, 80f));
    Check("skyfall strike: three quarters half-way from the dome's edge to the blast's", Near(into[2].Damage, 60f) && Near(into[2].Cripple, 60f));
    Check("skyfall strike: half at the blast radius", Near(into[3].Damage, 40f) && Near(into[3].Cripple, 40f));
    Check("skyfall strike: a player caught in a dome is killed and takes no blast",
        into[4].Kill && !into[4].Care && into[4].Damage == 0f && into[4].Cripple == 0f);
    Check("skyfall strike: creatures are never killed outright or cared for", into.Take(4).All(e => !e.Kill && !e.Care));

    SkyfallStrike.Decide(landing, 5, true, 9, bodies, v, into);
    Check("skyfall strike: festival strikes the same creatures and cares for the three living players near",
        into.Select(e => e.Body).SequenceEqual(new[] { 0, 1, 2, 3, 6, 7, 8 }));
    Check("skyfall strike: festival deals no damage, only cripple",
        into.Take(4).All(e => e.Damage == 0f) && Near(into[0].Cripple, 80f) && Near(into[3].Cripple, 40f));
    Check("skyfall strike: festival never kills, and cares for the caster too", into.Skip(4).All(e => e.Care && !e.Kill));

    SkyfallStrike.Decide(landing, 5, false, 0, bodies, v, into);
    Check("skyfall strike: with no known caster nobody is killed", into.Select(e => e.Body).SequenceEqual(new[] { 0, 1, 2, 3 }));
    SkyfallStrike.Decide(landing, double.NaN, false, 9, bodies, v, into);
    Check("skyfall strike: with no ground found under a landing nobody is struck or killed", into.Count == 0);
    SkyfallStrike.Decide(landing, double.NaN, true, 9, bodies, v, into);
    Check("skyfall strike: with no ground found festival cares for nobody", into.Count == 0);

    var nail = new SkyImpact { Index = 24, Wave = 3, X = 10, Z = 20, Nail = true };
    var near = new List<SkyBody> { C(20), C(21), C(22.5), P(19, 7), P(21, 8) };
    SkyfallStrike.Decide(nail, 5, false, 9, near, v, into);
    Check("skyfall strike: the World-Nail strikes inside its own dome and blast", into.Select(e => e.Body).SequenceEqual(new[] { 0, 1, 3 }));
    Check("skyfall strike: the World-Nail's damage, full in its dome, three quarters at 11 m", Near(into[0].Damage, 400f) && Near(into[1].Damage, 300f));
    Check("skyfall strike: the World-Nail's dome kills", into[2].Kill);

    Check("skyfall strike: a landing reaches 4 m, the World-Nail 12 m", SkyfallStrike.Reach(false, v) == 4.0 && SkyfallStrike.Reach(true, v) == 12.0);
    SkyfallValues wide = SkyfallValues.Default; wide.DomeRadius = 6f;
    Check("skyfall strike: a dome wider than the blast reaches as far as the dome, at full damage",
        SkyfallStrike.Reach(false, wide) == 6.0 && Near(SkyfallStrike.Share(5.0, false, wide), 1f));

    Check("skyfall kill: a thousand times the player's health", SkyfallStrike.Lethal(500f) == 5e5f);
    Check("skyfall kill: never below 100000", SkyfallStrike.Lethal(100f) == 1e5f && SkyfallStrike.Lethal(0f) == 1e5f &&
        SkyfallStrike.Lethal(-3f) == 1e5f && SkyfallStrike.Lethal(float.NaN) == 1e5f);
    Check("skyfall kill: never above 1e9, so the game's multipliers cannot make it infinite",
        SkyfallStrike.Lethal(1e8f) == 1e9f && SkyfallStrike.Lethal(float.PositiveInfinity) == 1e9f && !float.IsInfinity(SkyfallStrike.Lethal(1e30f) * 1e6f));

    SkyImpact[] plan = SkyfallPattern.Plan(4242u, 0, 30, 0, 0, v);
    int[] order = SkyfallStrike.QueueOrder(plan);
    Check("skyfall queue: the World-Nail is queued first, then the rain in plan order",
        order.Length == plan.Length && order[0] == plan.Length - 1 && plan[order[0]].Nail && order.Skip(1).SequenceEqual(Enumerable.Range(0, plan.Length - 1)));
    var q = new BounceQueue<int>();
    for (int k = 0; k < BounceQueue<int>.Cap - 3; k++) q.Add(-1, 100f);
    var kept = new List<int>();
    foreach (int k in order) if (q.Add(k, plan[k].LandAt)) kept.Add(k);
    Check("skyfall queue: a nearly full queue keeps the World-Nail and drops rain, never the finale",
        kept.Count == 3 && plan[kept[0]].Nail && !plan[kept[1]].Nail && !plan[kept[2]].Nail && q.Count == BounceQueue<int>.Cap);
}
```
The positions work for a hemisphere and for a cylinder reading of `SkyfallCaught.Inside`:
- a head 1.5 m above the landing is inside a 2.5 m dome;
- a head 3 m out is outside it, but inside the 4 m care radius;
- a head 9 m out is inside the World-Nail's 10 m dome, and 11 m out is outside.

- [ ] **Step 3: Add `SkyBody`, `SkyEffect`, `SkyfallStrike`** at the end of `SkyfallRules.cs`:
  - `SkyBody`: a creature (`At` = its point nearest the landing, from its PositionHandler) or a player (`At` = their head; `Account` = their account id).
  - `SkyEffect`: what one landing does to one body (`Body` = its index in the list).
  - `SkyfallStrike`:
    - `public const float EdgeShare = 0.5f;`
    - `public static double Reach(bool nail, SkyfallValues v)`: `Math.Max(dome, blast)` for that landing kind.
    - `public static float Share(double distance, bool nail, SkyfallValues v) => BlastMath.Fraction((float)distance, dome, (float)Reach(nail, v), EdgeShare);`
    - `public static float Lethal(float maxHealth)`: `!(maxHealth > 0f)` gives `1e5f`; else `maxHealth * 1000f` held to [1e5f, 1e9f].
    - `public static void Decide(SkyImpact impact, double groundY, bool festival, int casterAccount, IList<SkyBody> bodies, SkyfallValues v, List<SkyEffect> into)`: clears `into`, then adds at most one effect per body, in body order:
      - a dead body: nothing;
      - a creature: `s = Share(|At - (X, groundY, Z)|, Nail, v)`; when `s > 0`, `Damage = festival ? 0 : (Nail ? NailDamage : ImpactDamage) * s` and `Cripple = ImpactCripple * s`;
      - a player in festival mode: `Care` when `SkyfallCaught.Inside(At, X, groundY, Z, FestivalCareRadius)`;
      - a player in normal mode: `Kill` when `casterAccount != 0 && Account != casterAccount && SkyfallCaught.Inside(At, X, groundY, Z, Nail ? NailDomeRadius : DomeRadius)`;
      - a player never gets Damage or Cripple;
      - a non-finite `groundY` (no ground found) adds nothing. This already holds through `BlastMath.Fraction(NaN) == 0` and `SkyfallCaught.Inside`'s NaN rule; the test pins it.
    - `public static int[] QueueOrder(SkyImpact[] impacts)`: the indices of the Nail impacts first, then every other index in plan order (a loop, no LINQ).

- [ ] **Step 5: Create `QuickshotRing/PlayerCare.cs`.** Usings: `Alta.Networking.Scripts.Player`, `Alta.StatSystem`. The cores are moved exactly from TestRangeHelpers.cs:70-75 and 87-91; the reason texts are the range's own.

```csharp
namespace QuickshotRing {
    // Heal and Feed, shared by the play-test range (TestRangeHelpers.cs) and Skyfall's festival mode (SkyfallServer.cs). Server only:
    // the game syncs both stats from the server. Neither catches: the caller does, per player.
    internal static class PlayerCare {
        /// <summary>Health back to its maximum (food is not touched). Null when done, else why not.</summary>
        internal static string? Heal(Player p, out float before, out float after, out float max) {
            before = after = max = 0f;
            HealthObject? h = p.PlayerController != null ? p.PlayerController.Health : null;
            if (h == null) return "has no health to fill";
            before = h.Health; max = h.MaxHealth;
            if (!(before > 0f)) return "is dead: respawn first";
            h.Health = max;
            after = h.Health;
            return null;
        }

        /// <summary>True when the player's health went up.</summary>
        internal static bool Heal(Player p) => Heal(p, out float before, out float after, out _) == null && after > before;

        /// <summary>The 'Hunger' stat to its maximum (the stat SmoothLocomotion reads by that name; its Base setter syncs it like the
        /// game's own drain). Dead players and spirits are left alone. Null when done, else why not.</summary>
        internal static string? Feed(Player p, out float before, out float after, out float max) {
            before = after = max = 0f;
            HealthObject? h = p.PlayerController != null ? p.PlayerController.Health : null;
            if (h == null || !(h.Health > 0f)) return "no body or dead";
            if (h.StatManager == null || !h.StatManager.GetStat("Hunger", out Stat hunger) || hunger == null) return "no hunger";
            before = hunger.Value;
            hunger.Base = hunger.Maximum;
            after = hunger.Value; max = hunger.Maximum;
            return null;
        }

        /// <summary>True when the player's hunger went up.</summary>
        internal static bool Feed(Player p) => Feed(p, out float before, out float after, out _) == null && after > before;
    }
}
```

- [ ] **Step 8: Create `QuickshotRing/SkyfallServer.cs`: its state and `Start`.**
  - `internal static class SkyfallServer`; the header comment says what it decides and that it plays no sound or effect (the headless server).
  - `sealed class Running { public SkyStorm S = null!; public string Caster = ""; public PlayerController? Wielder; public int Total, Left, Dealt, Skipped, Dropped, Struck, Killed, Spared, Healed, Fed; }`
  - `struct Due { public Running R; public int Index; }`
  - The queue is `static readonly BounceQueue<Due> landings`, with a reused list for `TakeDue`.
  - `static Collider[] buf = new Collider[2048]; static bool overlapWarned, queueWarned, landWarned; const int ColliderCap = 8192;` (the ground depth is `Skyfall.GroundBelowMetres`).
  - `internal static void Start(SkyStorm s)` has the section `SkyfallServer.Start` and runs on the server only (`if (!NetworkSceneManager.IsServer) return;`):
    1. no impacts: return (Begin calls Start only for Skyfall and Festival; a fizzle's arrow is `Skyfall.Held`'s);
    2. make a `Running`:
       - `Caster` is the online name for `CasterAccount` (`Player.SafeGetPlayer`), else the grant's saved name, else the account number;
       - `Wielder` is that player's `PlayerController`, or null;
       - `Total` is `Impacts.Length`.
    3. Queue the impacts in `SkyfallStrike.QueueOrder(s.Impacts)` order (the World-Nail first, then the rain in plan order), each at `s.StartTime + impact.LandAt`. `Left` counts what was queued, `Dropped` what was not.
    4. On the first drop of a session, warn: `[Archery] Skyfall: {BounceQueue<Due>.Cap} landings are waiting; arrow {id}'s storm lost {n} rain landing(s) (said once a session)`.
    5. The Debug line: `[Archery] Skyfall: storm of arrow {id} by '{Caster}' started on the server{(s.Festival ? " (festival)" : "")}: {Left} landing(s) over {SkyfallPattern.EndsAt(Skyfall.Values):0.0} s round ({s.CX:0.0}, {s.CZ:0.0})`.

- [ ] **Step 9: `Tick`, the landing and the batched hit.**
  - `internal static void Tick()`:
    - `if (landings.Count == 0) return;` (one test while idle; clients never queue);
    - `now = Time.time`;
    - due landings, in this order, so one throw never loses the rest:
      1. `due.Clear()`, then `landings.TakeDue(now, due)`;
      2. `foreach (Due d in due) { try { Landing(d, v); } catch (Exception e) { if (!landWarned) { landWarned = true; MelonLogger.Warning($"[Archery] Skyfall: a landing failed (said once): {e.Message}"); } } }` with `v = Skyfall.Values`;
      3. `try { Deal(); } finally { batch.Clear(); }`;
      4. then, whatever happened above, each landing's `R.Left--`, and every `Running` that reached 0 gets `Ended(R)`.
  - `Landing(Due d, SkyfallValues v)` has the section `hit SkyfallServer.Impact (one landing)` with context `S.ArrowId`:
    1. `SkyImpact i = d.R.S.Impacts[d.Index]`;
    2. when `Skyfall.GroundAt(i.X, i.Z, s.CY + 0.5, s.Release.Y - Skyfall.GroundBelowMetres, out float gy)` is false: `Skipped++`, plus the Debug line `[Archery] Skyfall: arrow {id} landing {i.Index} skipped: no loaded ground under it`, and return;
    3. `Dealt++; point = (X, gy, Z)`;
    4. creatures:
       - `Targets.Overlap(point, (float)SkyfallStrike.Reach(i.Nail, v), ref buf, ColliderCap, ref overlapWarned, "Skyfall")`;
       - each collider gives `GetComponentInParent<ImpactDetector>()?.HealthTarget`, kept when `Targets.IsCreature(h)` and not seen this landing;
       - `At = Seek.H(h.PositionHandler != null ? h.PositionHandler.GetPosition(point) : h.transform.position)`, `Alive = h.Health > 0f`;
    5. players, as Ricochet.cs:227-233:
       - every `Player` in `Player.AllPlayers` with a `PlayerController` whose `Health is PlayerHealth`, and with a head (`Targets.HeadOf`);
       - `Alive = ph.Health > 0f && pc.IsAlive`, `Account = p.UserInfo?.Identifier ?? 0`;
    6. `SkyfallStrike.Decide(i, gy, s.Festival, s.CasterAccount, bodies, v, effects)`, then:
       - each creature effect is added to `batch[h]`: Damage and Cripple summed, the first `Point` and `Running` kept;
       - each player effect acts at most once per player per tick (a `HashSet<Player>` cleared per tick): `Kill` gives `Kill(p, d.R, i, point)`, `Care` gives `Care(p, d.R)`;
    7. the Debug line `[Archery] Skyfall: arrow {id} landing {i.Index} ({(i.Nail ? "the World-Nail" : "wave " + (i.Wave + 1))}) at {point}: {c} creature(s), {k} kill(s), {f} cared for`.
  - `Deal()` has the section `hit SkyfallServer.Deal (one hit per creature)`. For each `(h, b)` in `batch`:
    - skip when `h == null || !(h.Health > 0f) || (b.Damage <= 0f && b.Cripple <= 0f)`;
    - `var hit = new ImpactHit(b.Damage, b.Cripple, 1f, 1f, null, ImpactType.Explosive, b.Point, Vector3.zero, Vector3.zero, null, null, b.R.Wielder != null ? b.R.Wielder : null);`;
    - `h.DetectImpact(hit)` under `Hits.Dealing = true` / `finally Hits.Dealing = false` (as Hits.Apply at Hits.cs:227-240), each in its own try/catch that warns `[Archery] Skyfall: a hit on '{h.name}' failed: {e.Message}`;
    - `b.R.Struck++`;
    - the Debug line `[Archery] Skyfall: arrow {id} '{h.name}' damage {b.Damage:0.#}, cripple {b.Cripple:0.#}; health {before:0.#} -> {h.Health:0.#}`.
    
    `Tick` clears the batch afterwards, in a `finally`. In festival mode Decide has already made the damage 0, so the hit is cripple only.

- [ ] **Step 10: `Kill`, `Care` and the storm's line.**
  - `Kill(Player p, Running r, SkyImpact i, Vector3 point)` has the section `hit SkyfallServer.Kill (a player caught in a dome)`:
    - `ph = (PlayerHealth)p.PlayerController.Health`, `before = ph.Health`;
    - `var hit = new ImpactHit(SkyfallStrike.Lethal(ph.MaxHealth), 0f, 1f, 1f, null, ImpactType.Explosive, point, Vector3.zero, Vector3.zero, null, null, null);` (the 8th and 9th arguments are the normal and the direction, D Alta.Impact/ImpactHit.cs:113);
    - `try { Hits.Dealing = true; ph.DetectImpact(hit); } catch (Exception e) { MelonLogger.Warning($"[Archery] Skyfall: the kill on '{Builds.NameOf(p)}' failed: {e.Message}"); } finally { Hits.Dealing = false; }`: the game's death path can throw on the headless server, and one throw must not end the landing;

  - One Summary line per caught player, exact:

    - not killed: `[Archery] Skyfall: '{Builds.NameOf(p)}' was caught in a dome of '{r.Caster}''s storm (arrow {r.S.ArrowId}, {where}) but not killed: health {before:0.#} -> {after:0.#} (spawn invincibility, the immortals switch, locked controls or the server's damage settings)`

**Why the curves are pulled out:** the unfold order (core first), the hold's climb and the sky dial's slew are the timing decisions. Unit tests can pin them; nothing else in this task can be unit-tested.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the curves test.** In `QuickshotRing.Tests/SkyfallPatternTests.cs`, add this method. Call `SkyfallLookCurves();` as the last line of its `Run()`, after Task 6's `LooksOnlyKeepThePattern();`.

```csharp
// Task 10: the looks' timing curves (Unity-free, SkyfallCurves): ease, fade envelope, the sigil's core-first unfold, the hold's
// three rings, and the sky dial's slew. Looks only: nothing here decides damage.
static void SkyfallLookCurves() {
    Check("skyfall curves: ease is 0 at the start", SkyfallCurves.Ease(0f) == 0f);
    Check("skyfall curves: ease is 1 at the end", SkyfallCurves.Ease(1f) == 1f);
    Check("skyfall curves: ease is a half half-way", Near(SkyfallCurves.Ease(0.5f), 0.5f));
    Check("skyfall curves: ease is a smoothstep (0.25 gives 0.15625)", Near(SkyfallCurves.Ease(0.25f), 0.15625f));
    Check("skyfall curves: ease holds still outside 0 to 1", SkyfallCurves.Ease(-1f) == 0f && SkyfallCurves.Ease(2f) == 1f);
    Check("skyfall curves: ease of a broken time is 0", SkyfallCurves.Ease(float.NaN) == 0f);

    Check("skyfall curves: an envelope starts at 0", SkyfallCurves.Envelope(0f, 2f, 0.5f, 0.5f) == 0f);
    Check("skyfall curves: an envelope is half in at half its fade-in", Near(SkyfallCurves.Envelope(0.25f, 2f, 0.5f, 0.5f), 0.5f));
    Check("skyfall curves: an envelope is full between its fades", SkyfallCurves.Envelope(1f, 2f, 0.5f, 0.5f) == 1f);
    Check("skyfall curves: an envelope is half out at half its fade-out", Near(SkyfallCurves.Envelope(1.75f, 2f, 0.5f, 0.5f), 0.5f));
    Check("skyfall curves: an envelope is 0 at its end and before its start",
        SkyfallCurves.Envelope(2f, 2f, 0.5f, 0.5f) == 0f && SkyfallCurves.Envelope(-0.1f, 2f, 0.5f, 0.5f) == 0f);
    Check("skyfall curves: no fade-in shows at once", SkyfallCurves.Envelope(0f, 2f, 0f, 0.5f) == 1f);
    Check("skyfall curves: no life or a broken time shows nothing",
        SkyfallCurves.Envelope(0.5f, 0f, 0.5f, 0.5f) == 0f && SkyfallCurves.Envelope(float.NaN, 2f, 0.5f, 0.5f) == 0f);

    Check("skyfall curves: the core is shut at the release", SkyfallCurves.Unfold(0f, 0, 6, 1.75f) == 0f);
    Check("skyfall curves: the core is open after its share of the build (0.7 of 1.75 s)", Near(SkyfallCurves.Unfold(0.7f, 0, 6, 1.75f), 1f));
    Check("skyfall curves: the outer band is still shut at 1.05 s", Near(SkyfallCurves.Unfold(1.05f, 5, 6, 1.75f), 0f));
    Check("skyfall curves: band 2 of 6 at 0.875 s is 0.71825 open", Near(SkyfallCurves.Unfold(0.875f, 2, 6, 1.75f), 0.71825f));
    bool coreFirst = true, allOpen = true;
    for (int s = 0; s <= 35; s++)
        for (int b = 0; b < 5; b++)
            if (SkyfallCurves.Unfold(s * 0.05f, b, 6, 1.75f) < SkyfallCurves.Unfold(s * 0.05f, b + 1, 6, 1.75f)) coreFirst = false;
    for (int b = 0; b < 6; b++) if (!(SkyfallCurves.Unfold(1.75f, b, 6, 1.75f) >= 0.9999f)) allOpen = false;
    Check("skyfall curves: at every moment a band is never more open than the one inside it (core first)", coreFirst);
    Check("skyfall curves: every band is open when the build time is up", allOpen);
    Check("skyfall curves: a lone band takes the whole time, and no time opens it at once",
        Near(SkyfallCurves.Unfold(0.3f, 0, 1, 1f), SkyfallCurves.Ease(0.3f)) && SkyfallCurves.Unfold(0f, 3, 6, 0f) == 1f);

    Check("skyfall curves: the hold's rings start at the tip", SkyfallCurves.HoldRingDistance(0, 0f, 3.6f) == 0f);
    Check("skyfall curves: the farthest ring climbs the whole span", Near(SkyfallCurves.HoldRingDistance(2, 1f, 3.6f), 3.6f));
    Check("skyfall curves: the nearest ring climbs 0.3 of the span", Near(SkyfallCurves.HoldRingDistance(0, 1f, 3.6f), 1.08f));
    Check("skyfall curves: the middle ring half-way through the hold is at 1.116 m", Near(SkyfallCurves.HoldRingDistance(1, 0.5f, 3.6f), 1.116f));
    Check("skyfall curves: the hold's progress and ring index are held to their ranges",
        SkyfallCurves.HoldRingDistance(1, 2f, 3.6f) == SkyfallCurves.HoldRingDistance(1, 1f, 3.6f) &&
        SkyfallCurves.HoldRingDistance(5, 1f, 3.6f) == SkyfallCurves.HoldRingDistance(2, 1f, 3.6f));
    Check("skyfall curves: the farthest ring is full size when armed", Near(SkyfallCurves.HoldRingScale(2, 1f), 1f));
    Check("skyfall curves: the nearest ring starts at 0.33 of full size", Near(SkyfallCurves.HoldRingScale(0, 0f), 0.33f));
    Check("skyfall curves: the middle ring ends at 0.8 of full size", Near(SkyfallCurves.HoldRingScale(1, 1f), 0.8f));
    Check("skyfall curves: the farthest ring half-way through is 0.8 of full size", Near(SkyfallCurves.HoldRingScale(2, 0.5f), 0.8f));

    Check("skyfall curves: the dial moves up at most dt / fade seconds", Near(SkyfallCurves.Toward(0f, 1f, 0.5f, 2f), 0.25f));
    Check("skyfall curves: the dial moves down at most dt / fade seconds", Near(SkyfallCurves.Toward(1f, 0f, 0.5f, 2f), 0.75f));
    Check("skyfall curves: the dial never passes what it wants", SkyfallCurves.Toward(0.9f, 1f, 0.5f, 2f) == 1f);
    Check("skyfall curves: the dial stays where it is wanted", SkyfallCurves.Toward(0.3f, 0.3f, 0.1f, 2f) == 0.3f);
    Check("skyfall curves: no fade time jumps at once", SkyfallCurves.Toward(0f, 1f, 0.1f, 0f) == 1f);
    Check("skyfall curves: a broken dial starts from 0, and a backward step does nothing",
        Near(SkyfallCurves.Toward(float.NaN, 1f, 0.5f, 2f), 0.25f) && SkyfallCurves.Toward(0.5f, 1f, -1f, 2f) == 0.5f);
}
```

- [ ] **Step 2: Run the tests.** `dotnet run --project QuickshotRing.Tests -c Release`. Expected: a build error, `error CS0103: The name 'SkyfallCurves' does not exist in the current context`.

- [ ] **Step 3: Add `SkyfallCurves` (the Task 10 members).** In `QuickshotRing/SkyfallRules.cs`, add a new class as the last type in the namespace block (after Task 3's `SkyDial` and Task 4's types). Give it a doc comment: "Looks only: the timing curves the Skyfall looks draw with (tested). Nothing here decides damage." Tasks 11 and 12 append to it. Every member is `public static float`, with no Unity types and only `System.Math`. Clamps are written by hand, as at Homing.cs:36.
  - `public const float BandShare = 0.4f;`: the share of the open time each band takes.
  - `Ease(t)`: 0 for NaN or `t <= 0`, 1 for `t >= 1`, else `t*t*(3-2t)`.
  - `Envelope(t, life, fadeIn, fadeOut)`: 0 when `!(life > 0)`, `!(t >= 0)` or `t >= life`. Otherwise `min(a, b)`, where `a = fadeIn > 0 ? min(1, t / fadeIn) : 1` and `b = fadeOut > 0 ? min(1, (life - t) / fadeOut) : 1`. The ramps are linear.
  - `Unfold(t, band, bands, seconds)`:
    - When `!(seconds > 0)`: 1 if `t >= 0`, else 0.
    - `bands < 1` counts as 1; `band` is held to `[0, bands - 1]`.
    - For one band: `Ease(t / seconds)`.
    - Otherwise: `start = band * seconds * (1 - BandShare) / (bands - 1)`, and the result is `Ease((t - start) / (seconds * BandShare))`. The core (band 0) opens first; the last band ends exactly at `seconds`.
  - `HoldRingDistance(ring, progress, span)`: `span * share[ring] * Ease(p)`, with `share = { 0.30, 0.62, 1.0 }`, `ring` held to 0..2 and `p` held to 0..1 (NaN counts as 0).
  - `HoldRingScale(ring, progress)`: `size[ring] * (0.6 + 0.4 * Ease(p))`, with `size = { 0.55, 0.8, 1.0 }` and the same holds.
  - `Toward(shown, want, dt, seconds)`:
    - NaN `shown` or `want` count as 0; a NaN or negative `dt` moves nothing.
    - The step is `seconds > 0 ? dt / seconds : 1`.
    - The result is `shown` moved toward `want` by at most the step, never past it, then held to `[0, 1]`.

- [ ] **Step 5: Make the embedded glyphs.** Create `QuickshotRing/Art/admin_skill/make_skyfall_glyphs.py`, a PIL script. Its header comment says the pixels are the game's own glyphs, from the private export, embedded as the orb icons are.
  - It reads from `C:\ATTUnity\ExportedProject\Assets\Texture2D\` and `QuickshotRing\Art\game_icons\`, and writes to `QuickshotRing\Resources\`. If the export folder is missing, it exits 1 with one line naming it.
  - Each output is RGBA with RGB set to white (255) and the shape in alpha. Most game glyphs are black lines; only the ruins symbols and the Lightbeam glyph are white.

  | Output | Source | Size |
  |---|---|---|
  | `Skyfall_Rune1.png` .. `Skyfall_Rune5.png` | `Glyph_Ruins_Symbol_01.png` .. `_05.png`, alpha kept | 256 x 256 (LANCZOS) |
  | `Skyfall_LightBeam.png` | `Art\game_icons\Glyph_Bow_LightBeam.png`, alpha kept | 512 x 512 |
  | `Skyfall_Slash.png` | `Glyph_Slash.png`, alpha kept | 256 x 256 |
  | `Skyfall_Meander.png` | `ruins_02_emmissive.png`, rows 1024-1199 from the top (the meander groove; its alpha is 255 everywhere), alpha = the red channel | 1024 x 88 |

  Run `python -I QuickshotRing/Art/admin_skill/make_skyfall_glyphs.py`. Expected: eight `wrote QuickshotRing\Resources\Skyfall_*.png (W x H)` lines, then `ls QuickshotRing/Resources/Skyfall_*.png | wc -l` prints `8`.

  | ColourGoldDeep | `#FFCE00` | the gold of glyphs: the Lightbeam glyph in the sigil's core, the medallions' runes. |
