  - The colour properties are `Cfg.Colour(entry, fallback)`, with the default as the fallback (Cfg.cs:211). `SigilSpin` is parsed once per settings load (invariant culture, comma-separated; a missing band turns at 0) and cached until `Cfg.Reloaded`.
  - **Textures** (one lookup per session; again after a settings reload only if something was missing):
    - **One pass** over `Resources.FindObjectsOfTypeAll<Texture2D>()`, in the section `asset SkyfallSigil.FindTextures`, for the exact names `SpriteSheet_Combat`, `Glyph_Ruins_Symbol_01` .. `_05` and `Glyph_Bow_LightBeam`. The pattern is Tablet.cs:928-938, but this is the class's own copy (Tablet's is private) and it matches exact names only.
    - Any rune or Lightbeam texture the pass did not find comes from its embedded PNG.
    - `Meander` and `Slash` always come from their embedded PNG.
    - `Lightbeam` by name can be gone after `Tree.ApplyIcons` swaps the slot icon (Tree.cs:812-824), so the fallback is expected to be used.
    - **Embedded:** the stream code of Tree.cs:813-814 with `QuickshotRing.Resources.Skyfall_<name>.png`, loaded into `new Texture2D(2, 2, TextureFormat.RGBA32, true)` with `ImageConversion.LoadImage`.
      - If `mipmapCount <= 1` afterwards, copy the pixels into a `new Texture2D(w, h, RGBA32, true)` (`SetPixels32`, then `Apply(true, true)`) and destroy the first texture.
      - Set `filterMode = Trilinear`, `anisoLevel = 4`, `wrapMode = Clamp`; the meander gets `wrapModeU = Repeat`, `wrapModeV = Clamp`. Name it `"Archery_Skyfall_" + name`.
      - Debug, once per name: `[Archery] Skyfall: embedded glyph '{name}' {w}x{h}, mipmapCount {n} (said once)`.
  - **Materials** (section `asset SkyfallSigil.Materials`; made once per `Tex` and cached):
    - The additive source is the game's `Particles_Combat_White_Hard`. Find it through `Fx.FindEffect("Easter Egg Confetti Big").Prefab`'s `ParticleSystemRenderer` with that exact `sharedMaterial.name` (the pattern of Fx.cs:319-325). Failing that, search `Resources.FindObjectsOfTypeAll<ParticleSystemRenderer>()` by the exact name (Fx.cs:266-270). Never `"... Emmissive"`.
    - Per `Tex`, the additive copy is `new Material(src) { name = "Particles_Combat_White_Hard (Archery Skyfall " + tex + ")", renderQueue = 3060 }`. Its `_MainTex` is the texture; for `Combat`, `src.mainTexture` (or the pass's `SpriteSheet_Combat`).
    - The under copy is `new Material(Fx.LineMaterial()) { name = "Archery Skyfall under " + tex, renderQueue = 3050 }` with the same `_MainTex`. It needs `HasProperty("_MainTex")`, as Tablet.cs:1104-1108. Queue 3050 is drawn before 3060, so the alpha copy sits under the glow.
    - The shader has no colour property, so all tint and fade go through vertex colours.
    - The additive source is missing: every piece draws its under layer only, at full alpha. Warn once: `[Archery] Skyfall: the game's ring material 'Particles_Combat_White_Hard' was not found; the sigil is drawn see-through only, without its glow, until the settings are reloaded`.
    - The sheet texture or the line material is missing: `Build` and `Piece` return null. Warn once: `[Archery] Skyfall: the sigil's art (SpriteSheet_Combat or the flat material) was not found; no sigil until the settings are reloaded`.
  - The Summary line, once, the first time the art is ready: `[Archery] Skyfall: sigil art ready: rings on '{add}' over '{under}', runes {k} of 5 by name (the rest embedded), Lightbeam glyph {by name|embedded} (said once)`.
  - `Cfg.Reloaded` clears the failure flags, the spin cache and the "missing" note, so the next `Build` tries again.
  - **Nullable game lookups** (the rule for every `Fx.FindEffect` and `Fx.LineMaterial` use in Tasks 10-12): `Fx.FindEffect` returns `PooledObjectDefinition?` and `Fx.LineMaterial` returns `Material?` (Fx.cs:123, :21). Bind each result to a nullable local and test it before use, for example `PooledObjectDefinition? confetti = Fx.FindEffect("Easter Egg Confetti Big"); if (confetti != null) { ... confetti.Prefab ... }` and `Material? flat = Fx.LineMaterial(); if (flat == null) { /* the art-missing path above */ } else under = new Material(flat) { ... };`. Never dereference the call directly: that is CS8602/CS8604, and the build gate wants 0 warnings.
  - **`Warm()`** (no section of its own: it runs the `asset SkyfallSigil.FindTextures` and `asset SkyfallSigil.Materials` sections): runs the texture pass and the material setup that the first `Build` would run (the same caches), builds nothing, and returns at once when headless, when the class's `failed` flag is set, or when already done this settings load. Task 11's warm-up calls it while no storm runs, so no `FindObjectsOfTypeAll` runs in a release's frame (the design's anti-goal: "a frame stall when it fires").

- [ ] **Step 7: Add the pieces, the pools and the styles to `SkyfallSigil.cs`.**
  - **Host:** one `new GameObject("ArcherySkyfall")` with `DontDestroyOnLoad`, made at the first build (SeekCage.cs:240-243). Every pooled object is parented under it. `Layer(go)` sets `go.layer = SkyLayer`, and is called on EVERY GameObject that holds a renderer. A new GameObject starts on layer 0 and does not inherit its parent's.
  - **Quad:**
    - A GameObject with its own 4-vertex `Mesh` (the Tablet.Quad shape, Tablet.cs:645-654), a `MeshFilter` and the `MeshRenderer` for its blend.
    - `Blend.Both` adds an `"under"` child sharing the same mesh, with the under material and a `MaterialPropertyBlock` whose `_Color` is `(1, 1, 1, SigilUnderAlpha)` (Sprites/Default multiplies `_Color` into the vertex colour). The block is set when the piece is taken.
    - Shadows are off on every renderer, as Volley.cs:1518-1519.
    - UVs: `Combat` uses cell (row r from the top, col c) at `u = c/8 .. (c+1)/8` and `v = 1-(r+1)/8 .. 1-r/8`, each inset by `0.5/2048`. Every other `Tex` uses 0..1.
    - `Paint` rewrites `mesh.colors` only when a channel moved by more than 1/255.
    - The meander is a `Quad` whose mesh is an annulus instead: 64 segments, `u = 6 * i / 64` (six repeats round the ring), `v` 0 on the inner edge and 1 on the outer.
  - **Pools:**
    - Loose pieces: at most 400.
    - Sigils per style: Sky 6, Ground 6, Hold 8, NailBand 16.
    - Take a free one, else build one, else return null. Debug once per pool: `[Archery] Skyfall: the {pool} pool is full ({cap}); a look is skipped (said once)`. This is the `LockCage.Take` pattern at SeekCage.cs:158-178.
    - `Release` hides the object (`SetActive(false)`) and marks it free.
    - A reused sigil keeps its rune symbols; the seed sets only each band's start angle: `360 * LookSeed.Unit(seed, band, 43)`. `accent` turns the NailBand's inner cyan ring on or off.
  - **Geometry:**
    - A sigil is `Root` (scale = its radius, so every share below is in radii), then one child per band (turned about its local y), then the pieces.
    - A piece lies flat in its band's XZ plane. At angle θ round the band its pose is `localPosition = (sin θ · r, 0, cos θ · r)` and `localRotation = Quaternion.AngleAxis(θ, Vector3.up) * Quaternion.Euler(90f, 0f, 0f)`, so a rune's top points outward. A centred ring has θ = 0.
    - A `Combat` ring of radius r is a quad of side `2r / RingShare[row]`, with `RingShare = { [1] 0.45, [2] 0.70, [3] 0.58 }`: the ring's middle line as a share of its cell, measured from `kit-previews\spritesheet_combat_rows.png`. These are constants, checked by eye in Task 14.
    - Symbols round a band are `Rune1 + (int)(5 * LookSeed.Unit(poolSlot, i, 41))`.
  - **The styles.** The composition below is fixed in code; colours come from the settings. "r" is the radius share, "size" the quad side as a share of the sigil's radius.

  | Style (bands) | Band | Pieces | Colour | Blend |
  |---|---|---|---|---|
  | Sky (6) | 0 core | Lightbeam glyph size 0.34; Combat r0 c3 starburst size 0.5; Combat r3 c2 soft ring r 0.3 | GoldDeep; GoldPale; Gold a0.5 | Both; Add; Add |
  | | 1 medallions | 6 x (Combat r2 c3 ring size 0.15 + a rune size 0.09) at r 0.42, θ = 30 + 60k | Gold; GoldDeep | Both |
  | | 2 | Combat r1 c2 spiky ring, r 0.52 | Gold | Both |
  | | 3 runes | 14 runes size 0.1 at r 0.7; Combat r2 c4 ring r 0.62 | Cyan; CyanPale | Both |
  | | 4 | meander annulus 0.82-0.94 | Cyan | Both |
  | | 5 rim | Combat r2 c0 ring r 1.0; Combat r3 c1 soft ring r 1.0 | Gold; GoldPale a0.5 | Both; Add |
  | Ground (4) | 0 | Lightbeam glyph size 0.3 | FireHot | Add |
  | | 1 | 10 runes size 0.1 at r 0.58; Combat r2 c2 ring r 0.5 | Fire; FireHot | Add |
  | | 2 | meander annulus 0.74-0.86 | Fire | Add |
  | | 3 | Combat r2 c0 ring r 1.0; Combat r3 c1 soft ring r 1.0 | FireHot; Fire a0.5 | Add |
  | Hold (2) | 0 | Combat r2 c1 ring r 1.0; Combat r3 c3 soft ring r 1.0 | Gold (CyanPale when accent); GoldPale a0.5 | Both; Add |
  | | 1 | 8 runes size 0.22 at r 0.8 | GoldDeep (Cyan when accent) | Both |
  | NailBand (2) | 0 | Combat r2 c0 ring r 1.0; Combat r3 c2 soft ring r 1.0; accent only: Combat r2 c3 ring r 0.45 | Gold; GoldPale a0.5; CyanPale | Both; Add; Both |
  | | 1 | 12 runes size 0.08 at r 0.83 | GoldDeep | Both |

  - **Spin** (degrees per second): Sky from `SigilSpin`; Ground uses its first four values / 3; Hold `{ 40, -25 }`; NailBand `{ 12, -18 }`.
  - **`Show(sinceOpen, openSeconds, alpha, mask)`** (section `look SkyfallSigil.Show`), for each band k:
    - `open = SkyfallCurves.Unfold(sinceOpen, k, Bands, openSeconds)`.
    - The band is inactive when `open <= 0` or bit k of `mask` is clear.
    - Its local scale is `0.6 + 0.4 * open`, and its angle `start_k + spin_k * max(0, sinceOpen)`.
    - Each piece is painted with its colour, at alpha `own alpha * alpha * open`.
    - There is no per-frame allocation.
  - **`Runes(into)`:** for every rune piece of an active band, append its world position, rotation, world side (side × `Root.lossyScale.x`), symbol and current colour; return the count.
  - **`Check()`**, clients only (0 when headless or before Init):
    - Parse `SigilSpin`. If it does not give 6 numbers, warn once: `[Archery] Skyfall: SigilSpin has {n} numbers for the sky sigil's 6 bands; the rest stand still`.
    - Evaluate `SkyfallCurves.Unfold` for every style's bands at t = 0, 0.5, 1, 1.5, 2 with `Skyfall.Values.BuildSeconds`.
    - Throw `InvalidOperationException("a Skyfall sigil number is not a number")` on NaN.
    - Return the count evaluated.
  - **`Clear()`:** release every sigil and piece. The pooled objects stay: they are DontDestroyOnLoad.
  - **Fail soft:** `Build` and `Piece` run inside try/catch. A throw sets the class's `failed` flag and warns once, as `SeekLook.FailedOnce` (SeekLook.cs:134-138): `[Archery] Skyfall: the sigil could not be shown ({e.Message}); no sigil until the settings are reloaded`. While the flag is set, both return null. `Cfg.Reloaded` clears it.

- [ ] **Step 8: Create `QuickshotRing/SkyfallSky.cs`: the cave dial.**
  - `internal static class SkyfallSky`. The header comment covers:
    - the game's own cave dimming, untinted;
    - what it writes (`LightIntensityModifier`, `skyColorFade`, through `Snap`, which raises no `FinishedLerp`, so `DisableSkyColor` (DayTimeManager.cs:354-367) never blackens the skybox);
    - every way out (below);
    - "nothing on the server".
  - `Init(cat)` adds one entry: `SkyDim` (bool, `true`), `Clients (looks only): dim the sky near a storm on this machine (the game's own cave dimming, never tinted). Off: Skyfall never touches the sky here.`
  - `Dim(key, centre, from, until, strength)` adds or replaces one request.
    - `until` is held to at most `from + 60` (`const float MaxDimSeconds = 60f`, the timeout).
    - The request table holds at most 32; beyond that a new key is ignored (Debug once).
    - It does nothing on the headless server.
  - `Undim(key)` sets that request's `until` to now.
  - **The dial** (section `asset SkyfallSky.FindDial (DayTimeManager)`):
    - `AtmosphereManager.instance?.AtmospherePlugins` `.OfType<DayTimeManager>()` first (AtmosphereManager.cs:17, :58).
    - Then `Traverse.Create(dtm).Field("LightIntensityModifier").GetValue<Alta.Utilities.LerpingFloatValue>()` and `.Field("skyColorFade")`, the pattern at Fx.cs:255.
    - Cached. While nothing is found, it is searched again at most once a second (`Time.unscaledTime`).
    - A throw sets `dialFailed`, warns once (`[Archery] Skyfall: the sky dial could not be found ({e.Message}); the sky is not dimmed until the settings are reloaded`) and is cleared by `Cfg.Reloaded`.
    - A Summary line, once, when it first drives: `[Archery] Skyfall: the sky dial is live (sun to {SkySun:0.##}, sky fade to {fade:0.##}) (said once)`.
  - **`Frame()`**, in this order:
    1. If headless, return. If `!SkyDim`, or `Player.Current` is null, `Restore()` and return.
    2. If the cached DayTimeManager is destroyed (`== null` while the reference is set), forget it and both lerpers, and set `driving = false, shown = 0` without writing anything. The next scene's DayTimeManager reset itself on Awake (DayTimeManager.cs:329-338).
    3. Drop requests with `now > until + SkyFadeSeconds` or `now > from + MaxDimSeconds`.
    4. `want` = the largest `SkyDial.Amount(true, !dtm.IsEnabled, now - from, until - now, v.SkyFadeSeconds) * strength` over the requests whose centre is within `v.SkyDimMetres` of the local head. The head is `Targets.HeadOf(Player.Current as Player)`; if there is none, nothing is in range.
    5. `canWrite = dtm.IsEnabled && dtm.IsInitialized && !light.IsLerping && !fade.IsLerping`. If it is false, set `shown = 0` and `driving = false`, and return. The game owns the dial in a cave or during its own fade; after one, ours starts again from 0.
    6. `shown = SkyfallCurves.Toward(shown, want, Time.deltaTime, v.SkyFadeSeconds)`.
    7. While `shown > 0`: `light.Snap(1 - shown * (1 - v.SkySun))` and `fade.Snap(shown * Mathf.Min(v.SkyFade, 0.95f))`, each only when it differs from `Current` by more than 1e-4. Set `driving = true`.
    8. When `shown` reaches 0 while driving: `light.Snap(1f)`, `fade.Snap(0f)`, `driving = false`.
  - **`Restore()`:** clear the requests. If driving and the DayTimeManager is alive and `IsEnabled`, then `light.Lerp(1f, Mathf.Max(0.1f, v.SkyFadeSeconds))` and `fade.Lerp(0f, ...)` (the game's own fade). Set `driving = false, shown = 0`. It never throws.
  - **The ways out:**
    - a storm ends: its request's `until` passes;
    - a cave: `IsEnabled` goes false and the game's `Disable()` fades it;
    - a scene change or disconnect: the DayTimeManager is destroyed, or `Player.Current` is null;
    - a timeout: `MaxDimSeconds`;
    - the setting is switched off: `Restore`.
  - **`Check()`:** evaluate `SkyDial.Amount` and `SkyfallCurves.Toward` with the current `Skyfall.Values` at 5 times. Throw on NaN, and return the count. It returns 0 when headless.

- [ ] **Step 9: Create `QuickshotRing/SkyfallHold.cs`: the caster's hold looks.**
  - `internal static class SkyfallHold`; clients only, the local caster only. The header comment lists what the caster sees and that nothing is sent.
  - `Init(cat)` entries (descriptions start `Clients (looks only), the archer's own hold: `):

  | Key | Default | Description (after the prefix) |
  |---|---|---|
  | HoldRingRadius | `0.5` | metres: the largest of the three glyph rings round the arrow, at full hold. |
  | HoldRingSpan | `3.6` | metres ahead of the arrow tip that the farthest ring climbs to. |
  | HoldSigilAlpha | `0.3` | how strongly the faint sigil shows in the sky while holding. |
  | HoldGroundAlpha | `0.35` | how strongly the target glyph shows on the ground under it. |
  | HoldDim | `0.5` | the share of the storm's sky dim while holding. |
  | HoldFollowSeconds | `0.15` | how quickly the faint sigil follows the aim (smoothing, seconds). |
  | HoldSound | `"Shrine Activate Sounds"` | played once when a hold at the sky starts. |
  | ArmSound | `"Skill Orb Acquire Sounds"` | the chime when the hold is armed. |
  | ArmPulses | `2` | haptic pulses in the bow hand when armed. |
  | ArmPulseStrength | `0.85` | their strength (0 to 1). |

  - **State** is per `BowString`, in `Dictionary<BowString, Hold>`. A `Hold` holds:
    - the last `ShotPose` and `heldSeconds`;
    - `LastFrame`, `AimedSince`, `EndedAt = -1`, `Presence`;
    - `Sounded`, `SoundedAt`, `Armed`, `ArmedAt`;
    - three Hold-style sigils (rings 0 and 2 plain, ring 1 accent: gold, cyan, gold), one Sky sigil, one Ground sigil;
    - the smoothed centre, `GroundY` (NaN) and `NextGround`.
  - **`Frame(bow, aim, heldSeconds)`** (section `look SkyfallHold.Frame`) only records. It returns when headless, when `!Skyfall.LocalHolds`, or when `bow == null`. It stores the aim, `heldSeconds` and `LastFrame = Time.time`, and creates the record on first use. Drawing waits for `Tick`, so the looks follow the arrow's pose in this frame's LateUpdate (as Volley.DrawHeld does) instead of the HeldUpdate pose.
  - **`End(bow)`:** sets `EndedAt = Time.time` and calls `SkyfallSky.Undim(SkyfallSky.HoldKey)`.
  - **`Tick()`**, for each hold:
    1. A hold with no `Frame` for 0.25 s ends as `End` would.
    2. The arrow is the docked one (the `ChargeFx.ArrowObj` pattern, ChargeFx.cs:206-211). With no arrow, the hold ends.
    3. `tip = arrow.ArrowTip != null ? ArrowTip.transform.position : arrow.transform.position + fwd * 0.6f`, where `fwd = arrow.transform.forward`.
    4. `aimed = SkyfallRules.Aimed(aim, v) && EndedAt < 0 && !Skyfall.CasterBusy(Skyfall.OwnAccount, Time.time)`. While the caster's storm still runs, a sky shot is a normal shot (Task 8's `ShooterAsks`), so no hold look shows.
       - `heldSeconds` is the time since the 68% crossing: the clock Task 8's arm reads. So the thump comes exactly when a release would ask to cast.
       - The aim is not timed separately. Like the cast, it is judged as it is now; the cast itself is judged on the wire pose at the release.
    5. `Presence = SkyfallCurves.Toward(Presence, aimed ? 1 : 0, dt, 0.25f)`. When it reaches 0 with `EndedAt >= 0`, release everything and drop the record.
    6. `p = clamp01(heldSeconds / v.ArmSeconds)` (0 when ArmSeconds <= 0).
    7. **Sound:** on the first aimed tick of a hold, `VolleyFx.Play(HoldSound, tip)`. At most once every 3 s.
    8. **Arm:** when `SkyfallRules.Armed(aim, heldSeconds, v)` first becomes true:
       - `VolleyFx.Play(ArmSound, tip)`;
       - `VolleyFx.Pulses(Volley.BowHand(bow), ArmPulses, ArmPulseStrength, 0.08f)` (the pattern at Shatter.cs:487-488);
       - set `ArmedAt`;
       - Debug: `[Archery] Skyfall: hold armed after {heldSeconds:0.00} s, sigil at {centre}`.
       
       `Armed` clears when `heldSeconds` drops below `ArmSeconds` again.
    9. **Rings:** ring k is placed at `tip + fwd * SkyfallCurves.HoldRingDistance(k, p, HoldRingSpan)` with `facing = Quaternion.FromToRotation(Vector3.up, fwd)`. Its radius is `HoldRingRadius * HoldRingScale(k, p)`, and it is shown with `Show(Time.time - AimedSince, 0.25f, alpha)`, so it flares open in 0.25 s and its bands turn from then on.
       - `AimedSince` is set on the first aimed tick of a hold, and again whenever the aim comes back after it broke.
       - The three sigils are built with `seed = (uint)bow.GetInstanceID() + k`.
       - `alpha = Presence * (0.7f + 0.3f * p)`.
       - For the 0.25 s after `ArmedAt`, the alpha is 1 (the flash).
    10. **Faint sigil:** the target is `SigilCentre(Seek.H(arrow.transform.position), Seek.H(fwd), v)`, using the same rule as the cast (`Seek.H` is internal, Seek.cs:129; back to Vector3 with a private `V(HVec)` as Seek.cs:128).
        - The smoothed centre moves toward it by `1 - exp(-dt / HoldFollowSeconds)`.
        - Place it at the smoothed centre with `Quaternion.identity` and radius `v.SigilRadius`.
        - `Show(p * v.BuildSeconds, v.BuildSeconds, HoldSigilAlpha * Presence * Ease(p))`.
    11. **Ground glyph:** at most 10 times a second, `Skyfall.GroundAt(c.x, c.z, c.y + 0.5, tip.y - Skyfall.GroundBelowMetres, out y)` (the same depth as SkyfallServer, Task 9). On a hit, place the Ground sigil at `(c.x, y + 0.15, c.z)` with radius `v.RainRadius + v.DomeRadius`. It shows only bands 0 and 3, the glyph and the outer ring: `Show(p * v.BuildSeconds, v.BuildSeconds, HoldGroundAlpha * Presence * Ease(p), 0b1001)`. With no ground it is hidden.
    12. **Sky dim:** `SkyfallSky.Dim(SkyfallSky.HoldKey, c, AimedSince, Time.time + v.SkyFadeSeconds + 0.3f, HoldDim * Presence * Ease(p))`. `until` must stay more than `SkyFadeSeconds` ahead, or SkyDial's ramp-out caps the dim (at `+ 0.3 s` alone the sun drops by at most 1.5%). `End()` still releases it at once through `Undim`.
  - A throw anywhere in `Tick` sets `holdFailed`, releases every hold's sigils, and warns once: `[Archery] Skyfall: the hold looks could not be shown ({e.Message}); the hold is drawn without them until the settings are reloaded`. `Cfg.Reloaded` clears the flag.
  - When `Player.Current` is null, `Tick` calls `Clear()`.
  - **`Clear()`:** release all and empty the table.
  - **`Check()`:** evaluate `HoldRingDistance` and `HoldRingScale` for rings 0-2 at p = 0, 0.5 and 1 with the current settings. Throw on NaN and return 9. It returns 0 when headless.

**Interfaces:**

It also calls `Begin` for a `Fizzled` storm, whose `Impacts` is empty and whose `CX/CY/CZ` are NaN when the centre is unknown.
  - Task 9: `Skyfall.GroundAt(double x, double z, double top, double bottom, out float y)`, `Skyfall.GroundBelowMetres`.

- Produces (Task 12 uses exactly these):
  - `SkyfallCurves` (Unity-free): `public static readonly float[] CoolStops = { 0f, 0.15f, 0.4f, 1f };`, `public static float Fall(float u)`, `public static float Glow(float cool)`, `public static int RampStop(float cool, out float blend)`, `public static float Dome(float t, float domeAt, float landAt, float grow, float linger, float shrink, bool ground)`.
  - `SkyfallLook.cs`:
    ```csharp
    internal static class SkyfallLook {
        internal static void Init(MelonPreferences_Category cat); internal static int Check();
        internal static void Start(SkyStorm s, bool caster);              // contract
        internal static void Tick();                                      // contract, Main.OnLateUpdate
        internal static void Fizzle(uint arrowId);                        // that storm puffs out now (idempotent)
        internal static SkyfallSigil.Sigil? TakeSkySigil(uint arrowId);   // the storm stops drawing its sky sigil and hands it over (Task 12's shatter)
        internal static void Scorch(Vector3 at, float size, uint seed);
        internal static bool GetScaledParts(PooledObjectDefinition? def, Vector3 at, Quaternion rot, float k);  // false for a null def or an empty pool; also scales LOCAL-scaling systems; restored on release
        internal static void Firework(Vector3 at, int colourIndex, bool always);   // festival burst in FireworkColours[colourIndex % n]; always = ignore the budget
        internal static void Confetti(Vector3 at, float k);
        internal static ExplosionBudget Budget { get; }                    // this machine's, cap Values.MaxLiveExplosions
        internal static float ExplosionLifeSeconds { get; }
        internal static string ExplosionName { get; }                      // [Skyfall] ExplosionEffect
        internal static float FlashSeconds { get; }                        // [Skyfall] FlashSeconds (the Nail's light uses it too)
        internal enum ArrowRole { Head, Shaft, Vane }
        internal sealed class ArrowPart { public Mesh Mesh = null!; public Material[] Materials = null!; public string Name = "";
                                          public Vector3 Pos; public Quaternion Rot; public Vector3 Scale; public ArrowRole Role; }   // in the arrow's frame: +z toward the point, the point at the origin, real metres
        internal static IReadOnlyList<ArrowPart>? PlainArrow();           // the plain Arrow (56460)'s LOD0 parts, read once; null = not available (said once)
        internal static float PlainArrowLength { get; }                    // point to nock, metres (about 1.2)
        internal static void Clear();
    }
    ```

**What this task decides** (the timeline; t = `Time.time - s.StartTime`, the same on every machine):

| Moment | Look |
|---|---|
| 0 | `ReleaseSound` at the release point. A gold ghost of the plain Arrow (scale 1) streaks from `Release` to the sigil centre in `StreakSeconds`, slowing as it arrives (fraction `1 - Fall(1 - u)`). |
| 0 to `BuildSeconds` | The sky sigil (Style.Sky, radius `SigilRadius`) unfolds core first: `Show(t, BuildSeconds, alpha)`. |
| `StreakSeconds` | `SigilSound` and the storm's one `Fx.Flash` (`GoldPale`) at the centre. |
| `TwinDelaySeconds` | The ground twin (Style.Ground, radius `RainRadius + DomeRadius`, at the centre's ground + 0.12 m) starts: `Show(t - TwinDelay, BuildSeconds - TwinDelay, alpha)`, with `TwinSound` there. |
| each `DomeAt` | The impact's dome grows in over `DomeGrowSeconds`. It stays until `LandAt + DomeLingerSeconds`, then shrinks over `DomeShrinkSeconds`. Radius `DomeRadius` (`NailDomeRadius` for the Nail). It fades by scale only: the game's dome shader has no known alpha. |
| `LandAt - FallSeconds` to `LandAt` | A rain impact's giant arrow falls (`Fall(u)`) from the sigil plane to its spot, with its trail. In festival mode it falls to `FestivalBurstHeight` above the spot. |
| each `LandAt` (rain) | Normal: the explosion share (below), its scorch, sparks. Festival: firework, confetti, heal burst. Both: "OUT!" for this player in festival mode. |
| `LandAt` (Nail) | Only the dome and "OUT!" here. The rest is Task 12. |
| `NailLandAt` | The twin burns away over 0.4 s. If Task 12 has not taken the sky sigil by `NailLandAt + 1.5`, it fades over 0.6 s here. |
| fizzle | The sky sigil's core alone (mask `0b1`) puffs out over `FizzleSeconds`, with a `FizzlePuff` (parts x2) and gold sparks at the centre and `FizzleSound`. Domes, rain and twin go at once; the dim is released. A storm relayed as `Fizzled` never shows a dome, a giant arrow or the twin at all: only the streak, the core and the puff. |

- **Every wave lands at once** (Task 3), so only a share of each wave explodes: `int perWave = Math.Max(1, (v.MaxLiveExplosions + v.Waves - 1) / v.Waves);   // ceil without float` (4 by default; a plain `MaxLiveExplosions / Waves` is integer division and would give 2 for 12 / 5). The impacts whose `Index % ArrowsPerWave < perWave` try the `Budget` (`TryTake(now, ExplosionLifeSeconds)`). The rest, and refusals, show `ImpactSparks` sparks only.
  - Without this, waves 1 and 2 would fill the 12 and wave 3 would show no explosion at all.
  - `OverBudgetSound` plays only when no explosion was started in the same frame, so it never stacks on 'Huge Explosion'.
  - Festival fireworks use the same share with their own `ExplosionBudget` (lifetime `FireworkLifeSeconds`). Each Firework carries an active point light, and the budget bounds those too.
- **The caster's fizzle after a take:** when `Skyfall.LocalTakenAt` changes, every caster storm with `LocalTakenAt - S.StartTime <= 1.0` (`const float TakenWindowSeconds = 1f`) fizzles.
  - A take that reached this game within a second of the release can have reached the server before the shot, which refused it.
  - An older storm was surely accepted, and spectators see it run, so it keeps running here too.
- **Late landings:** a landing first seen more than 1 s after its `LandAt` (a hitch, or a storm started late) is marked done without effects (`const float LateSeconds = 1f`).
- **Distance:** a storm whose centre is farther than `LookMaxMetres` from this player's head at `Start` is not drawn. The caster always draws theirs. The far art is on layer 1 anyway.
- **Ground:** each landing's ground comes from `Skyfall.GroundAt(X, Z, CY + 0.5, Release.Y - Skyfall.GroundBelowMetres, out y)`. These are SkyfallServer's bounds (Task 9), so the client's dome sits where the server's blast is. It is cast once at `Start` and retried at `DomeAt` while unknown. With no ground, that impact skips every ground look (dome, scorch, effects, "OUT!"), and its arrow falls to `Release.Y` and is released.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the curves test.** In `QuickshotRing.Tests/SkyfallPatternTests.cs`, add this method and call `SkyfallRainCurves();` as the last line of `Run()`:

```csharp
// Task 11: the storm look's curves: the giant arrows' fall, a scorch cooling from fire to char, and a danger dome's size (looks only).
static void SkyfallRainCurves() {
    Check("skyfall curves: a giant arrow starts its fall at the sigil", SkyfallCurves.Fall(0f) == 0f);
    Check("skyfall curves: half-way through its time a giant arrow has fallen a quarter of the way", Near(SkyfallCurves.Fall(0.5f), 0.25f));
    Check("skyfall curves: a giant arrow reaches its spot at the end", SkyfallCurves.Fall(1f) == 1f);
    Check("skyfall curves: the fall holds still outside its time", SkyfallCurves.Fall(1.5f) == 1f && SkyfallCurves.Fall(-0.2f) == 0f);
    Check("skyfall curves: a broken time falls nowhere", SkyfallCurves.Fall(float.NaN) == 0f);
    Check("skyfall curves: a giant arrow speeds up as it falls",
        SkyfallCurves.Fall(0.75f) - SkyfallCurves.Fall(0.5f) > SkyfallCurves.Fall(0.5f) - SkyfallCurves.Fall(0.25f));

    Check("skyfall curves: a fresh scorch glows fully", SkyfallCurves.Glow(0f) == 1f);
    Check("skyfall curves: a scorch glows half at 0.2 of its cooling", Near(SkyfallCurves.Glow(0.2f), 0.5f));
    Check("skyfall curves: a scorch has stopped glowing by 0.4 of its cooling", SkyfallCurves.Glow(0.4f) == 0f && SkyfallCurves.Glow(1f) == 0f);

    Check("skyfall curves: a fresh scorch is the first fire colour", SkyfallCurves.RampStop(0f, out float b0) == 0 && b0 == 0f);
    Check("skyfall curves: at 0.15 a scorch is exactly the second colour", SkyfallCurves.RampStop(0.15f, out float b1) == 1 && Near(b1, 0f));
    Check("skyfall curves: at 0.275 a scorch is half-way from the second colour to the third",
        SkyfallCurves.RampStop(0.275f, out float b2) == 1 && Near(b2, 0.5f));
    Check("skyfall curves: at 0.7 a scorch is half-way from the third colour to char", SkyfallCurves.RampStop(0.7f, out float b3) == 2 && Near(b3, 0.5f));
    Check("skyfall curves: a cooled or broken scorch is held to the ends of the ramp",
        SkyfallCurves.RampStop(1f, out float b4) == 2 && Near(b4, 1f) && SkyfallCurves.RampStop(2f, out float b5) == 2 && Near(b5, 1f)
        && SkyfallCurves.RampStop(float.NaN, out float b6) == 0 && b6 == 0f);

    Check("skyfall curves: a dome with no ground under it never shows",
        SkyfallCurves.Dome(0.5f, 0f, 1.75f, 0.3f, 0.2f, 0.3f, false) == 0f && SkyfallCurves.Dome(1.75f, 0f, 1.75f, 0.3f, 0.2f, 0.3f, false) == 0f);
    Check("skyfall curves: a dome is not there before its DomeAt", SkyfallCurves.Dome(-0.01f, 0f, 1.75f, 0.3f, 0.2f, 0.3f, true) == 0f);
    Check("skyfall curves: a dome is half grown half-way through its grow time", Near(SkyfallCurves.Dome(0.15f, 0f, 1.75f, 0.3f, 0.2f, 0.3f, true), 0.5f));
    Check("skyfall curves: a dome is full until its arrow has landed and lingered",
        Near(SkyfallCurves.Dome(1f, 0f, 1.75f, 0.3f, 0.2f, 0.3f, true), 1f) && Near(SkyfallCurves.Dome(1.9f, 0f, 1.75f, 0.3f, 0.2f, 0.3f, true), 1f));
    Check("skyfall curves: a dome has shrunk away after its shrink time", SkyfallCurves.Dome(2.3f, 0f, 1.75f, 0.3f, 0.2f, 0.3f, true) == 0f);
}
```

- [ ] **Step 2: Run the tests.** `dotnet run --project QuickshotRing.Tests -c Release`. Expected: a build error, `error CS0117: 'SkyfallCurves' does not contain a definition for 'Fall'`. The same error names `Glow`, `RampStop` and `Dome`.

- [ ] **Step 3: Add the members to `SkyfallCurves`** (SkyfallRules.cs, after Task 10's members):
  - `CoolStops = { 0f, 0.15f, 0.4f, 1f }`: where a scorch's colour is `FireHot`, `Fire`, `FireDeep` and `Char` (SkyfallLook maps the stops to the colours).
  - `Fall(u)`: 0 for NaN or `u <= 0`, 1 for `u >= 1`, else `u * u`. The giant arrows speed up into the ground.
  - `Glow(cool)`: `1 - Ease(cool / 0.4f)`. The scorch's fire glow is gone by the third stop.
  - `RampStop(cool, out blend)`:
    - `cool` is held to `[0, 1]`, and NaN counts as 0.
    - The result `i` is the last stop index with `CoolStops[i] <= cool`, at most 2.
    - `blend = (cool - CoolStops[i]) / (CoolStops[i + 1] - CoolStops[i])`.
  - `Dome(t, domeAt, landAt, grow, linger, shrink, ground)`: a danger dome's size, 0 (hidden) to 1 (its full radius).
    - 0 when `!ground`, when `t` is NaN, or when `t < domeAt`: no dome without ground (Review Focus 3).
    - Otherwise `Math.Min(grow > 0 ? Ease((t - domeAt) / grow) : 1f, 1f - (shrink > 0 ? Ease((t - landAt - linger) / shrink) : (t >= landAt + linger ? 1f : 0f)))`.

- [ ] **Step 4: Run the tests.** `dotnet run --project QuickshotRing.Tests -c Release`. Expected: "ALL PASS (N)", where N is the previous count plus 19.

  - **Settings:** `Init(cat)` creates these entries. They are per client, never `Cfg.Shared`. Every description starts `Clients (looks only): `.

  | Key | Default | Description (after the prefix) |
  |---|---|---|
  | LookMaxMetres | `600` | a storm whose sigil is farther than this from this player is not drawn here (the archer always draws their own). |
  | StreakSeconds | `0.35` | seconds the archer's arrow takes to streak up into the sigil as a gold ghost. |
  | TwinDelaySeconds | `0.25` | seconds after the release before the sigil's twin starts to burn into the ground. |
  | RainAlpha | `0.55` | how solid the see-through gold giant arrows are. |
  | RainFan | `0.5` | where a giant arrow starts in the sigil: this share of the way from the sigil's centre to its landing spot (0 = all from the centre, 1 = straight down). |
  | RainTrailMetres | `9` | the length of a giant arrow's trail. |
  | RainTrailWidth | `0.9` | its width at the arrow, in metres. |
  | DomeGrowSeconds | `0.3` | seconds a danger dome takes to grow in. |
  | DomeLingerSeconds | `0.2` | seconds a dome stays after its arrow lands. |
  | DomeShrinkSeconds | `0.3` | seconds it then takes to shrink away. |
  | ExplosionEffect | `"Explosion"` | the game's effect for a landing. It plays 'Huge Explosion' itself: never add that sound. |
  | ExplosionScale | `1.6` | its size, times the game's own. |
  | ExplosionLifeSeconds | `6` | how long one counts against MaxLiveExplosions (the game's effect lives 6 s). |
  | ImpactSparks | `24` | the sparks of a landing that shows no explosion. |
  | OverBudgetSound | `"Small Explosion"` | the sound of a landing that shows no explosion, played only when no explosion started in that frame. |
  | ScorchSize | `3.5` | metres across a landing's scorch. |
  | ScorchSeconds | `14` | seconds a scorch stays (it fades over its last 3). |
  | ScorchCoolSeconds | `6` | seconds a scorch takes to cool from fire to char. |
  | ReleaseSound | `"Lightbeam Shot Effect Sounds"` | at the release, as the ghost streaks up. |
  | SigilSound | `"Shrine Activate Sounds"` | at the sigil when the ghost reaches it. |
  | TwinSound | `"Shockwave Sounds"` | where the twin burns into the ground. |
  | FlashBrightness | `3` | the storm's one light, when the sigil opens. |
  | FlashRange | `40` | its range in metres. |
  | FlashSeconds | `0.5` | its life in seconds (also the World-Nail's light). |
  | FizzleSeconds | `0.8` | seconds a refused cast's sigil takes to puff out. |
  | FizzlePuff | `"Large Smoke Puff"` | the puff it ends in. |
  | FizzleSound | `"Small Explosion"` | its sound. |
  | FireworkEffect | `"Firework Explosion"` | festival: a landing's burst. |
  | FireworkColours | `"#FFCE00, #66F6FF, #FFF285"` | festival: the fireworks' colours, in turn. |
  | FireworkLifeSeconds | `8` | festival: how long a firework counts against MaxLiveExplosions (each carries the game's own light). |
  | FestivalBurstHeight | `6` | festival: metres above its spot where a giant arrow bursts. |
  | ConfettiEffect | `"Easter Egg Confetti Big"` | festival: shown at every other landing. |
  | HealEffect | `"Healing Explosion"` | festival: at a landing with a player within FestivalCareRadius of it. |
  | OutText | `"OUT!"` | festival: what a player caught in a dome sees, on their own screen only. |
  | OutColour | `"#FFF285"` | its colour. |
  | OutSeconds | `2.5` | how long it shows. |
  | OutDistance | `1.2` | metres in front of the face (the game's popup turns to face the player only within about 3 m). |

  - **The plain Arrow's parts** (`PlainArrow()`, section `asset SkyfallLook.PlainArrow`, read once):
    - The source is `PrefabManager.GetPrefab(56460u)`, the plain Arrow (Range.cs:91). It is never instantiated; on a client its meshes and materials are intact (PrefabManager strips them only on the headless server).
    - The renderers are `lods[0].renderers` of its `GetComponentInChildren<LODGroup>(true)`. Failing that, every MeshRenderer whose name ends `_LOD0`; failing that, every MeshRenderer. Do not test `activeInHierarchy`: this is an asset, not a scene object (client-looks map, gap 1).
    - Each part's pose in the prefab root's frame: `root.InverseTransformPoint(position)`, `Inverse(root.rotation) * rotation`, and `lossyScale` divided per axis by the root's. This reproduces the arrow exactly (runtime-looks.md:166).
    - `Materials = r.sharedMaterials` (a copy of the array). `Role` comes from the mesh name, lower case: contains `spike` -> Head, `feather` -> Vane, otherwise Shaft.
    - The point is the largest z of the Head parts' transformed mesh-bounds corners (all parts if there is no head); the nock is the smallest z of all. Every `Pos.z` is shifted by `-point`, and `PlainArrowLength = point - nock`.
    - A Summary line, once: `[Archery] Skyfall: giant arrows are the Arrow's {n} parts ({names}), {PlainArrowLength:0.00} m (said once)`.
    - Missing: warn once `[Archery] Skyfall: the plain Arrow's meshes were not found; no giant arrows until the settings are reloaded`, and return null.

    - Copy it: `new Material(src) { name = "SelectedPiece (Archery Skyfall gold)" }`, with `_TintColor = (Gold.r, Gold.g, Gold.b, RainAlpha)`. If `_TintColor` is missing, use `Volley.HeavyLook.Tint(copy, colour)`; when that returns "", destroy the copy.
    - Fallback: `new Material(flat) { color = gold at RainAlpha }` from a null-tested `Material? flat = Fx.LineMaterial()` (Task 10's nullable rule; with no flat material either, no giant arrows). Warn once: `[Archery] Skyfall: the Etheral Arrow's material was not found; the giant arrows are flat gold`.

Look first on `PrefabManager.GetPrefab(1798u)` ('Arrow Light Beam Effect', kit #4): its TrailRenderers and ParticleSystemRenderers, by that exact `sharedMaterial.name`. Then `FindObjectsOfTypeAll<Material>()` by the name; then `Fx.LineMaterial()`.
    - Copy it as `"... (Archery Skyfall trail)"`. The colours go through `LineRenderer.startColor/endColor`: `GoldPale` at alpha 0.9 to alpha 0.
  - **The dome** (section `asset SkyfallLook.Dome`, once):

Before trying the `Budget`: an explosion within `Values.MinImpactSpacing` of one started on this machine less than `ExplosionLifeSeconds` ago (any storm; keep the last 64 points and start times in a ring) shows the sparks below instead and takes no budget slot. Every started rain explosion goes into the ring. This keeps the design's "explosions are spaced 1.5 m apart within 6 s" across overlapping storms and in the pattern's crowded mode. While the flag is set, `Start` returns at once. `Cfg.Reloaded` clears the flag, the material caches and the missing-asset notes.

### Task 12: Client looks III: the World-Nail and the sigil's shatter

**Files:**
- Create: `QuickshotRing/SkyfallNail.cs`
- Modify: `QuickshotRing/SkyfallRules.cs`: append `Slide`, `BandHeight`, `BandRadius`, `RollOut` and `RollFrame` to `SkyfallCurves`.
- Modify: `QuickshotRing/SkyfallLook.cs` (Task 11): four one-line calls: in `Start`, `Fizzle`, `Clear` and `Tick`'s warm-up.
- Modify: `QuickshotRing/Skyfall.cs`: `Init` (one call).
- Modify: `QuickshotRing/Main.cs`: one section, and one line after Task 11's `SkyfallLook.Tick` line.
- Modify: `QuickshotRing/SelfCheck.cs`: add `SkyfallNail.Check()` to the looks line.
- Test: `QuickshotRing.Tests/SkyfallPatternTests.cs`: one new method, called last from `Run()`.

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes:
  - From the contract: `SkyStorm`, `SkyImpact` (the one with `Nail == true`), `SkyCode`.
  - Task 3: `SkyfallPattern.NailLandAt(v)`.
  - Task 6: `Skyfall.Values` (`NailLength`, `NailSlideSeconds`, `NailDomeRadius`, `NailStandSeconds`, `SigilHeight`, `SigilRadius`).
  - Task 9: `Skyfall.GroundAt(double, double, double, double, out float)`, `Skyfall.GroundBelowMetres`.
  - Task 10: `SkyfallSigil.Build(Style.NailBand, seed, accent)`, `Piece`, `Tex`, `Blend`, `Sigil.Place/Show/Runes/Release`, `RuneSpot`, `Quad.Place/Cell/Paint/Release`, `Layer`, the colours; `SkyfallCurves.Ease/Envelope`.
  - Task 11: `SkyfallLook.PlainArrow()`, `PlainArrowLength`, `ArrowRole`, `ArrowPart`, `TakeSkySigil`, `Scorch`, `GetScaledParts`, `Firework`, `Confetti`, `Budget`, `ExplosionLifeSeconds`, `ExplosionName`, `FlashSeconds`.
  - Existing: `Fx.FindEffect`, `Fx.GetScaled`, `Fx.Flash`, `VolleyFx.Play`, `VolleyFx.Sparks.Emit`, `LookSeed.Unit`.
- Produces:
  - `SkyfallCurves` (Unity-free): `public static float Slide(float u)`, `BandHeight(int band, int bands)`, `BandRadius(int band, int bands)`, `RollOut(float t, float seconds)` (all `float`), and `public static int RollFrame(float t, float seconds)`.
  - `SkyfallNail.cs`: `internal static class SkyfallNail` with `Init(MelonPreferences_Category cat)`, `int Check()`, `Start(SkyStorm s)` and `Tick()` (the contract), `Stop(uint arrowId)`, `Clear()` and `Warm()` (the gold head's search, ahead of time, from Task 11's warm-up).

**What this task decides.** Times are seconds after the release: `land = SkyfallPattern.NailLandAt(v)`, `slide = land - v.NailSlideSeconds`, `bands = slide - NailBandLeadSeconds`.

| Moment | Look |
|---|---|
| `bands` | Seven NailBand sigils open over 0.5 s each, band k 0.08k s later, stacked over the centre between the ground and the sigil. Band k (0 = lowest) sits at `ground + BandHeight(k, 7) * (CY - ground)`, with radius `NailDomeRadius * NailBandScale * BandRadius(k, 7)`. Odd bands are the accent (cyan inner ring), so gold and cyan alternate as in the picture. |
| `slide` | The Nail appears with its point at the sigil plane (`CY`), with the flash `NailAppearEffect` (parts x `NailAppearScale`) and `NailAppearSound` at the sigil centre. |
| `slide` to `land` | It slides straight down: `pointY = CY + (bottom - CY) * Slide(u)`, with `bottom = ground - NailEmbedShare * NailLength`. As the point passes band k, that band flares: alpha 0.75 rises to 1 and back over 0.3 s (`0.75 + 0.25 * Envelope(t - passed_k, 0.3, 0, 0.3)`). |
| `land`, normal | The biggest blast: 'Explosion' x `NailExplosionScale` (it plays 'Huge Explosion' itself; it takes from `SkyfallLook.Budget` but plays even when refused: it is the finale, one per storm), `NailShockwave` x `NailShockwaveScale`, `NailSlam` via `GetScaledParts` x `NailSlamScale` (the flint debris), `Scorch(ground, NailScorchSize, id)`, and the storm's second and last light, `Fx.Flash(ground + Vector3.up * 2f, SkyfallSigil.FireHot, NailFlashBrightness, NailFlashRange, SkyfallLook.FlashSeconds)`. |
| `land`, festival | The Nail goes at once and bursts into the largest firework. That is `FestivalNailFireworks` `Firework(at, k, always: true)`: number 0 at `ground + 8 m` over the centre, number k >= 1 round it at radius `NailDomeRadius * 0.5` and angle `360 * (k - 1) / (N - 1)` degrees, at `ground + 6 + 2k` m (60 degrees apart for the default 7). Then three `Confetti(at, 2f)` among them, and `NailLevelUp` at the ground (native size). |
| `land`, both | Each band drops and rolls out over the ground as a ring pair: the hard ring (`Combat` row 2, `Cell(2, RollFrame(...))`, the game's thick-to-broken frames) and a soft ring (row 3, same frame, alpha x0.5). Each starts at its band's radius and grows by `NailRollMetres * RollOut(t - land, NailRollSeconds + 0.1k)`, fading by `1 - Ease(...)`. They lie flat at `ground + 0.1 + 0.02k` (no z-fighting). Even bands are `Gold`, odd bands `CyanPale`. |
| `land + ShatterDelaySeconds` | The sky sigil shatters (`SkyfallLook.TakeSkySigil`). Every rune becomes a falling rune piece, and the rim breaks into two broken-ring pieces (row 2, cols 6 and 7, `Gold`) that grow to 1.3 x over 0.6 s and fade. `ShatterSound` plays at the centre. |
| `land` to `land + NailStandSeconds` | Normal mode: the Nail stands, embedded. |
| `land + NailStandSeconds` | It crumbles top to bottom in five puffs, `CrumbleStepSeconds` apart. Puff j sits at `ground + (0.9 - 0.2j) * NailLength * (1 - NailEmbedShare)`: `CrumbleEffect` via `GetScaledParts` x `CrumbleScale`, and 20 `FireHot` sparks (`Emit(p, Vector3.up, 20, c, 80f, 3f, 3f)`). `CrumbleSound` plays at j = 0. The vanes hide at j = 0, the shaft at j = 2, the head at j = 4; then the Nail is released. |

- **The model.** One root `ArcherySkyfallNail` per storm (at most 2 pooled, 1 per storm). Under it are three containers, one per `ArrowRole`, each at the root's origin. Every `ArrowPart` is a child of its role's container at the part's own pose, with its real `Materials` (shared, never modified).
  - The head gets the material named `NailHeadMaterial` instead, when that is set and found (the design's "fancy spike in gold").
  - Each container's `localScale = (S * k, S * k, S)`, where `S = v.NailLength / SkyfallLook.PlainArrowLength` and `k` = `NailShaftThick` 2.9, `NailHeadThick` 2.1 or `NailVaneThick` 2.3. That is thicker along the arrow's own x and y, with z its length.
  - The parts sit inside the scaled containers, so a feather's offset from the shaft grows with the shaft. Any rotation of a part (the spike's 90 degrees about x) is kept exactly: Unity draws with the full matrix.
  - The root's `+z` is the point (`Quaternion.LookRotation(Vector3.down, yaw)`, with `yaw = Euler(0, 360 * LookSeed.Unit(id, 0, 61), 0) * Vector3.forward`), and the root's origin is the point. The root's position is `(CX, pointY, CZ)`.
  - Every part is on layer 1 (`SkyfallSigil.Layer`). Shadows follow `NailShadows`.
  - It is built at `slide`, not at `Start`: eight objects, made once per pooled Nail.
- **The falling runes:**
  - For each `RuneSpot` (at most 64 live for all storms), a `Piece(spot.Symbol, 0, 0, Blend.Both)` at the spot's pose and size, painted the spot's colour.
  - Velocity: `outward * ShatterKick * (0.6 + 0.8 * LookSeed.Unit(id, j, 71)) + up * 2`, where `outward` is the horizontal unit vector from the centre to the spot.
  - It spins about its own normal at `60 + 60 * LookSeed.Unit(id, j, 72)` degrees a second, turning the other way when `LookSeed.Unit(id, j, 73) < 0.5`.
  - Each frame: `v.y -= ShatterGravity * dt`, then `pos += v * dt`.
  - Alpha is `Envelope(age, ShatterSeconds, 0, 1.2)`. It is released at `ShatterSeconds`.
- **No ground** (`Skyfall.GroundAt` false at `Start`, and again at `bands`):
  - The bands span `CY - SigilHeight` to `CY`, and the Nail slides to `CY - SigilHeight`.
  - At `land` the Nail and the bands are released with no impact look; the shatter still happens (sky art).
- **A late frame:** an impact first seen more than 1 s after `land` skips its effects, but still rolls the bands, shatters and stands.
- **A fizzled storm** never gets a Nail (`Start` returns), and `Stop` releases everything of one storm at once.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the curves test.** In `QuickshotRing.Tests/SkyfallPatternTests.cs`, add this method and call `SkyfallNailCurves();` as the last line of `Run()`:

```csharp
// Task 12: the World-Nail's curves: its slide, the seven bands it passes, and the bands rolling out over the ground (looks only).
static void SkyfallNailCurves() {
    Check("skyfall curves: the Nail starts its slide at the sigil", SkyfallCurves.Slide(0f) == 0f);
    Check("skyfall curves: half-way through its slide the Nail has gone an eighth of the way", Near(SkyfallCurves.Slide(0.5f), 0.125f));
    Check("skyfall curves: the slide ends in the ground and holds still outside its time",
        SkyfallCurves.Slide(1f) == 1f && SkyfallCurves.Slide(2f) == 1f && SkyfallCurves.Slide(-1f) == 0f);
    Check("skyfall curves: a broken slide time goes nowhere", SkyfallCurves.Slide(float.NaN) == 0f);
    Check("skyfall curves: the Nail is fastest at the end (it is driven in)",
        SkyfallCurves.Slide(1f) - SkyfallCurves.Slide(0.9f) > SkyfallCurves.Slide(0.1f) - SkyfallCurves.Slide(0f));

    Check("skyfall curves: the lowest of seven bands is an eighth of the way up", Near(SkyfallCurves.BandHeight(0, 7), 0.125f));
    Check("skyfall curves: the highest of seven bands is seven eighths of the way up", Near(SkyfallCurves.BandHeight(6, 7), 0.875f));
    Check("skyfall curves: the middle band is half-way up", Near(SkyfallCurves.BandHeight(3, 7), 0.5f));
    Check("skyfall curves: a band index outside the seven is held to the ends",
        SkyfallCurves.BandHeight(9, 7) == SkyfallCurves.BandHeight(6, 7) && SkyfallCurves.BandHeight(-2, 7) == SkyfallCurves.BandHeight(0, 7));
    Check("skyfall curves: the lowest band is the widest (full size)", SkyfallCurves.BandRadius(0, 7) == 1f);
    Check("skyfall curves: the highest band is 0.45 of the lowest", Near(SkyfallCurves.BandRadius(6, 7), 0.45f));
    Check("skyfall curves: the middle band is 0.725 of the lowest", Near(SkyfallCurves.BandRadius(3, 7), 0.725f));
    Check("skyfall curves: a single band is full size", SkyfallCurves.BandRadius(0, 1) == 1f);

    Check("skyfall curves: a band starts rolling out from where it fell", SkyfallCurves.RollOut(0f, 1.4f) == 0f);
    Check("skyfall curves: half-way through, a band has rolled three quarters of the way", Near(SkyfallCurves.RollOut(0.7f, 1.4f), 0.75f));
    Check("skyfall curves: a band ends its roll and stays there", SkyfallCurves.RollOut(1.4f, 1.4f) == 1f && SkyfallCurves.RollOut(3f, 1.4f) == 1f);
    Check("skyfall curves: no roll time rolls out at once", SkyfallCurves.RollOut(0.5f, 0f) == 1f);
    Check("skyfall curves: a rolling ring starts as the thick hand-drawn ring (frame 0)", SkyfallCurves.RollFrame(0f, 1.2f) == 0);
    Check("skyfall curves: a little past half-way a rolling ring is on frame 4", SkyfallCurves.RollFrame(0.65f, 1.2f) == 4);
    Check("skyfall curves: near its end a rolling ring is the broken ring (frame 7)", SkyfallCurves.RollFrame(1.19f, 1.2f) == 7);
    Check("skyfall curves: a rolling ring never goes past frame 7", SkyfallCurves.RollFrame(1.2f, 1.2f) == 7 && SkyfallCurves.RollFrame(5f, 1.2f) == 7);
    Check("skyfall curves: a broken or early time shows frame 0", SkyfallCurves.RollFrame(-1f, 1.2f) == 0 && SkyfallCurves.RollFrame(float.NaN, 1.2f) == 0);
    bool forward = true; int last = 0;
    for (int s = 0; s <= 120; s++) { int f = SkyfallCurves.RollFrame(s * 0.01f, 1.2f); if (f < last) forward = false; last = f; }
    Check("skyfall curves: a rolling ring only ever breaks up further", forward);
}
```

- [ ] **Step 2: Run the tests.** `dotnet run --project QuickshotRing.Tests -c Release`. Expected: a build error, `error CS0117: 'SkyfallCurves' does not contain a definition for 'Slide'`. The same error names `BandHeight`, `BandRadius`, `RollOut` and `RollFrame`.

- [ ] **Step 3: Add the members to `SkyfallCurves`** (SkyfallRules.cs, after Task 11's members):
  - `Slide(u)`: 0 for NaN or `u <= 0`, 1 for `u >= 1`, else `u * u * u`.
  - `BandHeight(band, bands)`: `bands < 1` counts as 1, and `band` is held to `[0, bands - 1]`. The result is `(band + 1f) / (bands + 1f)`.
  - `BandRadius(band, bands)`: the same holds. The result is `bands > 1 ? 1 - 0.55f * band / (bands - 1) : 1`.
  - `RollOut(t, seconds)`: 1 when `!(seconds > 0)`. Otherwise `u = t / seconds` held to `[0, 1]` (NaN counts as 0), and the result is `1 - (1 - u) * (1 - u)`.
  - `RollFrame(t, seconds)`: 7 when `!(seconds > 0)`. Otherwise, with `u` as above, `Math.Min(7, (int)(8 * u))`.

- [ ] **Step 4: Run the tests.** `dotnet run --project QuickshotRing.Tests -c Release`. Expected: "ALL PASS (N)", where N is the previous count plus 23.

  - **`Run`** (one per storm, by `ArrowId`) holds:
    - `S`, `Land`, `SlideAt`, `BandsAt` and `Ground` (NaN = unknown), all worked out once;
    - the seven band sigils and their `PassedAt` times;
    - the model (root, three containers, the part renderers);
    - the roll pieces (14), the falling runes, and the two broken-ring pieces;
    - the flags `Shown`, `Landed`, `Shattered`, `CrumbleStep`.
  - **`Start(s)`** (section `look SkyfallNail.Start`):
    - Return when headless, when `s.Code == SkyCode.Fizzled`, when the storm has no `Nail` impact, or when a run for it exists.
    - Cast the ground at the centre with Task 11's bounds, `Skyfall.GroundAt(CX, CZ, CY + 0.5, Release.Y - Skyfall.GroundBelowMetres, out g)`.
    - Record the run. Nothing is built yet.
  - **`Tick()`** (no section of its own; Main's covers it):
    - Return when headless, and `Clear()` when `Player.Current` is null.
    - Each run follows the table, with `t = Time.time - S.StartTime`.
    - The bands are built at `BandsAt` (`Build(Style.NailBand, S.ArrowId + (uint)k, accent: (k & 1) == 1)`, `Place(centre_k, Quaternion.identity, radius_k)`) and shown with `Show(t - BandsAt - 0.08f * k, 0.5f, alpha_k)`.
    - The model is taken from its pool and placed at `SlideAt`. Then the impact, the shatter, the falling runes, the roll and the crumble, each at its moment.
    - The run ends after the crumble's last puff in normal mode, or after the roll and the falling runes in festival mode or with no ground.
  - **`Stop(arrowId)`:** release everything of that run at once (bands, model, rolls, runes) and drop it.
  - **`Clear()`:** `Stop` every run.
  - **Fail soft:** the body of `Tick` and `Start` are each in a try/catch. A throw sets `nailFailed`, stops every run, and warns once: `[Archery] Skyfall: the World-Nail could not be shown ({e.Message}); storms end without it until the settings are reloaded`. `Cfg.Reloaded` clears the flag and the head material.
    - The model alone failing (`PlainArrow()` is null) is not a throw: that run keeps its bands, roll and shatter, and skips the Nail. Debug once: `[Archery] Skyfall: no World-Nail model (the Arrow's parts are missing); bands and shatter only`.
  - Debug, once per storm at `land`: `[Archery] Skyfall: World-Nail of storm {id} landed at ({CX:0.0}, {ground:0.0}, {CZ:0.0}){(festival ? ", festival" : "")}`.
  - **`Check()`:** evaluate `Slide`, `BandHeight` and `BandRadius` for 7 bands, and `RollOut` and `RollFrame` with `NailRollSeconds`, with the current settings. Throw on NaN; return the count. It returns 0 when headless.

The Nail is copies of the real Arrow's four LOD0 renderers with their own materials (the ghost-copy code), about `NailLength` 46 m long, thickened along their own axes (shaft x2.9, head x2.1, vanes x2.3); it slides point-first through seven bands in `NailSlideSeconds` 2.5; the impact is the biggest blast, rings rolling out over the ground and flint debris, and the sigil shatters into falling runes; it stands `NailStandSeconds` 20, then crumbles into 'Large Smoke Puff' and embers. Festival: the largest firework instead. |
| `SkyfallSky.cs` | Clients: the sky dial, the game's own cave dimming (`DayTimeManager`'s `LightIntensityModifier` and `skyColorFade`), untinted and driven by `SkyDial`: only within `SkyDimMetres` 150 of the sigil, from the release until the World-Nail has landed plus 1 s, then eased back over `SkyFadeSeconds`; the sun to `SkySun` 0.5 and `skyColorFade` to at most `SkyFade` 0.6 (never above 0.95: the skybox turns black), faded over `SkyFadeSeconds` 2. `Restore` runs on every way out: the storm's end, a cave, a scene change, a disconnect, and a timeout. |

- **No new network traffic, with ONE owner-approved exception (Skyfall, 2026-11-19).** Everything is driven by messages the game already sends. The Quickdraw 4 line is signalled by sending the normal arrow flight sync with the impossible speed 999 (`Quickdraw4.Marker`); clients treat that as "line shot" and never start a flight. **The exception** (`SkyfallLink.cs`) is one message to a Skyfall holder's game. What: the game's own player text message (`PlayerCommunicationManager.SendMessageToPlayer`, a `PlayerTextMessage`). It carries only the tag text `QSR_SKYFALL_V1|<holds 0/1>|<festival 0/1>|<shared-settings check>` (`SkyfallSignal`). When: on `skyfall give` and `skyfall take` (to that player, if online), on `skyfall festival on|off` (to every holder online), and once each time a holder joins (queued until their `PlayerController` exists). Nothing shows: the holder's game latches it in a postfix on `PlayerTextMessage.Serialize` and swallows it in a prefix on `PlayerMessageDisplay.Display`. It is never sent per shot and never goes to other players. Everything else of Skyfall rides the shot claim the game already sends (`ShotClaim.Sky`, `Marks`). Any further exception needs the owner's approval.

- [ ] **Step 16: The Skyfall bullet.** Insert after the new :192 bullet:

```
- **Skyfall (the admin skill, 0.18.0; design `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-11-19-skyfall-admin-skill-design.md`).** **Scope.** It is given and taken ONLY by the server console (`skyfall`, `skyfall give <player>|all`, `skyfall take <player>`, `skyfall festival on|off`; `SkyfallCommands.cs`). It never changes the tree, the build block, an orb, or any shot that is not a cast. **The cast.** It needs the grant and a full draw (strength at least `MinStrength` 0.9; an armed Holy Arrow always sends 1.0) at `MinElevationDegrees` 25 or more, drawn for `ArmSeconds` 1.0 (the draw clock) and released at the sky (aim and strength judged on the wire pose). The caster's game decides "armed" and asks for the cast in its claim (`ShotClaim.Sky`). The server keeps the request only when `SkyfallRules.ServerAccepts` agrees: both the claimed time since the 68% crossing and the time the server decides with (`ClaimTrust.EffectiveMs`) are at least `ArmSeconds`. The server's own copy of a draw arrives 60-380 ms late, so never time the hold there. A refused request fizzles: the arrow is used up, the sigil puffs out and nothing falls. There is one storm per caster (`CasterBusy`), and the caster's game agrees. **The seam.** `Draws.Release` decides Skyfall first on every machine and returns the neutral resolution; `Volley.Fire` branches once to start the storm. `HolyArrow` (its arming, and the `LightBeamSkill.DestroyShotArrow` prefix), `Quickdraw4.ClientShot` and `Claims.PutResolved` stand aside for a cast. A shot that is not a cast must reach exactly the code it reached in 0.17.15. **What every machine draws.** A cast is relayed unsteered, Kind Early, stage 0, with no targets: its `SkyCode` rides the window bits and its sigil centre the marks. Every machine rebuilds the same storm from the arrow id, those marks and the `[shared]` `[Skyfall]` settings. So the pattern uses `SkyfallRules.Unit`, never `LookSeed` ("looks only"). **The real arrow.** It is held hidden on every machine from the release (no mesh is copied: the ghost is the plain Arrow), and the server destroys it about 1 s later (`Skyfall.Held`). This is Skyfall's own hold, not Seek's. **Damage.** Only the server deals it, from its due-time queue. Players are hurt ONLY inside a dome as its arrow lands: killed by the game's normal death, never the caster, whatever the PvP setting (owner: "Instantly kill them"). Festival mode never kills. The headless server never plays a sound or a pooled effect. **Looks.** At most 2-4 `Fx.Flash` lights per storm, no camera shake, and no more than 3 bright flashes a second. The sky is only dimmed, and `SkyfallSky.Restore` runs on every way out. **Testing.** `[Skyfall] SpectatorTest` (per machine) also runs the caster's own release through the spectator path and logs both landing patterns, so one client can check that a spectator rebuilds the same storm. **Friends.** A friend on an older DLL sees a plain arrow fly and vanish. Friends need every Skyfall build, because the settings and the claim field are shared.
```

- [ ] **Step 8: Give the owner the play-test list (game terms).** Send this list as it stands. "Console" means the Console button of the TavernLauncher Server window. "Test menu" means the archery test tools window.

**A. Getting it.**
- Do: in the Console, type `skyfall`.
  - You should see a reply saying nobody holds Skyfall and festival mode is off.
- Do: type `skyfall give <your in-game name>`.
  - The reply says Skyfall is given to you, with your account number.
  - In VR, nothing at all appears: no message, no text in front of your face. Any text with "QSR_SKYFALL" on screen is a failure.

**B. Normal shots stay normal.**
- Do: shoot a few times as usual: a level shot, then a quick shot at the sky released at once, then a half draw at the sky held for 2 s.
  - Each is exactly your normal shot: your usual ghosts, rings and charge.
  - No glyph rings climb the arrow, and no sigil appears.

**C. The hold.**
- Do: stand in open flat ground; the field west of the archery shrine works. In the test menu, press "Spawn 3 Birch Goteras" and "Spawn 3 Turabadas". They appear about 6 m ahead and freeze after 2 s.
- Do: walk back about 20 steps (15-20 m) and face them. Draw fully and aim high into the sky above them, at least a third of the way from the horizon to straight up. Hold still.
  - Within the first second: three gold glyph rings flare round the arrow and climb along it; a faint sigil of gold and cyan glyphs forms in the sky where you point, about 30 m above you; a thin target glyph shows on the ground under it; the sky dims gently; the shrine-activation sound swells.
  - At one second: one thump in your bow hand. That means "armed".
- Do: tilt the bow up and down.
  - Steeper brings the sigil closer, but never nearer than about 15 m. Flatter moves it farther, but never beyond about 60 m.
- Do: drop the aim below about 25 degrees.
  - The hold looks go away.

**D. The release.**
- Do: aim back up, hold until the thump, and let go.
  - Your arrow streaks up into the sigil as a ghost, and the real arrow is gone: one arrow is used up.
  - The sigil unfolds from its centre and its bands spin up, and its twin burns onto the ground.
  - Every landing spot gets an orange dome, like the game's dynamite warning. None is within about 3 m of you.
  - There are about 1.75 s of build-up before the first arrow lands: time to run.
  - No camera shake, no flicker, and no freeze at the moment you let go.

**E. The rain.**
- You should see 3 waves of 8 giant arrows, one second apart. Each is about 5 m long, see-through gold, with the Lightbeam's trail.
- Each falls into its own dome and explodes with the game's explosion: one big boom each, never doubled. Burning claw-shaped scorches stay on the ground.
- Creatures in a blast take heavy damage and are knocked down; the Birch Goteras fall over.
- Nothing is flung: no loose items or arrows fly off.
- You are never hurt.

**F. The World-Nail.**
- One second after the last wave, one enormous real arrow (bark shaft, blue and white feathers, gold tip, as tall as a cliff at about 46 m) slides point-first down through seven glowing ring bands, over about 2.5 s, into the sigil's centre.
- The impact:
  - the biggest blast;
  - rings rolling out over the ground;
  - flint debris;
  - creatures within about 12 m knocked down;
  - the sky sigil shattering into falling runes.
- About a second after the Nail lands, the sky eases back to normal over about 2 s. It is normal again long before the Nail crumbles.
- The Nail stands for 20 s, then crumbles into large smoke and embers.
- Afterwards nothing is left except loot from creatures that died.
- Do (on your next cast): walk into the Nail's big dome (about 20 m across, at the centre) before it lands, and stay in it.
  - You are NOT killed: the caster never is.
  - If you do die, that is a failure: report it. Your body and items stay where you fell.
- Do: press "Remove test enemies" in the test menu.

**G. One storm at a time.**
- Do: while a storm is still running (before the Nail crumbles), draw at the sky again, hold until the thump, and release.
  - That shot is a normal arrow, with no second storm.

**H. A friend in a dome** (needs a friend online with the new QuickshotRing.dll; skip it if nobody is around, and say so).
- Do: tell your friend first that in normal mode a dome kills.
- Do: cast, and have your friend stand inside a dome as its arrow lands.
  - They die the game's normal way; their body and items stay where they fell.
- Do: cast again, with your friend outside every dome.
  - They take no damage and no push.
- Do: during a storm, point together at one dome.
  - The domes, the rain and the Nail are in the same places for both of you.
- Do: have your friend aim at the sky and hold.
  - Their shots stay normal, because they do not hold Skyfall.
- If a friend still has the OLD QuickshotRing.dll, they see only a plain arrow fly up and vanish, nothing worse.

**I. Festival.**
- Do: in the Console, type `skyfall festival on`.
  - The reply says festival is on. Nothing pops up in VR.
- Do: cast again (as in C and D, with creatures spawned).
  - The same sigil and domes, but the impacts are fireworks (gold and cyan), big Easter-egg confetti, and healing bursts near players.
  - Creatures are knocked over but lose no health.
  - The Nail bursts into the largest firework.
- Do: stand inside a dome as it lands.
  - "OUT!" appears in front of your face, only on your screen, and you do not die.
  - If you were hurt or hungry and within about 4 m of an impact, you are healed and fed.
- Do: type `skyfall festival off`.

**J. Taking it away.**
- Do: type `skyfall take <your name>`.
  - The reply says it is taken, and nothing pops up in VR.
- Do: draw fully at the sky and hold for 2 s.
  - No rings, no sigil, no thump. Releasing gives a normal arrow.
- Do: type `skyfall give all`, then `skyfall`.
  - Everyone online is listed.
- Do: after your friend has left, type `skyfall take <friend's name>`.
  - It works, even though they are offline.

**K. A restart keeps the grant.**
- Do: type `skyfall give <your name>`. Stop the server in the launcher, then start it again with port 1757 filled in.
- Do: in the Console, type `skyfall`.
  - You are still listed, and festival is off.
- Do: join and hold a full draw at the sky.
  - The rings and the sigil appear again: the server told your game again.
- Do: quit only your game and rejoin, then hold at the sky.
  - The same.

**L. The spectator check.**
- Do: tell me before you start L.
- Do: once I have switched it on, cast once.
  - You see one storm, just as before (not two).

- [ ] **Step 9: Switch SpectatorTest on for L, then off.** When the owner reaches L:
